# Axolotl Game Art Style Guide: "Cute Cartoon"

Design language for the axolotl kids game (ages 8-12, web first, then mobile). This document is self-contained. The prototype it was taken from is throwaway, so every value is copied in here rather than linked.

**Source tags used in every table:**

| Tag | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `cartoon.js` | Value copied from `prototypes/art-styles/cartoon.js`. Approved. |
| `cartoon.html` | Value copied from `prototypes/art-styles/cartoon.html`. Approved. |
| `derived (provisional)` | New value worked out for this guide. Not yet seen in a prototype. Listed again in section 12. |

---

## 1. Purpose and source of truth

**Purpose.** Lock the look of the game so artists, animators, and UI builders all draw the same axolotl in the same world. Where this guide and a prototype disagree, the prototype code is the record of what was approved, and this guide must be corrected.

**Source of truth.**

| Item | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Prototype folder | `prototypes/art-styles/` (`cartoon.js`, `cartoon.html`) | `cartoon.js` |
| Prototype commit | `937139e` (feat(prototypes): add axolotl art-style prototypes) | `cartoon.js` |
| Logical px | The unit of the 480 x 360 (4:3) reference viewport. All sizes below are in logical px unless stated. 1 logical px = 1 CSS px when the canvas is exactly 480 x 360. | `cartoon.js` |
| Reference viewport | 480 x 360, 4:3. Canvas is contain-fit and centred; the leftover area (letterbox) is filled with the colour in the palette (3a). | `cartoon.js`, `cartoon.html` |
| Water line (SURF) | y = 80 logical px | `cartoon.js` |
| Pixel density | Device pixel ratio capped at 2 | `cartoon.js` |
| Smallest supported canvas | 460 x 320 CSS px landscape, 320 x 440 portrait. The banking card, the tank arrival card, the first-find cards (the Paint shell card with its short body, then its short title, 8), the Home Tank hub and decorate controls, the Treasure Chest (with its short section lines, 8), the explorer card, the axolotl choices card (its title shows at both sizes, 8), the refuge screen (its title shows only when it fits beside Back, 8), and the character creator and restyle step screens (the Outfit tab included) show all their text at or above it (the Home Tank name panel and the decorate caption show only when they fit; on a step screen the helper line, the picture button labels and the creator heading's "Step N of 11:" start show only when they fit, the preview shrinks to 0.6 and then hides with the chosen picture button as the preview, and the restyle heading may hide while the bold current tab names the step; the Outfit tab's outfit summary shows only when it fits, and its choices view may hide the tab row; the explorer card's title and outfit summary show only when they fit, 8); below it, the buttons still stay on the canvas. In-trip messages (the find toast, the snack banner and the first-trip hint) never cover the controls, or open water in the middle of the play area, at any size: they show at the top or bottom edge, else over the Trip Bag, else over the status panels, and inside the middle they cover only the panels themselves. The snack banner and the first-trip hint with no such place at 24 px try every place again at 20 px before they wait. A message with no such place waits. Shown alone, only one long toast waits, at 460 x 320 with Go home showing, which does not happen in play; at 320 x 440 none waits (8, HUD overlay fit). | `derived (provisional)` |

**Reconciliation notes (code wins over any earlier written spec):**

- Tail fin height. The code builds the top fin as `2 + 9 * sin(...)` and the bottom fin as `2 + 7 * sin(...)`, measured outward from the tail core edge. So the fin reaches 2 px at both ends and peaks at 11 px (top) and 9 px (bottom). Earlier notes quoted "max 9 / max 7"; those are the sine amplitudes only. This guide uses the code values (section 6).
- Bubble spawn offset. The code places a bubble 49 px in front of the body centre (unscaled by the 0.95 scene scale) and 4 px lower. This guide records it as written.

**MVP additions.** MVP visuals from the [Axolotl MVP PRD](prds/axolotl-mvp.md) are specified in this guide. All are derived (provisional) and listed in section 12.

**PRD conflict rows resolved here**

| PRD row | Resolution |
|---|---|
| An "invulnerability flash" after a hit | No flashing. Protected time is a steady bubble ring (6q). The flashing rule is in section 10. |
| Art style guide: accessories "never cover the eyes", but glasses are a planned Wearable | Face slot with eye zones. Face items draw below the eyes (6a layer 11.5, 6i). |
| Section 7b had no rarity colours, badge shapes, Home Tank kit, collectibles, or hazard art | Rarity colours 3e and badge shapes 8. Tilapia 7c. Home Spot 7d. Treasures, Dash pot, hint sparkle, and Paint shells 7e. Home Tank kit 7f. 7b updated. |
| Section 7e says the hint star is the cue for a hidden treasure; PRD H3 also gives the hint sparkle to the last 3 snacks | The last 3 snacks each show the Common hint star (7e, Last-snack hint star), always on (PRD open question 16). The Area exit, its sign and the Area stars are in 7g. Follow-up: rename "hint star" to "hint sparkle" across the guide; this guide keeps "hint star" for now. |
| No Wearable slot anchors, no face, gill, or spot variants, and no Dream colour patterns | Slot anchors 6i. Face, gill, and spot variants 6l, 6m, 6n, 6o. Dream colours 3f and patterns 6p. |
| Non-art rows: ADR 0007 "No harsh fail states" (now amended by ADR 0008), and "no network requests" | No guide change. Leg-loss art stays gentle (6q). |
| PRD open question 4: Home Spot, burrow or buoy | Burrow (7d). |
| Sections 4a and 6o scaled sizes by x0.85 / x1.00 / x1.15, and there were no Tail, Body, Age, or boy and girl cues (PRD F2, F4, open question 35) | Sizes x0.72 / x0.85 / x1.00, with Large at the prototype scale (4a, 6o). Tail length 6r, Body 6s, Age 6t, Boy or girl cues 6u. |
| PRD conflict row: "The art style guide (6i) has four Wearable slots and layers 8.5 to 11.5. Its Wearable test pose (6k) checks four slots." | Resolved (issue #16). Eight slots are canonical, in PRD F8 order: Head, Neck, Face, Shirt, Back, Tail, Socks, Feet. Anchors, footprints and keep-clear notes are in 6i. The layers are in 6a (2.5, 3.5, 8.2 and 9.7 are new). The test pose is 6k and the lost-leg rule is 6q. The perk icons are 7b, the four new Wearable icons 7e, the Outfit tab and the explorer card 8, the crops and the frame check 11. |
| CONTEXT.md, flagged ambiguity: "Slot count differs: the PRD (F8) has eight Slots, while `WearableSlot` in `src/logic/catalogue.ts` and art style guide 6i have four." | Resolved: this guide has the PRD's eight Slots, `WearableSlot` in the code has them too (issue #16), and the flagged ambiguity is gone from CONTEXT.md. |
| PRD F8: "When a leg is lost, its sock and shoe disappear with it, and both come back when the leg regrows." Section 6q grows a regrowing leg gradually, from the root to the toes. | They come back only when the leg is fully regrown (100%), on the frame of the regrown burst. A part-grown leg wears nothing (6i, 6q). |

---

## 2. Principles

| Do | Don't |
|---|---|
| Use one dark ink outline colour everywhere, with round joins and round caps. | Never use pure black. Never use more than one outline colour. |
| Keep the outline weight the same on every object no matter its size. | Don't thin the outline on small or distant objects. |
| Make the axolotl the brightest, warmest, most readable thing on screen. | Don't let a background object compete with the hero for colour or contrast. |
| Round every shape: soft ellipses, blobs, gentle curves. | No sharp corners, spikes, or scary silhouettes. |
| Use flat colour plus one simple highlight per object. | No gradients on characters, no texture noise, no realistic shading. |
| Show meaning with shape, motion, and text as well as colour. | Never rely on hue alone to tell morphs, water states, or hazards apart. |
| Keep the world sunny, friendly, and a little silly. Sad scenes stay gentle. | No gore, no dead animals, no frightening faces. |
| Move with soft, slow, looping motion. | No sudden flashes, shakes, or fast strobing. |

### 3b. Morph role-slot table

A morph is a colour swap done through named role slots. The shapes, line weights, and layer order never change. Leucistic is the prototype colourway. The other eight are derived (provisional).

A dash means "keep the Leucistic value"; it has not been decided separately yet (flagged in section 12).

| White albino | Light eye with a ring, no glints, the lightest body of all morphs. As built: the eye is #e2557a with a 1.5 px ring in ink #2b2350, drawn just inside the bead edge so the eye keeps its size. It has no shimmer glints, which tells it from Golden albino and Copper. | `derived (provisional)` |

Nine speckles in #a8613a, under the shines: body (-6,-2) r 1.2, (-20,-2) r 1.1, (-32,3) r 1.0, (-12,6) r 1.0, (-26,7) r 1.0; tail t 0.2 offset -0.35 r 1.1, t 0.4 offset 0.3 r 1.0, t 0.6 offset -0.2 r 0.9, t 0.8 offset 0.1 r 0.8. Three shimmer glints rgba(255,255,255,0.8), over the shines: body (-28,-4) r 1.8, (2,-9) r 1.8, tail t 0.5 offset 0.15 r 1.6. As built: body (8,-8) r 3.4 (on the head), (-6,-3) r 4.2, (-24,1) r 3.6; tail t 0.35 offset -0.3 r 2.8, t 0.55 offset 0.2 r 2.2 (t 0.55, not 0.65, so it stays on the tail core on every tail length, body and age). Clipped to the silhouette, no outline, at least 2 units inside the edge (section 6h). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Axanthic | Pale fins, normal shine, no eye ring, grey fin rays in rgba(94,96,106,0.65), plus the **Axanthic rim**: 2 px #e4e6eb just inside the ink along the upper outline, from the snout to the look's fin band start (tail point 3, or tail point 1 on a Boy, 6u), not on the fin. **The Axanthic rim is required**: ink against the Axanthic body is only 3.18:1, while ink against the rim is 11.53:1 (section 10). It differs from the Melanoid rim by colour (#e4e6eb, not #cec9f0) and by extent (it stops at the fin band start and leaves the fin; the Melanoid rim runs on along the top fin edge). Melanoid also has a matte body and an eye ring; Axanthic has neither. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Melanoid | Darkest value of all morphs; matte (no shiny flecks; body shine alpha 0.45 instead of 0.75); 2 px rim light in #cec9f0 along the upper silhouette, top fin edge included. **The rim light is required**: ink against the melanoid body is only 1.74:1, while ink against the rim is 9.07:1 (section 10). As built: the rim runs from the snout to the tail tip along the top fin edge, 2 px just inside the ink; the eye ring is 1.5 px #cec9f0, drawn just inside the bead edge. | `derived (provisional)` |
| GFP | Soft glow halo in #7dff8f at about 0.35 alpha, outside the outline, pulsing no faster than every 2.4 s; small sparkles | `derived (provisional)` |

**Luminance order** (darkest to lightest, relative luminance): melanoid 0.077, axanthic 0.182, wild 0.446, leucistic 0.596, copper 0.624, golden 0.689, GFP 0.858, piebald 0.880, white albino 0.926. Close pairs are separated by non-hue cues: leucistic and copper by speckles, glints and gill colour; leucistic and golden by eye value and pattern; GFP, piebald and white albino by glow halo, patches, eye ring and gill colour (white albino has a light ringed eye and pink gills, piebald has dark patches and a dark bead eye, leucistic has a dark bead eye and no patches). The UI never shows a morph by colour alone: always its name plus a distinct badge shape. Dream colours (3f) are paints, not morphs. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

### 3c. Water states

| State | Shallow / mid / deep | Haze | Other | Non-hue cues | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Clean | #55e4d8 / #1cb6cc / #0a5c98 | rgba(40,200,215,0.12) | Full-strength light rays; no particles | Light rays present, clear water | `cartoon.js` |
| Murky | #9cc7a4 / #5c9a80 / #2f5d55 | rgba(110,130,70,0.28) | Light rays at half strength; silt motes in rgba(200,180,120,0.5) | Visible silt particles, dimmer rays | `derived (provisional)` |
| Polluted | #b3b8a3 / #7a8577 / #434b46 | rgba(90,95,85,0.38) | No light rays; friendly rounded debris with ink outlines; drooping reeds | Debris silhouettes, no rays, sagging plants | `derived (provisional)` |

Rules for all states:

- Same gradient stop positions as clean (0, 0.45, 1) and the same stack order. `derived (provisional)`
- Polluted is sad, never scary. No dead animals, no frightening shapes. `derived (provisional)`
- Haze is never drawn over the axolotl (the prototype draws haze before the hero). `cartoon.js`
- State is shown by particle density, light-ray presence, debris silhouettes, and plant posture, so it still reads without colour. `derived (provisional)`

**Habitat looks (issue #75).** The three states above are the canal's. A habitat is a look: the water gradient, haze, surface line, bed and edge plants. The canal look is the values above, unchanged. The lily pad pond (Area 2) is the second look (`src/art/habitatLook.ts`). It keeps the same gradient stop positions (0, 0.45, 1), the same stack order and the same state rules as the canal; only the colours, the bed and the edge plants change. It is a warmer, yellower green than the canal teal.

| Part | Value | Contrast with ink | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Water shallow / mid / deep | #a4e57a / #58bf6a / #2f8f58 (relative luminance 0.6534 / 0.4038 / 0.2095) | 9.64:1 / 6.22:1 / 3.56:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Haze | rgba(130,210,90,0.12), a soft leaf green. It only lightens every water stop, so the rule below holds. Never drawn over the hero. | - | `derived (provisional)` |
| Surface line | rgba(246,255,226,0.95), the canal line warmed toward cream, same width and wave | - | `derived (provisional)` |
| Bed top / bottom | #b8a064 / #9a8450, a soft olive mud bed, lighter than the canal bed | 5.65:1 / 3.97:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Bed lip | #d9c48c | 8.38:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Mud dots | rgba(120,96,47,0.45). Over the bed bottom they blend to a luminance of about 0.183. | 3.19:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Edge reed stalk / light stalk / leaf | #3a8a40 / #4a9a44 / #58a843 | 3.35:1 / 4.11:1 / 4.86:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Edge cattail head | #a8693a | 3.25:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Stream | A lighter band of water rgba(240,255,228,0.3), white flow lines rgba(255,255,255,0.6) ending in left-pointing chevrons, and two stones. Geometry is in 7a. | - | `derived (provisional)` |

**Pond contrast rule.** Every pond background colour, water stops, bed stops, mud dots and edge reeds included, has relative luminance 0.1687 or more. Ink #2b2350 (luminance 0.0229) is then 3:1 or better against it. So on any pond background every cue object keeps its ink outline at 3:1 on its own, whatever its fill, and the pair method of section 10 is not needed there. The pond is the one place where Leucistic reaches 3:1 along its plain body edge (the canal residual in section 12 does not occur in the pond), and where the plastic worm, whose fills are under 9:1 with ink, is held by its outline. The canal cannot promise this: its deep water and bed bottom sit where both ink and a light fill fall under 3:1. Non-hue cues for the pond: the mud bed with its dots, the tall edge reeds with cattails, the stream band with its flow chevrons, and the surface line, so it still reads without colour.

**Area looks: the pond at dusk (Area 3, issue #98).** An area may use a variant of its habitat's look ([ADR 0016](adr/0016-heron-timer-warnings-and-area-look-variants.md)). The pond at dusk (`pond-dusk` in `src/art/habitatLook.ts`) is the first: the lily pad pond's look, slightly warmer and dimmer, for Area 3. It is a whole palette baked into the background chunks, the haze and the surface line, never an overlay over gameplay depths. It keeps the pond's gradient stop positions (0, 0.45, 1), stack order, state rules, bed shape, mud and pebble counts (22 and 5), edge reed geometry and stream geometry (7a); only the colours below change, plus the sky, the air wash and the ray strength. The look also says what its area needs beside colours: the stream, the root arch as its cave (7b), the pond props, and 2 fireflies per screen (7b). The Area map is keyed by habitat looks, so Area 3 shows as a pond marker (8). Every water stop is darker and lower in hue than the pond's, and keeps more than 0.8 of the pond stop's luminance, so it still reads as a green pond.

| Part | Value | Contrast with ink | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Water shallow / mid / deep | #b4d872 / #6fae5c / #428a4e (relative luminance 0.6003 / 0.3442 / 0.1989; hue 81 / 106 / 130, against the pond's 96 / 130 / 146) | 8.91:1 / 5.40:1 / 3.41:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Haze | rgba(225,170,95,0.12), a warm amber. It lightens every water stop (the deep stop to luminance 0.2186), so the rule below holds. Never drawn over the hero. | - | `derived (provisional)` |
| Surface line | rgba(255,236,204,0.95), a warmer cream than the pond's, same width and wave | - | `derived (provisional)` |
| Bed top / bottom | #b09060 / #9a7c4c (luminance 0.3002 / 0.2181), the pond mud warmer and dimmer | 4.80:1 / 3.67:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Bed lip | #d4b884 (0.4994) | 7.53:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Mud dots | rgba(120,96,47,0.40): the pond's dot at 0.40 in place of 0.45, so the dimmer bed keeps the rule. Over the bed bottom they blend to #8c7140, luminance 0.1776. | 3.12:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Edge reed stalk / light stalk / leaf | #4a8848 / #5a9646 / #68a644 (0.1953 / 0.2443 / 0.3063), the pond reeds a little warmer | 3.36:1 / 4.03:1 / 4.88:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Edge cattail head | #a8693a, the pond's | 3.25:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Sky | #c9a6e0 (lavender) at the top to #ffcf9e (peach) at the horizon, the prototype's gradient positions (7a). Seen through the air wash: #c29acf (0.3909) and #f0bd96 (0.5712). | - | `derived (provisional)` |
| Air wash | rgba(150,80,100,0.14), a dim rose, baked into the background chunk over the sky band (y 0 to SURF + 6) after the sun, clouds, bank and willows and before the water. The water starts at SURF + 3 and covers the band's last 3 px, so the wash lies in the air band only: never over the water, the haze or any gameplay depth. | - | `derived (provisional)` |
| Light rays | The pond's three rays at 0.6 of their alpha | - | `derived (provisional)` |
| Stream | The pond's stream colours and geometry (7a), placed by the stream mouth rule (7a) | - | `derived (provisional)` |

**Dusk contrast rule.** The pond contrast rule holds at dusk: every dusk background colour has relative luminance 0.1687 or more, so ink is 3:1 or better against it. That covers every sample of the water gradient with and without the haze, every sample of the bed gradient with the mud dots and the haze, the bed lip, the edge reeds, the sky seen through the wash, the stream band and flow lines over every dusk water colour (tapered or not), and the stream stones. `src/art/pondArt.test.ts` checks it, and checks every cue fill by the pair method on every dusk background. Recorded exceptions, all off the water or dark on purpose:

- The bank soil #a2623a is under the floor in every look (0.1672) and falls to 0.1603 under the wash (ink 2.88:1); the soil detail #7f4a2a is 0.1001 under the wash. The bank is above the water, and every cue object stays in the water, except the heron shadow's wing tip (7h).
- The root arch (7b): its bark (0.1705, 0.1968 under the haze), rootlets and soil mat (the bank soil, 0.1941 under the haze) pass; its mat's soil dots (0.1236 under the haze) and its hollow #2b1d1a (the hollow log's interior, 0.0319 under the haze) are under the floor. Both are scenery. What sits on the hollow is a hidden treasure (not a cue object), a Dash pot, a revealed treasure or a hint star (9:1 pairs, or white with ink), which pass on any background by the pair method (10), not by the floor.

Non-hue cues at dusk are the pond's (the mud bed and its dots, the edge reeds with cattails, the stream chevrons, the surface line), plus the root arch's roots and the fireflies' bright tails over the water.

### 3e. Rarity colours

There are four rarities, in this order: Common, Rare, Epic, Legendary. Each has a fill, a badge shape (section 8), and its name in text. Shape and name carry the meaning. Colour is the weakest cue.

| Rarity | Fill | Relative luminance | Contrast with ink | Badge shape | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Common | #1fb98d (a teal-leaning green) | 0.369 | 5.75:1 | Circle | `derived (provisional)` |
| Rare | #5ab8ff | 0.437 | 6.67:1 | Rounded diamond | `derived (provisional)` |
| Epic | #b07cff | 0.309 | 4.92:1 | Rounded shield | `derived (provisional)` |
| Legendary | #ffc12e | 0.596 | 8.86:1 | Rounded five-point star | `derived (provisional)` |

**Greyscale contrast between rarity fills**

| Pair | Contrast | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Common / Rare | 1.16:1 (was 1.80:1 with the old pale grey #e3ecef) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Common / Epic | 1.17:1 (was 2.44:1) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Common / Legendary | 1.54:1 (was 1.36:1) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Rare / Epic | 1.36:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Rare / Legendary | 1.33:1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Epic / Legendary | 1.80:1 | `derived (provisional)` |

No pair reaches 3:1, so value alone never identifies a rarity. Shape and name do. All four fills pass 4.5:1 with ink, so ink text or icons may sit on them. Legendary #ffc12e is also the Legendary wearable trim (6i).

**Common green (issue #68).** Common was pale grey #e3ecef; it is now #1fb98d in the badge and in the rarity glow, so one rarity always has one colour. Rare, Epic and Legendary keep their fills; Legendary keeps gold #ffc12e. #1fb98d has 5.75:1 with ink (badges are UI on a panel, so 4.5:1 is enough) and 2.51:1 with the white panel, so the badge's ink outline (8) carries its edge. Its hue is 163 (HSL; 167 in OKLCH). The new green makes two pairs closer than before: Common / Rare (1.16:1, was 1.80:1) and Common / Epic (1.17:1, was 2.44:1). That is allowed because the badge shape and the rarity name carry rarity, and colour stays the weakest cue (10, PRD X2).

Separation from the other greens and teals:

| #a8f0cf | Mint Dream colour (3f) | 1.91:1 | 153 | 0.205; Mint is pale, so value and chroma separate them more than hue | `derived (provisional)` |
| #55e4d8 | Secondary button (3d) | 1.61:1 | 175 | 0.147 | `derived (provisional)` |

A Common glow over the canal water mid tone reads by hue only. That is by design: colour is the weakest rarity cue, and the glow never replaces the badge shape or the rarity name.

**Rarity glow colour.** The rarity glow (7e) is the badge fill of its rarity from the table above, at 0.35 alpha at most (3g). Paint shells and Dream colour swatches glow by their own rarity.

### 3f. Dream colours

There are fourteen Dream colours. They are found in Paint shells: twelve in the canal, one per colour, the thirteenth, Lily green, in the lily pad pond (Area 2, issue #75), and the fourteenth, Firefly gold, in the pond at dusk (Area 3, issue #98), so there are fourteen Paint shells in all. A Dream colour is a paint over the real morph, never a morph (PRD F14, C12). It swaps the body and gill role slots of 3b. The real morph keeps its eye and mouth slots.

Each colour is two picks (body and gill) plus fixed sRGB mixes:

- Far side = body mixed 12% toward ink.
- Fin = body mixed 40% toward white.
- Belly = body mixed 55% toward white.
- Near filament = gill mixed 22% toward white.
- Far stem = gill mixed 10% toward ink.
- Far filament = near filament mixed 8% toward ink.

**Rim light.** 2 px #cec9f0 along the upper silhouette, top fin edge included, as Melanoid. Ink and rim are 9.07:1, so one of them is 3.01:1 or better on any background (10).

**Eye ring.** 1.5 px #cec9f0 around the morph eye, whenever the morph eye against the Dream body is under 3:1. Ring against eye bead is 10.98:1. Ring against Midnight blue is 6.24:1, Galaxy 7.39:1, Tie-dye galaxy 7.09:1. It also covers the Golden albino and White albino light eyes on dark Dream colours (#d6336c against Midnight blue is 2.14:1; #e2557a against Midnight blue is 2.74:1).

**Greyscale note.** Mint, Sky blue, Lavender, Sunset orange, Bubblegum, Lily green, and Firefly gold are 1.00 to 1.28:1 apart (Midnight blue is 6.25:1 or more from them; Lily green is 8.02:1 from it, Firefly gold 6.97:1). Lily green is the lightest solid, relative luminance 0.801 (Mint 0.752), and is 1.06 to 1.28:1 from the other six light solids. Firefly gold (0.690) is 1.08 to 1.15:1 from the other six light solids. So the name label is the greyscale cue for solids (PRD F15). Every Dream colour carries the Dream marker (6p), so it never reads as a real morph.

**Lily green (issue #75).** The pond's Paint shell holds it. It is a light yellow-green body #ddf274 (hue 70) with a green gill #4fb83a, the same two picks and mixes as every other colour above. It is a plain solid: no pattern, no rim light and no eye ring, as the other light solids. The morph eye bead #1b1535 against the body is 14.13:1. Its spots follow the rule of 3b for Dream colours with a body relative luminance of 0.2 or more: ink at 0.30 (its luminance is 0.801). Its near gill stem #4fb83a against the body is 2.06:1, so the ink outline carries the gills as on every colour. Its swatch tile is the plain one with one shine (8). A Girl's cheek heart (6u) on Lily green: the white edge is 1.23:1 against the body, ink 11.67:1 and the heart fill #ff5c9a 2.35:1, so the ink carries the heart (10).

Separation of Lily green from the nearby greens. It is yellower than the rest, and its name label is always shown with it (PRD F15):

Colour is the weakest rarity cue (3e): Lily green is Rare, shown by the rounded diamond badge and the word "Rare" (8), never by its colour.

**Firefly gold (issue #98).** The Area 3 Paint shell holds it. It is a warm gold body #ffd52a (hue 48, relative luminance 0.690) with an amber gill #e08214, the same two picks and mixes as every other colour above (Dream colour slots table). It is a plain solid: no pattern, no rim light and no Dream eye ring, as the other light solids. The body against ink is 10.15:1, so 3.19:1 or better on any background (10). The morph eye bead #1b1535 against the body is 12.28:1. The Golden albino eye #d6336c is 3.25:1 against it and the White albino eye #e2557a 2.55:1; both albinos keep their own 1.5 ink eye ring (3b), 10.15:1 against the body, so the eye still reads. Its spots follow the rule of 3b for Dream colours with a body relative luminance of 0.2 or more: ink at 0.30. Its near gill stem #e08214 against the body is 2.01:1, so the ink outline carries the gills as on every colour. Its swatch tile is the plain one with one shine (8). A Girl's cheek heart (6u) on Firefly gold: the white edge is 1.42:1 against the body, ink 10.15:1 and the heart fill #ff5c9a 2.04:1, so the ink carries the heart (10).

Colour is the weakest rarity cue (3e): Firefly gold is Rare, shown by the rounded diamond badge and the word "Rare" (8), never by its colour.

## 4. Line and shape rules

### 4a. Core rules

| Rule | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Outline colour | #2b2350 only. Never pure black. | `cartoon.js` |
| Main line width (LW) | 3.5 logical px | `cartoon.js` |
| Line join and cap | Round, round | `cartoon.js` |
| Outline weight vs object size | Constant. A small object keeps the same line as a big one. Clouds are scaled, so they draw their line at LW / cloud scale to stay 3.5 px on screen. | `cartoon.js` |
| Size variants | Small, Medium and Large (6o) are a uniform scale of the whole axolotl, ink, wearables and Dream overlay included (x0.72 / x0.85 / x1.00; Large is the prototype scale). This is the one exception to the constant-outline rule. It keeps every clearance in 6i true at every size (PRD F13). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Corners | Rounded everywhere (ellipses and smooth curves) | `cartoon.js` |
| Pixel density | Draw at device pixel ratio, capped at 2 | `cartoon.js` |

|---|---|---|
| Main outline (axolotl body, sun, clouds before scaling, soil, grass, bed edge) | 3.5 | `cartoon.js` |

### 4c. The three outline techniques

| Technique | How it works | Use it for | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `blob` | Stroke the path at 2 x LW first, then fill on top. The fill hides the inner half, so the whole outline sits **outside** the silhouette and fills never shrink. Parts that share a silhouette (head, body, tail) are merged into one path first, so there are no inner seams. | Axolotl silhouette, clouds, foliage | `cartoon.js` |
| `shape` | Fill, then a normal centred stroke on the edge. | Sun, soil, grass, pebbles, lily pads, petals, mouth | `cartoon.js` |
| `inkMany` | For a group of tubes: draw **all** the ink strokes first (width + 2 x ol), then **all** the colour strokes (width). Overlapping tubes merge into one inked shape with no line across the joint. | Gills, legs and toes, reeds, willow strands, sun rays | `cartoon.js` |

### 4d. Close-ups and portraits

When the axolotl is drawn much larger than in the scene (cards, portraits, store art), do not keep 3.5 px. Scale the outline with the character: about **2.9% of snout-to-tail length L**. This is the in-scene ratio (3.5 px outline on a roughly 121 px axolotl). Source: `derived (provisional)`.

### 4e. Sparkle shapes

| Shape | Construction | Used for | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Twinkle | Two crossed round-capped tubes (a plus shape). Arms #ffe14a, width 3, with `ol` 1.6. White core of radius 0.32 x the arm radius. It matches the swim-test tail sparkles. | The regrowth twinkles by a regrowing leg's toes (6q), the regeneration twinkles along the tail, and the pot crumble | `derived (provisional)` |
| 4-point star | Filled concave four-point star. Outer radius R, inner radius 0.38R, tip rounding 0.15R. Fill #ffffff, ink outline 1.6 (1.8 when R is 6 or more). | The treasure hint, the snack speed-up star (6q), the Dream marker, Legendary wearable twinkles, Galaxy stars (no outline needed on the dark Galaxy bases), and the refuge clean-up sparkle (R 10, 7i). The refuge nudge badge's star is the same shape in solid ink with no white fill (R 6.4, 7i). | `derived (provisional)` |

Neither shape ever blinks. Only slow size or position changes are allowed (section 9).

## 5. Highlights, shading, depth

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Lighting | Flat colour with one soft shine per object. No cast shadows, no gradients on characters. | `cartoon.js` |
| Shine shape | Small rounded ellipse or arc, upper-left of the object. | `cartoon.js` |
| Axolotl body shine | rgba(255,255,255,0.75): body ellipse at (-16,-9) 12 x 3, head ellipse at (17,-16) 7 x 2.8 | `cartoon.js` |
| Other shines | Sun rgba(255,255,255,0.9); cloud #cfeeff; willow #9af27a; grass #a6f58a; lily pad #a2f58c; pebbles rgba(255,255,255,0.8); bubble glint #ffffff | `cartoon.js` |
| Shine travels with the character | Shines are drawn in the axolotl's local space, so they flip when it turns to face left. This means light is not locked to the world. This is **accepted on purpose**. | `cartoon.js` |
| Depth by colour | Far-side body parts use a darker, slightly more saturated tone (far legs #f58fb0; far gills #e8507f and #f26b92). Near parts are brighter. | `cartoon.js` |
| Depth by layer | Back to front: sky, bank and willows, water, light rays, canal bed and pebbles, reeds, haze, axolotl, bubbles, water surface line, lily pads and flower. | `cartoon.js` |
| Depth by layer, game additions | Layers the game adds to the prototype order. Depths are in 7e; the full order is in `src/art/layers.ts`. The cave interior (8) and treasure icons under cover (9) sit behind the reeds. The cave rocks, the pond's hollow log front and the past-the-current marker (12) sit just in front of the reeds, under the haze. The queasy cheek tint (41) sits just over the axolotl (40) and under the bubbles (50). Dash pots and revealed treasures (28) sit above the haze and below the collectibles. Hint stars (75) sit above the lily pads and below fx. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Atmosphere | Haze rgba(40,200,215,0.12) sits over the plants and bed but **under** the hero. The hero is never hazed. | `cartoon.js` |
| Light rays | Three slanted white shafts from the surface, alpha 0.12 +/- 0.05 fading to 0 at y 300. | `cartoon.js` |

---

## 6. Axolotl character sheet

### 6a. Construction and layer order

The axolotl is authored **facing right** in local units, then drawn at x0.95 in the scene. Mirroring for left-facing is allowed. It mirrors about the anchor (local x = 0), so a left-facing sprite sits on the same spot as when it faces right. This applies to the axolotl and the tilapia. Snout-to-tail length **L = 127 local units** (about 121 px in the scene at Large, about 25% of the 480 px width). Source: `cartoon.js`.

L = 127 is the prototype proportions: Young, Regular, a Medium tail and no Boy cue. Tail, Body, Age and Boy or girl (6r to 6u) change the torso, belly, tail, legs and gills, so L runs from 101.6 to 147 local units; the default Boy is 131. The head, the face, the gill roots and the Head, Neck and Face anchors never move. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

Layer order, back to front (each layer is its own animated part in production):

| # | Layer | Notes | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Far gills | Darker far-side stem and filament colours | `cartoon.js` |
| 2 | Far legs | Far-side body colour | `cartoon.js` |
| 2.5 | Far-leg Socks and Feet | Wearable, Socks and Feet slots (6i), on each far leg that is present: the Sock first (sleeve, then foot pad), then the Feet item over it, so a Sock shows above the Shoe. Each is drawn in its leg's frame and turns with the leg's paddle. Fills are the item colours mixed 12% toward ink (the far-side depth rule of 3f); ink and shine as the near pair. Behind the silhouette (layer 4). | `derived (provisional)` |
| 3 | Near legs | Body colour | `cartoon.js` |
| 3.5 | Near-leg Socks and Feet | As 2.5, on each near leg that is present, in the item colours as authored. Still behind the silhouette (layer 4), so the head, the torso, the eyes and the mouth always paint over them. | `derived (provisional)` |
| 4 | Merged silhouette | Tail + torso + head as one `blob` | `cartoon.js` |
| 5 | Fin bands and fin rays | Lighter bands plus ray lines and core edge line | `cartoon.js` |
| 6 | Belly | Light ellipse | `cartoon.js` |
| 6.5 | Dream overlay | When a Dream colour is on (6p): the pattern, then the rim light (on the Dream colours that have one), then the Dream marker last. Clipped to the silhouette and fin fill (inset per 6p). | `derived (provisional)` |
| 7 | Shines | Body and head shine | `cartoon.js` |
| 8 | Spots | The player Spots option (6n). The prototype's three freckles were the first Freckles option; Freckles are now ten specks. | `cartoon.js`, `derived (provisional)` |
| 9 | Near gills | In front of the body, sprouting from the cheek | `cartoon.js` |
| 9.2 | Tail item | Wearable, Tail slot (6i). Above the near gills since issue #88 (8.5 before), so a near gill never hides it. | `derived (provisional)` |
| 9.3 | Shirt item | Wearable, Shirt slot (6i). Above the near gills since issue #88 (8.2 before), so a near gill never hides it. It covers the torso inside the torso edge band, so it covers the belly, the shines and the body marks beneath it (spots, morph marks and the Dream pattern on the torso). The band is 1 unit since issue #88, so the outer unit of the torso fill always shows; the 2 px rim light is 2 deep, so the Shirt covers its inner unit (12). The innermost of the three body-out layers: Shirt, then the Neck item (9.5), then the Back item (9.7). | `derived (provisional)` |
| 9.5 | Neck item | Wearable, Neck slot (6i) | `derived (provisional)` |
| 9.7 | Back item | Wearable, Back slot (6i). Outermost of Shirt, Neck and Back: it is above the Shirt and the Neck item, and above the near gills (layer 9), so it may cover near gill filaments and stems behind the gill-root zone, never a gill root (6i). It is above the Tail item (9.2) where the two meet on the shortest looks (12). | `derived (provisional)` |
| 10 | Blush | Two cheek ellipses | `cartoon.js` |
| 10.2 | Cheek heart | A Girl's cheek heart (6u, issue #87): after the blush, before the Head item. Only on a Girl, and only when the blush is on. | `derived (provisional)` |
| 10.5 | Head item | Wearable, Head slot (6i) | `derived (provisional)` |
| 11 | Mouth | Fill, outline, corner ticks. Above the Neck (9.5) and Head (10.5) items, so neither hides it. | `cartoon.js` |
| 11.5 | Face item | Wearable, Face slot (6i). It sits below the eyes on purpose: eyes always paint last, so no Face item can cover them. | `derived (provisional)` |
| 12 | Eyes | Bead plus two glints. Far eye first, then near. A Girl's three eyelashes per eye (6u) paint on this layer, inside each eye's blink squash and just before its bead, so the bead covers the lash roots and the near bead covers any far lash that reaches it. A Grumpy brow (6l) paints on this layer too, after its bead and outside the blink squash. This layer is above every Wearable layer (2.5 to 11.5), so no Wearable covers a lash or a brow. | `cartoon.js`, `derived (provisional)` |

Notes:

- Decimal rows are new. Whole numbers keep the prototype numbering, because code comments use them.
- Layers 2 to 8 follow the look's legs, torso, belly and tail (6r to 6u), and so do the Tail item (9.2) and the Shirt (9.3). The gills (layers 1 and 9) keep their roots and change only their length, with Age (6t). Layers 10 to 12 sit on the head and never move.
- Effects (6q): the leg-loss puff and the regrown burst draw in front of the axolotl, at depth 80 (`fx`). The regrowth twinkles are the rig's last part (below), so they draw over the axolotl too. The carried-home bubble draws in front of the axolotl, at depth 50 (`bubbles`). The protected ring draws just behind the axolotl, at depth 38 (`halo`): behind the stranded axolotl (39) and the axolotl (40), in front of the tilapia (35).
- The eight Wearable layers, back to front: Far-leg Socks and Feet 2.5, Near-leg Socks and Feet 3.5, Tail 9.2, Shirt 9.3, Neck 9.5, Back 9.7, Head 10.5, Face 11.5. Eyes (layer 12, a Girl's lashes and a Grumpy brow included) stay above every one of them, so no Wearable can cover an eye.
- Issue #88 moved the Tail item and the Shirt above the near gills. Every body Wearable now draws over the near gills, so a near gill never hides one.
- A Sock and a Shoe draw with their leg, behind the silhouette, and only while that leg is present (6i feet rule, 6q). So a lost or part-grown leg is the plain ghost outline, and the head, the torso and the gill roots can never be covered by them.

**Axolotl rig parts (as built, issue #46).** Every live axolotl is a rig of baked parts ([ADR 0013](adr/0013-axolotl-rig-and-frame-budget.md)): each part bakes the layers above once into its own texture, and the rig moves, turns and swaps the parts each frame. The parts draw in this order, back to front. Picture-button stills are not rigs (section 11).

| Order | Part | Layers | How it moves | Baked frames |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `gill-far-0` to `gill-far-2` | 1 | Shows the flutter frame nearest the gill phase, plus a small live turn about its gill root | 10 flutter frames over one 0.8 s gill cycle |
| 2 | `leg-0`, `leg-1` | 2 | Turns about its leg root with the paddle | One texture per leg state (present, lost, or regrowing in 10 steps), baked the first time that state shows |
| 3 | `wear-leg-0`, `wear-leg-1` | 2.5 | Turns with its leg. Only when Socks or Feet are worn; empty on a lost or regrowing leg | One per leg state, as the leg |
| 4 | `leg-2`, `leg-3` | 3 | As the far legs | As the far legs |
| 5 | `wear-leg-2`, `wear-leg-3` | 3.5 | As the far-leg wear parts | As the far-leg wear parts |
| 6 | `body` | 4 to 8 | Never moves. Baked straight and clipped at the tail root | 1; an Iridescent body bakes 16 sheen steps plus the still frame (stripe at x -10) |
| 7 | `tail` | 4 to 8 (an Iridescent tail: 4 to 6.2) | Drawn over the body across a 4-unit seam overlap, where the bend is 0. Bent live: on WebGL a Phaser Rope whose vertices are written along the tail samples; on Canvas the strip frame nearest the pose. The still pose shows a still bake | 1 on WebGL; on Canvas a 36-frame strip, 12 swim phases x 3 tail amplitudes (1, 1.2, 1.6) |
| 8 | Iridescent tail stripe | 6.5, the stripe pair only | Iridescent only. A flat-colour vector overlay drawn live between `tail` and `tail-over`, exact at every pose, with no texture repaint; redrawn only when the shown tail or the sheen step changes | None |
| 9 | `tail-over` | 6.5 to 8, after the stripe | Iridescent only. Bends with the tail | As `tail` |
| 10 | `gill-near-0` to `gill-near-2` | 9 | As the far gills | As the far gills |
| 11 | `tail-item` | 9.2 | Only when a Tail item is worn. Moves and turns with the Tail anchor, over the near gills | 1 |
| 12 | `face` | 9.3 to 11.5: the Shirt item first, then the Neck and Back items, blush, a Girl's cheek heart, the Head item, mouth and Face item | Never moves. Its box holds the head, the cheek heart zone, the mouth zone and each worn Shirt, Neck, Back, Head and Face footprint | 1 |
| 13 | `eyes` | 12, a Grumpy brow included | Shows the blink frame nearest the eye height | 3: open, half, closed |
| 14 | Twinkles | Effects | Pooled sprites, placed each frame where the regrowth and regeneration twinkles go | One strip of 13 radii, 0.6 to 4.2 by 0.3 |

Issue #88 changed the order: the near gills now come before the Tail item, and the Shirt moved out of `body` into `face`, so both draw over the near gills.

A part is baked again only when the look (any field, size included), the morph, the Dream colour, the outfit, a leg's state or the bake scale changes. A pose never re-bakes a part.

The table is the prototype proportions: Young (6t), Regular (6s), a Medium tail (6r), and no Boy or girl cue (6u). The Options row at the end lists what those options change; the head and face rows hold for every option.

| Torso | Ellipse centre (-8,4), radii 37 x 19: 74 long, 38 tall | Length 58% of L | `cartoon.js` |

| Belly | Ellipse centre (-6,14), radii 26 x 6.2 | - | `cartoon.js` |
| Longest gill | 37 (near middle gill) | About 29% of L | `cartoon.js` |

| Far eye | Radius 5.3 | 83% of the near eye (3/4 view) | `cartoon.js` |
| Mouth | Width 21 (x 28 to 49) | About 39% of head length | `cartoon.js` |

| Options | Tail length (6r), Body (6s), Age (6t) and Boy or girl (6u) change the torso, belly, tail core and fins (reach and band start), leg roots and lengths, and gill lengths. The head ellipse (26,-1) 27 x 22, the eyes, the mouth, the blush, the head shine and the gill roots never change. The torso's front end (the head joint) stays at x 29. | L 101.6 to 147 | `derived (provisional)` |

Three gills per side. They fan from straight up (top gill) to straight back (bottom gill), and the middle gill is the longest.

### 6h. Morph application

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Swap method | Replace colours through the role slots in 3b. Never redraw the shape. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Extras | A morph may add a pattern overlay, an eye variant, a rim light, or a halo (see the non-hue cue table in 3b). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Outline | Always #2b2350 and the same widths on every morph. | `cartoon.js` |
| Melanoid | Rim light is required: body vs ink is only 1.74:1 (section 10). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Wild | The Wild rim is required: body vs ink is only 6.80:1 (section 10). 2 px #e2e4b8 along the upper outline from the snout to the look's fin band start (tail point 3, or 1 on a Boy), not on the fin (the fin #cdd398 is 9.16:1 with ink and passes alone). It keeps the body colour, so the luminance order (3b) holds, and it differs from Melanoid's fin-inclusive #cec9f0 rim. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Axanthic | The Axanthic rim is required: body vs ink is only 3.18:1 (section 10). 2 px #e4e6eb along the upper outline from the snout to the look's fin band start (tail point 3, or 1 on a Boy), not on the fin (the fin #cfd1d8 is 9.43:1 with ink and passes alone). It differs from Melanoid's fin-inclusive #cec9f0 rim by colour and extent. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Copper and Piebald patterns | The Copper speckles and glints and the Piebald patches (3b) stay at least 2 units inside the silhouette and fin edges, so the colour next to the ink is always the body. Their bodies pass alone (section 10), so they need no rim. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Spots (as built) | Each axolotl draws the player's chosen spot pattern (none, freckles, dots, patches; 6n) at layer 8 in its morph's spot colour from the 3b spot table: Leucistic rgba(236,120,160,0.55), Wild rgba(113,74,119,0.55), Golden albino rgba(239,115,80,0.55), White albino rgba(236,120,160,0.55), Copper rgba(168,97,58,0.55), Piebald rgba(236,120,160,0.55), Axanthic rgba(228,230,235,0.40), Melanoid rgba(206,201,240,0.35). | `derived (provisional)` |
| GFP | Halo sits outside the outline; the pulse is gentle (section 10). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Dream colours | See 6p. The real morph never changes. A Dream colour swaps only the body and gill slots (3f). Eye and mouth stay. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Morph extras under a Dream colour | While a Dream colour is on, the real morph's body extras are off: Wild speckles and flecks, Golden shimmer glints, Copper speckles and glints, Piebald patches, the Axanthic rim, pale fin and fin rays, and Melanoid matte shine and rim light. The Dream rim light and eye ring rules (3f) apply instead. | `derived (provisional)` |

### 6i. Wearable slots and keep-clear zones

PRD C10, F8 to F13. All values are in the axolotl's local units, facing right (L = 127). There are eight slots, in PRD order: Head, Neck, Face, Shirt, Back, Tail, Socks, Feet (issue #16).

**Rules**

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Line rules | Worn Wearable silhouettes use ink 2.0 (`shape`, inked shapes and `blob`; issue #88 thinned it from 2.8, 4b). Tubes are 3.2 with `ol` 2.4, except the Sock sleeve (colour width 11.6, `ol` 2.4, ink width 16.4) and the Shell necklace cord (colour 4.6 with `ol` 0.6, 5.8 in all; 4.0 and 5.2 behind the chin). The glasses band is 3.5 (colour 2.1, `ol` 0.7). Same ink colour as the rest of the art. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Facing | Authored facing right. Mirrors with the character. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Scale | Drawn in the axolotl's local units and scaled with it, so every clearance holds at every size (6o, 4a). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Every option | The Head, Neck and Face anchors, the eye zones, the gill-root zone, the cheek heart zone and the mouth zone sit on the head, which never moves, so they hold for every Boy or girl, Tail, Body and Age (6r to 6u) as well as every size. The Tail, Shirt, Back, Socks and Feet anchors ride the look instead (the tail, the torso edge and the leg roots), so they are written below as rules, not fixed points. Every number below was checked over all 54 looks (2 Boy or girl x 3 Tail x 3 Body x 3 Age), every swim phase and tail amplitudes up to 1.6. | `derived (provisional)` |
| One item per slot | An axolotl wears at most one item in each of the eight slots. Items from different slots mix and match. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Layering | From the body out: the Shirt (layer 9.3), then the Neck item (9.5), then the Back item (9.7). The Tail item (9.2) sits just under the Shirt. The Tail item and the Shirt draw over the near gills (9) since issue #88, so a near gill never hides them. The Head (10.5) and Face (11.5) items keep their layers. Socks and Feet draw with their leg, behind the silhouette (2.5 far legs, 3.5 near legs): the Sock first, then the Feet item over it, so a Sock shows above the Shoe. Eyes (layer 12), a Girl's eyelashes included, stay above every Wearable layer. Full table in 6a. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Keep clear | Never cover the eyes (a Girl's eyelashes included) or the gill roots, in any slot, at any size, Boy or girl, Tail, Body or Age (PRD F13). Every Wearable also keeps off the cheek heart zone (zones below) on every look, so a Girl's cheek heart is never covered. The Shirt stays inside the torso edge band and behind the Shirt and Back front line (zones below). The Back item may cover near gill filaments and stems behind the gill-root zone, never a root. Socks and Feet paint behind the silhouette, so they can cover nothing on the body, the head or the gills. Behind it, their paint may reach into the gill-root zone box (as built): the Sock sleeve starts at the leg root, inside the torso, and the Feet envelope's square corner reaches up to 0.72 into the box's lower edge (1 at most) on a Baby Slim Boy's far-front leg. The silhouette paints over both, so no root is covered. The mouth zone is not a keep-clear zone for Wearables (issue #88): the mouth paints at 11, over the Neck (9.5) and Head (10.5) items, so an item under it never hides it. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Feet rule | One Socks item and one Feet item cover all four feet: the same item on each of the four legs, drawn while that leg is present. A lost leg's Sock and Shoe go with it: the lost-leg ghost (6q) is the bare leg and toes, never enlarged for them. They come back only when that leg is fully regrown (100%), on the frame of the regrown burst; a part-grown leg wears nothing. An axolotl can wear Socks alone, Feet alone, or both. The Socks and Feet perks keep working while at least one leg is left (PRD F8), so losing a leg changes the picture but never the perk. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Size | Every Wearable meets the size rule below (issue #88). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Frame | Every footprint fits the 166 x 142 axolotl texture frame (11) with 4 or more units of padding, so the frame does not grow (issue #88 did not grow it). Every Socks or Feet item's lowest ink must be at y 59 or less, 4 above the frame bottom (y 63). As built, the lowest Socks and Flippers ink is at y 56.24, on every look and swim phase. The Feet envelope is bigger than any Feet item: its square corner is lowest at y 58.87, 4.13 above the frame bottom, and it reaches x -62.6 to 47.2. The highest footprint is the Head at y -44; the Back box reaches y -37.9. The protected ring and the carry bubble (6q Size) need no change: they are sized from the look's ink box (`axolotlInkBounds`), whose regrowth glints already reach further than the Feet envelope (14 + 6.9 along the leg and 11 + 6.9 across, against t + 10.3 and 13.5). Wearables are not measured, as Head items are not. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Size rule (issue #88, owner rule).** This rule holds for every Wearable: the ones in the game now and every one added later. There are no per-item exceptions. When an item is too small, change its art, its footprint, a zone or a layer until it passes. Never change the numbers to fit an item. The rule's numbers live in `src/art/wearableFit.ts`, and `src/art/wearableArt.test.ts` holds every item to them.

Every Wearable must:

1. **Clear the swim-view floor.** Worn alone, it shows at least 64 CSS px² of its own visible paint in the swim view, on every look and at every swim phase. The swim view is measured at the Small phone viewport (320 x 440 CSS px, fit zoom 1) on a Small axolotl: 0.95 (the scene scale) x 0.72 (Small) = 0.684 CSS px per local unit, so the floor is about 136.8 square local units. Only the item's own colour counts: its fills that are half opaque or more, not its ink. A spot counts only where that fill is the top paint, so the item's own ink and every later layer hide it (for example the near gills, the blush, the cheek heart, the mouth, the Face item, a Girl's lashes or the eyes). Paint outside the item's clip does not count. The cases are all 54 body looks over 13 swim phases at tail amplitude 1. A Head, Neck or Face item also takes every Boy or girl with every eye and mouth, and the 54 looks with Grumpy eyes (and their brows). A Tail item also takes tail amplitude 1.6.
2. **Meet its slot's minimums** on every look (and every leg), as in the table below (`WEARABLE_SLOT_MINIMUMS`).
3. **Be listed in `WEARABLE_RECORD`** in `src/art/wearableArt.test.ts`, with its measured numbers: its least swim-view paint in CSS px², the case where that least is found, and its least value for each slot minimum. The test owns these numbers, so this guide does not copy them. The suite fails when a number moves.

| Slot | Minimums, ink included unless stated |
|---|---|
| Head | Ink across x is at least half the head width (the head ellipse is 54 wide). Ink rises 8 or more above the outer edge of the head outline. |
| Neck | Ink along x is 27 or more wide. |
| Face | Ink spans the whole eye span, from the near eye zone's left edge to the far eye zone's right edge. The item's outer outline encloses all of each eye zone. |
| Shirt | The item's silhouette (fill and its own ink) covers 75% or more of the torso side: the torso fill from the tail root to the front line. |
| Back | Ink along the back edge is 22 or more long, and at least half the torso length (2 rx - 30). Ink reaches 8 or more outside the torso outline. 40% or more of the item's fill is outside the silhouette (the torso and its outline ink). |
| Tail | Ink across the tail is 40% or more of the full tail height at the anchor (the core and both fins, at the still pose). Ink along the tail is 16 or more. |
| Socks | The sleeve's colour runs the whole leg tube (u 0 to l). The sleeve's ink reaches 1 or more past the bare leg tube's ink on each side. The foot pad covers all three toe tips (a disc of the toe ink's half width round each tip), its own ink included. |
| Feet | The item, its own ink included, covers all of the three toes' ink past the leg tube's ink, and all of the Sock's foot pad, the pad's ink included. |

The suite also holds every Wearable to these checks: it has art in its slot; a Tail item keeps at least one Dream marker star 0.6 or more clear at every look, swim phase and tail amplitude up to 1.6; a Socks or Feet item goes with its leg (6q) and its lowest ink is at y 59 or less; and no item is ever wholly hidden in a full outfit (one Wearable in every slot).

**To add a Wearable:**

1. Add it to its area's list in `src/logic/catalogue.ts` (`CANAL_TREASURES`, `POND_TREASURES` or `POND_2_TREASURES`) and give it art: its slug in `WEARABLE_ART_SLOT` and its painter in `src/art/drawWearables.ts`.
2. Run the suite. `src/art/wearableArt.test.ts` finds the new item in the catalogue and checks it against the floor, its slot's minimums and the other checks. It fails until the item's numbers are recorded.
3. Record its measured numbers in `WEARABLE_RECORD`.

The suite reads the whole catalogue, `ALL_TREASURES` (every area: the canal's, the pond's and Area 3's). A later area's list needs adding to `ALL_TREASURES`, and nothing else, for the suite to check its Wearables.

**Keep-clear zones**

| Zone | Geometry | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Near eye zone | Circle, centre (28,-6), radius 8.2 (eye radius 6.4 + 1.8) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Far eye zone | Circle, centre (42,-7), radius 7.1 (5.3 + 1.8) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Gill-root zone | Box, x -5 to 12, y -22 to 13. It holds all six roots of 6d plus the stem ink. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Mouth zone | Box, x 24.4 to 52.4, y 2.6 to 21.3: the ink of the first four mouths plus 1 unit (the prototype corner ticks at (26.4,7.6) and (50.4,4.6), and the Tongue-out tongue at the bottom). It did not grow for the four newer mouths: every mouth variant (6l) stays inside it, and all eight together reach x 24.6 to 52.2, y 3.2 to 20.3. Since issue #88 it is not a keep-clear zone for Wearables: it boxes the mouth for the rig's `face` part and the crops. The mouth paints at 11, over the Neck (9.5) and Head (10.5) items, so the Shell necklace may run under it. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Cheek heart zone | Circle, centre (18.2,9.6), radius about 5.32: the heart's bottom tip, 3.72 from the centre, plus the 1.6 of white outside the curve. It holds all of a Girl's cheek heart paint (6u, issue #87). Every Wearable keeps off it on every look. It stays clear of both eye zones, both lash reach circles and every lash, the gill-root zone (0.9 to its right), the mouth zone box (0.9 to its left), the Head slot and the glasses band. As built, the gaps from the zone to the items are: Sun hat 24.54, Shell necklace 0.39, Heart glasses band 1.40. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Lash reach | Near eye circle, centre (28,-6), radius 10.4 (eye radius 6.4 + 4.0); far eye circle, centre (42,-7), radius 9.3 (5.3 + 4.0). It is wider than the eye zone, so a Girl's eyelashes (6u) read at Small. Every lash corner also stays inside the head ellipse, at x 16.5 or more (clear of the gill stems, which stay left of about x 15.5) and at y -19.5 or lower (the Head item's lowest point). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Shirt and Back front line | The vertical line x = -5.5 (issue #88; -6.8 before). No Shirt or Back ink past x -5.5, which leaves 0.5 units to the gill-root zone (x -5). The Shirt's own front edge is on the line. The Back box's forward body-side corner is on the line on every look (Back row below). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Torso edge band | The 1 unit just inside the torso edge, all round (the torso ellipse minus its inset by 1; issue #88, 3 before). The Shirt never paints there, so the outer unit of the torso fill and the ink edge partner (10) stay on the body. The 2 px Wild, Axanthic, Melanoid and Dream rim light (3b, 3f) is 2 deep, so on the torso the Shirt covers its inner unit (12). As built, the inset is the ellipse with radii rx - 1 and ry - 1: exactly 1 unit in along both axes, and a little less round the diagonals. | `derived (provisional)` |

No wearable paint goes inside any zone. The exceptions: the clear lens tint over the eye zones; the glasses band, which reaches into the lash reach circles (the eyes paint above it); and Socks and Feet behind the silhouette (Keep clear rule above). The mouth zone is not one of these zones (Mouth zone row). Eye variants (6l) stay inside the eye zones. A Grumpy brow (6l) reaches past the near eye zone, but it paints on the eye layer, above every Wearable layer, so no Wearable covers it. A Girl's eyelashes (6u) root inside the eye zones and reach past them, up to the lash reach circle, on every eye variant. They count as part of the eyes (PRD F13). They paint on the eye layer (6a layer 12), above every Wearable layer (2.5 to 11.5), so a Wearable that reaches the space between the eye zone and the lash reach circle (for example the glasses band) never covers a lash.

Issue #88 grew it forward (to x 46, from 36) and lowered its top (to y -44, from -46): the item may pass the head outline forward and up. | 10.5 | Head | `derived (provisional)` |
| Neck | Necklaces, scarves, collars | (17,16), the throat | x 2 to 42, y 13.1 to 22.75 (issue #88; x 13 to 22, y 11 to 21 before): a collar box under the chin, below the gill-root zone (y 13) and over the mouth zone box. Its bottom is LW / 2 under the head's lowest point. The item's paint, not this box, keeps clear of the cheek heart zone and the gill-root zone, and stays LW / 2 or more inside the outer edge of the silhouette outline on every look. So the outline's outer half always shows and nothing hangs below the silhouette: the front legs stay visible. The mouth paints over the item's front. | 9.5 | Head and body | `derived (provisional)` |

As built, the frame's lower band (to y 5.7) overlaps the mouth zone box (from y 2.6); it keeps about 1 unit from the mouth ink itself (about 0.7 from the Big grin's back tick, 12). | 11.5 | Head | `derived (provisional)` |
| Shirt | Tank tops, T-shirts, sweaters, hoodies, wetsuit tops | The look's torso centre (cx, 4) (6s, 6t). | Torso only. The torso ellipse inset 1 all round (radii rx - 1 and ry - 1, so 1 unit of body shows at the edge; issue #88, 3 before), cut at the rear at the tail root x (6r) and at the front at x -5.5 on the front line (issue #88, -7.5 before), ink included (the 2.0 hem and neckline lines sit inside this). The front edge has a neckline. As built, the front cut is the chord at x -5.5 between the inset ellipse's two front corners, and the neckline is a concave curve from corner to corner that bows back 1.5 units (3 before), to x -7 at the torso centre line (y 4), ink included. Over all 54 looks: x -38 to -5.5, y -22 to 30. No sleeves: it never reaches a leg, the tail core or the head. | 9.3 | Body (the torso) | `derived (provisional)` |

The item turns to the edge's tangent there (-0.374 to -0.070 rad, rising toward the snout). | A box in the back frame (x' along the tangent toward the snout, y' across it, + into the body), worked out per look (`backFrameBox`, issue #88). Across: y' -14 (outward) to +3 (into the body), ink included, so 17 tall and proud of the back against the water (-8 to +5 before). The +3 edge keeps the Back item's fill off the Shirt's fill on every look. Along: 22 long, or half the torso length (2 rx - 30) when that is more (18 before). Its forward body-side corner (front end, y' +3) sits on the front line, x -5.5, the box's forward-most point as the frame rises toward the snout, and the box runs back from there. Rotates with the local tail direction. | 9.2 | Tail wave | `derived (provisional)` |
| Socks | Socks | Each leg root (6e), in leg space (below): u along the leg from the root, v across it. l is the leg length (far 14, near 15; x0.7 on a Baby), t the toe length (8.5; 5.95 on a Baby). | The sleeve: u 0 to l, ink width 16.4 (colour 11.6, `ol` 2.4; issue #88, colour 10.4 before), round ends. 11.6 is the widest colour whose box stays clear of the near lash reach on a Slim look's near-front leg. The foot pad over the toes: a rounded rectangle (corner radius 8), `shape` 2.0, ink included, u from l - 2.0 to l + t + 6.0, v +/-11.2 (Baby +/-9.9). Inside the Feet envelope. | 2.5 far legs, 3.5 near legs | Its leg (turns with the paddle) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Feet | Shoes, sneakers, boots, flippers | As Socks. | The Feet envelope, for any Feet item: u from l - 2.0 to l + t + 10.3, v +/-13.5, ink included (issue #88; l - 3.0 to l + t + 10.0 and v +/-12.0 before). It starts where the pad, a Shoe and the Flippers start, so its wider back corner stays within 1 of the gill-root zone, and its front reaches as far as the frame bottom allows at that width. As built it is a plain box with square corners (radius 0), so the measured extents (Frame rule above) are its corners; the Sock's foot pad and a shoe keep their corner radius 8. A shoe is a rounded rectangle (corner radius 8), `shape` 2.0, u from l - 2.0 to l + t + 7.6, v +/-13.2 (Baby +/-12.2); it reaches 1.5 or more past the pad's ink, and its fill covers the Sock's foot pad, so the Sock shows above it. Flippers are a fan (Flippers construction below). One item covers all four feet. | 2.5 far legs, 3.5 near legs, after the Sock on the same leg | Its leg (turns with the paddle) | `derived (provisional)` |

**Leg space.** Socks and Feet marks use a frame with its origin at the leg root. u runs along the leg and is l at the leg end (the tube's end, where the three toes start); v is across the leg. The frame turns with the leg's paddle angle (legs swing +/-0.35 rad, section 9), so a Sock and a Shoe follow the leg. The toes splay +/-0.6 rad from the leg line and reach u = l + t (tips) with 4.7 of ink round them (toe colour width 4.2, `ol` 2.6). Leg roots, lengths and toes are in 6e.

**Back frame.** The Back item's frame has its origin at the anchor on the torso top edge. x' runs along the edge's tangent toward the snout, y' is across it and + is into the body. The item rests on the edge, with its lower 3 units inside the silhouette and its upper 14 above it (issue #88; 5 and 8 before).

**Tail space.** Marks on the tail use two numbers. s runs from 0 at the tail root (x -30 on the prototype tail) to 1 at the tip (x -74); on every look it runs over that look's tail (6r). v is the offset from the tail centreline as a fraction of the local tail half-height, and + is down. Tail-space marks move with the tail wave. 6n, 6p, and the Tail slot use it.

**Clearance notes**

- The Head item to glasses gap is 1.8 units (glasses top at y -17.7).
- Gill stems of every style and every Age (6t) stay left of about x 15.5 (the far top stem at full flutter), so Head items (x 16 or more) never cover a stem. They may overlap far gill filament tips, which draw behind everything.
- Shirt. Torso only, and clear of the head: the head ellipse starts at x -1, 4.5 units in front of the Shirt's front edge (x -5.5). It stays 1 unit inside the torso edge, so the ink edge partner is never covered; it covers the inner unit of the 2 px rim light on the torso (torso edge band above). No sleeve covers a leg: legs draw behind the body (layers 2 and 3), so a lost or regrowing leg never changes the Shirt, and the Shirt never changes a leg. It covers the belly, the body shine, the spots, the morph marks and the Dream pattern on the torso. The morph still reads from its head, fins, gills, eyes and rim (3b), and the Dream marker on the top fin band (6p) is not under it. The Shirt ends at the tail root, so it never meets the waving tail core.
- Back. Its forward corner is at x -5.5 or less on every look, so the gill-root zone (x -5 to 12) is never reached and no root or first stem ink is covered. The Back item draws over the near gills (layer 9), so it may cover near gill filaments and the stems behind the zone. Measured with straight stems on the old 18 x 13 box (before issue #88), over every gill style and Age: it covered none of the near top and near bottom gills, and at most 63% of the length of the near middle gill's stem (Short style; 53% Fluffy, 42% Long). The issue #88 box is bigger, and these shares have not been measured again (12). Its root is never covered. It never reaches the head: the head ellipse starts at x -1. On a Baby with a Short tail it overlaps the Tail item (12). Its fill never meets the Tank top's fill, on every look.
- Socks and Feet. Behind the silhouette, so the head, the torso, the eyes, the mouth and the near gills always paint over them; the far gills (layer 1) never reach the legs. The body hides the top of the leg where it leaves the torso, so the Sock's top and cuff are never drawn. The part of the sleeve that shows above a Shoe is about 0 to 6 units: most on a Slim or Regular Young or Grown-up, little on a Chubby or Baby look, where the body hides most of the leg (12). The Sock's foot pad is under the Shoe at the sides and the front: the Shoe's fill edge (its ink edge less 1.0) lies 0.6 or more beyond the pad's ink edge there (1.0 at the sides, 0.6 at the front), so no pad ink shows round the Shoe. At the back the pad starts 1.0 behind the Shoe's fill edge, which is the Sock showing above the Shoe.
- Far-leg Socks and Feet are drawn in the item colours mixed 12% toward ink (6a layer 2.5). Their ink and shine are the same as on the near legs.

**Rarity look ladder** (Wearables and Decorations, PRD F9)

| Rarity | Look | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Common | One flat colour plus one shine | `derived (provisional)` |
| Rare | Adds one trim band in a second colour | `derived (provisional)` |
| Epic | Adds one rounded gem (radius 2.5) or a small repeat pattern | `derived (provisional)` |
| Legendary | Adds trim in #ffc12e and one static 4-point star (R 3) | `derived (provisional)` |

The ladder reads in greyscale by counted details, not colour.

**Worn looks, all twenty Wearables** (the eight canal ones, the seven pond ones of issue #75, then the five Area 3 ones of issue #98). Each is the 7e catalogue icon's design re-fitted to its footprint (the silhouette and the details are the same, the ink widths are not scaled, 4b). Every outer fill is one of the 9:1 icon colours of 3g and 7e. The details are counted by the ladder above. The pond and Area 3 rows give the least swim-view paint of each item in CSS px² beside the 64 floor (the size rule above): the number is a note of the build, and `WEARABLE_RECORD` in `src/art/wearableArt.test.ts` is the record. None of the pond or Area 3 items covers an eye, a gill root or a Girl's cheek heart. No worn look moves on its own: only the rig (the swim, the legs, the tail wave) moves it, so the Firefly cape's fireflies never glow or blink.

One shine, an ellipse 1.1 x 3 at (27.2,-33.2), tilted 0.2 rad. No trim. As built (issue #88): the brim is an ellipse centred (31,-25.18), radii 13 x 3.6, tilted -0.06 rad, so it rises toward the snout. Its ink fills the Head footprint: x 16.02 to 45.98, lowest y -19.5 on the head top, crown top y -41.4. The brim's lowest fill point (y -21.5) is inside the head outline, so the hat rests on the head. The brim passes the head outline forward (its front tip is 4 or more above the head top under it) and keeps 1.81 over the glasses band and gem. | `derived (provisional)` |

| Water-lily shirt | Rare | Shirt | The Tank top's silhouette (the whole Shirt footprint, `shape` 2.0, the neckline arc) in #d0ecff, with one hem band #ffe14a, 3 tall, starting 0.56 of the inset ry below the torso centre line, between two 0.6 ink lines and clipped inside the silhouette: the one trim band. A #ffe0ee five-petal water-lily blossom sits on the chest: five merged petal ellipses (radii 2.25 x 1.7, centred 2.6 out), a small `blob` with 0.6 of ink outside, and a centre of ink at alpha 0.30, radius 0.9. It is half way along the Shirt (or further back, where the neckline would cut it, keeping 0.2 from the neckline ink) and 0.2 of the inset ry above the centre line, clear of the band and the shine. The blossom is a picture detail, as the frog eyes on the Froggy boots; the counted Rare detail is the band. One shine placed as on the Tank top. No sleeves. Least swim-view paint 65.70 CSS px². | `derived (provisional)` |
| Pearl tail charm | Epic | Tail | A big #fbe6f2 pearl (radius 6.4, centred 1.8 into the core from the anchor, as the bow's knot) on a short #ffd9e6 chain (2.4 wide, y' -10.4 to -3.4) under one #55e4d8 gem (radius 2.5, outline 1.2, at y' -11.4: the Epic detail), with two #ffd9e6 ribbon tails hanging from the pearl over the core, one merged `blob` silhouette (ink 2.0 all outside). A thin ink ring (1.2) round the pearl sets it off the ribbons. One shine on the pearl. No trim. Painted in the Tail frame (x' along the local tail direction toward the head, y' down): the gem and chain stand above the tail's top edge, the pearl rests on it and the ribbons hang over the core. The ribbon toward the tail tip is short (to x' -7.6) and the one toward the head longer (to x' 10.2), as the Golden bow's, so a Dream marker star past it stays 0.6 or more clear at every swim phase. Everything, ink included, stays within 17.5 units of the anchor. Least swim-view paint 68.75 CSS px². | `derived (provisional)` |
| Firefly cape | Legendary | Back | A #d4d9ff cape lying along the back, tall and rounded at the front where it is clasped (collar corner radius 3), its upper edge flat and then falling 3.4 toward the rear from 0.45 of its length, with a scalloped hem of three scallops at the rear end. It is one `blob` silhouette (ink 2.0 all outside the fill) in the Back box of the look, ink included: the fills are 2.0 inside the box, so the cape runs the box's length and stands 13 proud of the back. The Legendary trim is one #ffc12e clasp band across the front (from 4.4 to 2 behind the front end, between two 0.6 ink lines, clipped inside the cape) and one static 4-point star (R 3, outline 1.2) 9 behind the front. Four fireflies sit on the cape, three in a low row and one high at the rear: each a yellow glow disc rgba(255,225,74,0.75), radius 2, round a small ink body (0.9 x 0.8). They are a flat fill inside the silhouette, so they are free, and they never move or glow on their own. No gem, as the Rainbow dragonfly wings' worn look (the icon has one at the clasp). One shine. Painted in the Back frame, so its lower edge (y' +1) keeps off the Shirt. Least swim-view paint 82.99 CSS px². | `derived (provisional)` |

**Flippers construction** (leg space, ink included, #ffe14a, `shape` 2.0). A fan from the ankle: the back edge is at u = l - 2.0 with v +/-9.5; the sides open to v +/-12.4 at u = l + 3.0 and to v +/-13.5 at u = l + t + 4.0; the tip is a rounded arc through u = l + t + 10.3 at v 0. It fills the Feet envelope's width and front (issue #88; v +/-8.5, +/-11.0 and +/-12.0 and the tip at l + t + 10.0 before), so it stays inside the envelope and fully covers the Sock's foot pad. These numbers are ink included, so the drawn path is 1.0 inside them (as built: the back edge at u = l - 1.0 with v +/-8.5, v +/-11.4 at u = l + 3.0, v +/-12.5 at u = l + t + 4.0, and the tip at u = l + t + 9.3). As built, the cuff band runs across the full width from the fan's back edge, whose ink is the cuff's first line, to one 1.2 ink line centred at u = l + 2.0 (the design had two lines, at u = l - 1.6 and l + 1.8). The ridges fan out from (l + 3.5, 0) to just inside the tip edge: to u = l + t + 7.7 at v 0 and to u = l + t + 7.1 at v -6.75 and +6.75. The shine sits clear of the ink: an ellipse of radii 2.6 x 0.9 at u = l + 4.5, v = -5.5 (the design had 3 x 1.1 at u = l + 4, v = -7). A Shoe (a future Feet item) stays inside the Feet envelope in the same way.

### 6j. Expressions

Only "happy" and "blink" exist in the prototype. Others are to be designed using the same eye and mouth construction. The eye variants in 6l are the resting look. An expression temporarily overrides them and then returns. Blink squashes any eye variant to 12% height.

| Expression | Notes | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Happy (default) | Open "D" smile, round beads, blush | `cartoon.js` |
| Blink | Eye squashed to 12% height for 0.14 s | `cartoon.js` |
| Curious | To design. A resting eye variant exists (6l). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Surprised | To design | `derived (provisional)` |
| Sleepy | To design. A resting eye variant exists (6l). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Sad | To design (gentle, never scary) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Determined | To design. A resting eye variant exists (6l). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Excited | To design | `derived (provisional)` |
| Regenerating | To design (sparkles, never gory) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Queasy (issue #75) | The look for a few seconds (3 s, tuning) after the axolotl eats a plastic worm by mistake and spits it out (PRD N3, A10, issue #75). It is not a face change: the eyes, the mouth, the blush and a Girl's cheek heart stay as they are, so it works on every eye and mouth variant, Boy or Girl, and every morph and Dream colour. It adds a soft leaf-green cheek tint and a gentle wobble (9). **Tint.** Two cheek patches in the axolotl's local units, on the two blush spots of the near face: an ellipse at (29.5,10.6), radii 5.4 x 3.9, and one at (44,8.6), radii 4.4 x 3.1. Each is two stacked ellipses, the outer rgba(86,196,72,0.30) and an inner one at 0.6 of its radii in rgba(86,196,72,0.22), so the middle is about 0.45 and the edge is soft with no gradient. No ink, no outline. The patches sit clear of both eye zones and the cheek heart zone (6i), so a Girl's heart stays whole; they overlap the mouth corners, which is why the tint is see-through. Drawn as one baked sprite over the axolotl at depth 41 (`queasyTint`, between the axolotl at 40 and the bubbles at 50), anchored at the axolotl's local origin so it turns, tilts and scales with it. It shows only while queasy and is not a Wearable layer. **Never scary or sick-looking** (ADR 0007): no green body, no swirling eyes, no frown, no tongue. **Cue without colour.** The tint is colour only, so the banner "Bleh! That was plastic, not food." (8) and the wobble (9) carry the state; under reduced motion the banner carries it alone (10). | `derived (provisional)` |

### 6l. Face variants

Character creator options (PRD F2, F6). r is the eye radius: near 6.4, far 5.3. Eye centres are in 6c. Lid lines are clipped to the bead and drawn in ink.

**Eyes**

| Variant | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Happy (default) | The prototype bead and two glints (6c) | `cartoon.js` |
| Sleepy | Bead clipped above a lid through the eye centre, curved down 0.15r at the middle. Lid line ink 2.0. Only glint 2, moved to (-0.3r, +0.45r). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Determined | Bead clipped above a straight lid from (-r, -0.6r) at the back to (+r, -0.25r) at the snout side, so it slopes down toward the snout. Lid line ink 2.0. Glints are clipped with the bead. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Curious | Bead radius 1.1r (near 7.04, far 5.83). Glint 1 radius 0.45r. No lid. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Grumpy | Half-shut (issue #87). Bead clipped above a straight lid from (-r, -0.2r) at the back to (+r, +0.2r) at the snout side, so the lid runs through the eye centre and slopes down toward the snout, and the lower half of the bead shows. Lid line ink 2.0. Only glint 2. Plus one short brow over each eye (Grumpy brows below). It tells from Determined by the bead shown, the glints and the brow. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Grumpy brows** (issue #87). Only Grumpy has brows, on a Boy and on a Girl. Each is a short straight stroke over the snout-side half of its bead, sloping down toward the snout.

| Detail | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Near brow | From (28,-13.9) to (32.5,-12.45): 4.73 long, about 17.9 degrees down toward the snout. It sits between the near eye's top lash and the far eye's lashes. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Far brow | From (42.4,-10.8) to (46.8,-9.35): 4.63 long, about 18.2 degrees down toward the snout. It sits low, between the far lashes and the head's top edge, just above the lid. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Ink | Ink 2.0 over a white under-stroke 3.6, the lash widths (6u), but with round caps, set for the brow only, then restored (4b). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Layer | On the eye layer (6a layer 12), after its bead. It paints outside the blink squash: squashed to the blink's 12% it would drop onto the closed eye and thin to about 0.4 units. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Clearances | The brow ink keeps 1.0 or more from every Grumpy lash ink at every blink (1.26 near, 1.13 far), and its white stays off the lash ink and off the open eye's lid ink. It stays inside the head, LW / 2 in from its outline, below y -18.5 (1 under the Head slot), inside the `face` picture crop and clear of the mouth zone. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Eye rules**

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Zones | All eye marks stay inside the eye zones (6i). A Girl's eyelashes reach past them: every painted corner, white under-stroke and ink, stays inside the lash reach circle (6i) at any blink (6u). A Grumpy brow is not an eye mark: it has its own bounds (Grumpy brows above). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Shape | No sharp shapes. The only brows are Grumpy's two short brows (above). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Eyelashes (Girl) | Every eye variant carries a Girl's three eyelashes (6u): three separate strokes aimed at the same three points at the upper back of the lash reach circle (6i). On Happy and Curious they grow out from under the bead's upper back. On Sleepy, Determined and Grumpy they root on the back half of the lid line and fan up into the space above the lid. They are not brows: no lash runs across the top of the eye, and the lid lines stay as above. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Visible bead | Never less than about 45% of the round bead area. Grumpy shows half. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Colour | The morph eye colour and any ring (3b, 3f) apply to every variant. | `derived (provisional)` |
