The creator shows the mouths in this order, with these labels: Smile, Open D, Tongue out, Small o, Big grin, Cat smile, Smirk, Blep. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Mouth rules.** All mouths stay inside the mouth zone (6i). No teeth: no mouth paints white. Every two mouths differ in at least one trait: fill or no fill, a one-sided line, the number of dips in the lower edge, a tongue (or a tongue at least twice the area of the other's), or ink box width, height or lowest point by 2 or more. On Melanoid the unfilled mouths (Smile, Cat smile, Smirk, Blep) are ink lines on a body that is only 1.74:1 with ink (section 12).

### 6m. Gill styles

Character creator option (PRD F2). Fluffy is the prototype (6d). Lengths are the stem length per gill. Bend is the sideways bow (near / far).

| Style | Near stems (top / middle / bottom) | Far stems (top / middle / bottom) | Side filaments per side | Filament length | Tip pair length | Bend (near / far) | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Fluffy (default) | 34 / 37 / 31 | 28 / 32 / 26 | 3, at u 0.35, 0.6, 0.82 | 13 x (1.05 - 0.55u) | 8 | 5 / 4 | `cartoon.js` |
| Long | 44 / 48 / 40 | 36 / 42 / 34 | 2, at u 0.5, 0.8 | 13 x (1.05 - 0.55u) | 10 | 6 / 5 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Short | 24 / 26 / 22 | 20 / 22 / 18 | 2, at u 0.45, 0.8 | 9 x (1.05 - 0.55u) | 6 | 4 / 3 | `derived (provisional)` |

Rules:

- Roots, angles, widths, colours, and flutter never change. So the gill-root zone (6i) is the same for every style.
- Styles read by silhouette: length and filament count.
- Long tips reach about y -63 local at Young. Age scales stems, filaments and tip pairs (6t): Grown-up Long gill ink reaches about y -74.4, which sets the top of the texture frame (section 11).

Source for the rules: `derived (provisional)`.

### 6n. Spots

Character creator option (PRD F2). Layer 8. Colours come from the spot table in 3b. Flat fills, no ink outline. Never on the head (x > 0). Tail marks use tail space (6i).

| Option | Marks | Source |
|---|---|---|
| None | No marks | `derived (provisional)` |
| Freckles | Ten tiny round specks, r 0.8 to 1.2, in one cluster on the shoulders, between the near middle and bottom gill stems: (-22,-10.5) r 1.0, (-27,-9.5) r 1.2, (-32,-8) r 0.9, (-20,-6.5) r 1.1, (-24,-6.5) r 0.8, (-29,-5.5) r 1.1, (-34,-4.5) r 1.0, (-22,-3) r 0.9, (-26,-3.5) r 1.0, (-31,-2.5) r 0.8. Centres span x -34 to -20 and y -10.5 to -2.5 (about 14 x 8); specks are at least 0.8 apart, edge to edge. No tail marks. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Dots | Six even, medium round dots, all r 2.8, evenly spaced along the back and then down the tail centre. Body: (-18,-5), (-28,-6.3), (-36,-3.2). Tail: s 0.34 v -0.1, s 0.54 v 0, s 0.7 v +0.1. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Patches | Three big, soft blotches, each of area about 113. Body: (-25,-5) ellipse 8.8 x 4.1, not rotated. Tail: s 0.2 v -0.15 ellipse 6.2 x 5.8, and s 0.47 v 0 ellipse 6 x 6. On a short tail (a Short tail, or any Baby tail) the two tail patches can overlap into one longer blotch (section 12). | `derived (provisional)` |

**Spot rules**

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Tell apart | The options step up in size and drop in count: 10 Freckles; 6 Dots, each 4 times or more the area of the biggest Freckle; 3 Patches, each 4 times or more the area of a Dot. They also differ in placement: a shoulder cluster, an even line along the back and tail, or big blotches over the back and tail. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Every look | Body marks move onto the look's torso (6g) and stay inside it; tail marks stay on the tail core at every swim phase and tail amplitude up to 1.6. No mark reaches the head (x > 0). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Clear of the gills | At the still pose, most of each option's mark area shows past the near gill stems on every look: at least 70% for Freckles and Dots, at least 50% for Patches. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Not a morph mark | Freckles put 9 or more specks in one 15 x 9 box, where no morph puts more than 4 marks (Wild reaches 4). Every Dot is 1.75 times or more the radius of the biggest Wild or Copper speckle or fleck (r 1.6). Every Patch is twice the area of the biggest Piebald patch (r 4.2) or more. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Picture buttons | The Spots step pictures use the `spots` crop of the body and tail (section 11). | `derived (provisional)` |

Spots draw on top of any Dream colour (PRD F14). Wild's own speckles and flecks are a morph extra (3b), not a spot option. On a Wild with no Dream colour they stay, and player spots add to them. While a Dream colour is on, they are off with the other morph extras (6h); only the player spots show. Copper speckles and glints and Piebald patches are morph extras too (3b), not player spot options, and follow the same rule. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

### 6o. Sizes

Character creator option (PRD F2, F4). Large is the old Medium and the prototype scale; Medium is the old Small.

| Size | Scale | Length in the scene (L = 127 x 0.95 x scale) | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Small | x0.72 | About 87 px | `derived (provisional)` |
| Medium (default) | x0.85 | About 103 px | `derived (provisional)` |
| Large | x1.00 (prototype) | About 121 px | `cartoon.js` |

Lengths are for the prototype proportions (L = 127). The default Boy (L 131) is about 90 / 106 / 124 px. Over every Tail, Body, Age and Boy or girl (L 101.6 to 147), lengths run from about 70 px (a Small Baby Girl with a Short tail) to about 140 px (a Large Grown-up Chubby Boy with a Long tail).

Rules:

- Uniform scale about local (0,0), including ink, wearables, and the Dream overlay (the 4a exception).
- Size is looks only. The hit area and speed never change (PRD F4).
- Age changes proportions only, never this scale (6t).
- Every 6i clearance holds at every size, because it is defined in local units.
- The live axolotl texture is baked at its base bake scale times this scale, so a Large axolotl is baked big, never upscaled. A size change re-bakes it (section 11).
- Effects follow the size: the leg-loss puff, regrowth twinkles and snack speed-up star sit on the look's leg roots at every size, and the protected ring and carry bubble scale with it and grow for a look that reaches further (6q).

Source for the rules: `derived (provisional)`.

### 6p. Dream colours on the body

PRD F14, F15. Slot colours and contrast checks are in 3f. Tail marks use tail space (6i).

**Rules**

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Slots | The 3f values replace the body, far side, fin, belly, and gill slots. Eye and mouth come from the real morph. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Morph extras | Off (6h). As built, a Dream palette keeps none of the morph's speckles, flecks, shimmer, matte shine, morph rim, or fin rays. Its only extras are the Dream spot colour (3b: white at 0.45 on Midnight blue, Galaxy, and Tie-dye galaxy; ink at 0.30 on the rest), the rim light, and the eye ring below. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Pattern overlay | Layer 6.5. Clipped to the silhouette and fin fill, and only where x <= 14, so the face keeps the plain Dream body colour. On Hearts, Tie-dye, and Iridescent the clip is inset 2 units from the silhouette and fin edges, so the colour next to the ink is always the body. Otherwise the Tie-dye #ff7fbf band would drop to 2.49:1 and the Iridescent stripe to 2.46:1 (section 10). On Rainbow, Galaxy, and Tie-dye galaxy the overlay runs to the edge under the rim light. Rainbow's head is band 1, which is its body colour. As built, on strongly waved tails up to 2 points per fin edge are trimmed from the inset clip at the tail tip, so the 2-unit inset holds; about the last 0.14 of s then shows the plain body colour on Hearts, Tie-dye, and Iridescent (12). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Dream marker | Three 4-point stars (4e), R 2.6, white with ink 1.6. On the top fin band at tail points s 0.55, 0.68, and 0.81, centred between the core edge and the fin edge. Shown on every Dream colour, solid or patterned. Real morphs never carry it. As built, it paints last in layer 6.5, over the pattern and the rim light. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Rim light and eye ring | Per 3f. As built: the rim light (2 px #cec9f0) is on Midnight blue, Rainbow, Galaxy, and Tie-dye galaxy. On a Dream colour the rim pass that real morphs paint before layer 6.5 (under the shines) is skipped, and the rim light paints in layer 6.5 over the pattern, then the marker. The eye ring (1.5 px #cec9f0) is always on the three dark Dream colours (Midnight blue, Galaxy, and Tie-dye galaxy). On every other Dream colour the morph's own eye ring, if it has one, stays with its eye. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Spots | Per 3b and 6n. Drawn on top, at layer 8, over the whole overlay. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Progress | A Dream colour never changes perks, the real morph, or Collection Book unlocks. | `derived (provisional)` |

|---|---|---|
| Hearts | Seven hearts, each 6 wide x 5.5 tall, #ff5f8d, no outline, rotated alternately +0.3 and -0.3 rad. Body: (-24,-7), (-12,6), (-2,-10), (6,15). Tail: s 0.25 v +0.3, s 0.5 v -0.3, s 0.75 v +0.1. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Tie-dye | On the torso, an Archimedean spiral of three repeating bands (#ff7fbf, #7fd4ff, #ffe14a), each 5 wide, 2 turns, centred (-10,3), outer radius about 18. On the tail, the same bands as slanted stripes every 0.12 of s. 1.2 px dividers rgba(255,255,255,0.8). As built, the spiral is one 5-wide stroke that changes band colour every half-turn, with a radial 1.2 px divider at each change. Each tail stripe's top end leans 0.04 of s toward the tip (stripe slant, provisional). | `derived (provisional)` |

It glides from x 14 to the tail tip every 4 s (section 9). Static at x -10 under reduced motion. As built, the white stripe is centred on the sheen x on the body axis, and the tint stripe trails it after the 2-unit gap. The glide ends at each look's own tail tip (6r to 6u). The stripe is also static at x -10 on picture buttons. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Rainbow | Six bands across the body axis. Band 1 #ff6b6b is the head (x > 14). Bands 2 to 6 (#ffa64d, #ffe14a, #7fe07f, #6cb8ff, #b07cff) split x 14 to -74 equally, about 17.6 each, with boundaries at x -3.6, -21.2, -38.8 (tail s 0.2) and -56.4 (s 0.6). Dividers 1.2 px rgba(255,255,255,0.8). Rim light. As built, bands 2 to 6 split x 14 to each look's own tail tip equally (-74 is the prototype tail tip), and the band 1 to 2 divider sits at x 14 - 0.6, so the face clip keeps all of it. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Galaxy | Base #2b2f7a. Two nebula ellipses rgba(255,127,191,0.35): body (-14,0) 14 x 8 rotated -0.2, and tail s 0.45 v 0 10 x 6. Ten white 4-point stars, no outline: body (-26,-8) R 3, (-16,8) R 2, (-6,-6) R 2.4, (4,10) R 1.8; tail s 0.1 v -0.3 R 2, s 0.2 v -0.4 R 2.2, s 0.35 v +0.3 R 1.6, s 0.5 v -0.2 R 2.6, s 0.65 v +0.35 R 1.8, s 0.8 v -0.1 R 2. Plus four white dots r 0.8 between them. Rim light and eye ring. Dot positions, as built (provisional): body (-21,0) and (-1,2); tail s 0.27 v 0 and s 0.72 v +0.1. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Tie-dye galaxy | Base #3a2f7a. The Tie-dye spiral and tail stripes using two bands only (#ff7fbf, #7fd4ff) at 0.75 alpha, with the white dividers. Six white 4-point stars, R 1.6 to 2.6, in the gaps. Rim light and eye ring. Star positions, as built (provisional), midway between spiral turns and one past the outer turn: (-5.1,3) R 1.6, (-10,10.1) R 1.8, (-19.3,3) R 2.0, (-10,-8.4) R 2.2, (3.6,3) R 2.4, (-28.1,3) R 2.6. | `derived (provisional)` |

### 6q. Legs, leg loss, regrowth, and protected time

PRD A5 to A8, ADR 0008. Always gentle: sparkles and outlines, never gore.

**Leg-root effects at every look (PRD F4, issue #49).** The leg-loss puff, the regrown burst and the snack speed-up star are placed at the look's own leg root (6e), taken from the look the axolotl texture currently paints and mapped to the world through the sprite's position, scale, facing and rotation. So they sit on the leg root at every size, Age, Body and Boy or girl. The puff, burst and star keep their own size; only their place follows the look. The regrowth twinkles by the toes are part of the axolotl rig (6a), so they follow the look with the leg. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Legs on the body**

| State | Look | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Present | Normal leg (6e). | `cartoon.js` |
| Lost | A ghost outline round the leg and toes: one outline of capsules, radius 8.2 round the leg tube and 5.6 round each toe, so it sits just outside the inked leg. First a white under-line 3.6 wide at alpha 0.275, then dashed ink 1.6 wide, dash 3, gap 3, at alpha 0.55. No fill. It paddles like a normal leg. Faint on purpose: not a cue object (section 10); the legs display shows leg state. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Regrowing | The same ghost, a little firmer: ink at alpha 0.75, under-line at 0.375. Inside it a whole small leg grows out from the leg root: its leg and toe lengths and tube widths are the normal leg's times the progress, and its outline is times (0.5 + 0.5 x progress). It has the slot colour (body colour for near legs, far side for far legs). Nothing grows below progress 0.04. Progress shows in steps of 0.1. Three twinkles (4e) sit by the toes: one each side of the toe end (11 out to the side and 3 along) and one 14 past it. Their arm radius is 3.8 x (0.6 + 0.4 x progress), times a pulse of 0.6 to 1.0 on the swim cycle. At 100% the regrown burst plays: three yellow eight-arm Tail Dash sparkles (7e) round the leg root. Motion in section 9. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Snack speed-up | A 4-point star R 7 (white, ink 1.8) at the helped leg's root (the look's root, above), drawn after the axolotl. It is a cue object (section 10): ink at alpha 1.0 while shown, and it never blinks. It is not shown with four legs. It is hidden once the trip leaves swimming. A leg a snack finishes also gets the normal regrown burst. Motion in section 9. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Socks and Feet, present leg | The leg wears the axolotl's Socks and Feet items (6i), drawn with it at layers 2.5 (far legs) and 3.5 (near legs), in the leg's frame, so they turn with the paddle. With four legs all four wear them. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Socks and Feet, lost leg | Neither is drawn. The leg is the ghost outline above, exactly the bare leg and toes, never enlarged for the Sock or Shoe it wore. The Sock and Shoe disappear on the same frame as the leg, under the same puff at the leg root, with no fade and no extra puff at the foot (the leg's own tube and toes also switch to the ghost under that one puff). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Socks and Feet, regrowing leg | Neither is drawn at any progress: they would float on a half-grown leg. The ghost, the growing leg and the twinkles by the toes are as above. At 100% the regrown burst (three sparkles) plays and the Sock and Shoe appear with the whole leg on that frame, with no fade. Under reduced motion the same: the burst is three still sparkles, and the Sock and Shoe appear at once. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Socks and Feet, no leg left | Not reachable: losing the fourth leg ends the trip (A11). With at least one leg left the Socks and Feet perks keep working, so the leg state changes the picture only (6i feet rule). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Never | Blood, a wound, a stump, or a detached limb. The leg never falls off. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Leg-loss puff.** At the lost leg's root (the look's root, 6e): one white cloud, drawn as a `blob` with ink 3.5. It is a centre puff 14 x 13 with six puffs round it, their centres on an ellipse 17 x 15.5, radius 11 and 9.5 in turn (each 0.92 as tall). One shine arc #cfeeff, 2.2 wide, radius 8.4, at the upper left. Five twinkles ring the cloud, 38 to 40 out, arm 5, 6 or 7: sun yellow plus arms 3.6 wide and diagonal arms 2.2 wide, each with ink 1.8 a side, and a white core r 2.2. The whole puff shows at scale 0.8. It covers the switch to the ghost outline. Colours are always white, sun yellow and ink, never red. Motion in section 9. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Legs display (HUD).** A white #ffffff panel (section 8 panel style): the label "Legs", then four leg icons in a row. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

| Detail | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Icon box | 32 x 32 px, gap 4, panel padding 12. The label is bold ink 22 px, 6 px before the first icon. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Icon shape | Leg capsule from (16,9) to (16,16), colour width 7.2. Three toe capsules from (16,16), length 9.5, colour width 3.6, splayed -0.95, 0, and +0.95 rad from straight down. Ink 2.4 (4b). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Present | Solid ink outline, fill #e8567e, one shine: a short white line rgba(255,255,255,0.75), 1.8 wide, at the upper left of the leg | `derived (provisional)` |
| Regrowing (at most one) | Dashed ink outline (2.4, dash 2, gap 4.6). Fill #e8567e rises from the bottom of the icon box (the toes) toward the root, in proportion to progress, in steps of 0.1. A small 4-point star at the top-right of the icon box: 14 px box, tip radius 5.8, curved sides, fill #ffe14a, ink 1.8. It is not the 4e star. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Snack speed-up badge | Ink-only snack icon, 16 px, at the bottom-right of the icon of the leg the latest snack helped, shown for 1.2 s. A leg a snack finishes keeps the badge on its now-present icon, not on the next leg that starts. It reads in greyscale by shape. It is hidden once the trip leaves swimming. Motion in section 9. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Lost | Dashed ink outline only, no fill | `derived (provisional)` |
| Order | Left to right: present, regrowing, lost | `derived (provisional)` |

In greyscale, fill against empty is 3.46:1, solid and dashed outlines differ, and the star marks regrowing. No state relies on hue (A8, X2). Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Protected ring** (replaces any "invulnerability flash")

| Detail | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Shape | An ellipse centred on the axolotl's anchor, local (0,0), radii 84 x 54 for the default look at Large (Size below) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Lines | Outer line ink #2b2350 2.0. Inner line #ffffff 2.4, on an ellipse 2.2 smaller in both radii. Both continuous and opaque: no fill, no dots, no dashes. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Glint | A white arc, 3 wide and 0.5 rad long, centred at the upper left (angle 1.25 pi, y down), on an ellipse 2.7 inside the ink line, so it stays inside the ink. It never moves. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Alpha | Opaque (alpha 1.0) and constant for the whole protected time (2 s, tuning), shown only while swimming. No blinking, no pulsing, no fill over the hero. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Motion | None of its own: the ring is static, so it is also the reduced-motion frame. It follows the axolotl and turns with its tilt and the leg-count wobble. It is never mirrored: facing left, it keeps its scale, so the glint stays at the upper left. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Cue | The ring shape is the greyscale cue: a continuous double line, ink outside and white inside. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Layer | Depth 38 (`halo`), just behind the axolotl (6a), so the axolotl paints over the ring where they overlap. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Size | Sized for the default look at Large (as built: ring radii 84 x 54 scene px and carry-home bubble radius 80, both round local (0,0)). A look that reaches further grows each by one uniform look scale, then both are scaled by the axolotl's size factor (x0.72 / x0.85 / x1.00, 6o), so they keep their clearances at every look and size. Reach is the look's ink box (outline included) over a whole swim cycle at tail amplitude 1.6 and a whole gill flutter cycle; size and the look's face, mouth and spots do not change it. Ring look scale: 1 + 0.95 x the larger of (side reach - 81.5) / 84 and (bottom reach - 52.6) / 54, never under 1. Side reach is the farther of the tail and the snout; 81.5 and 52.6 are the default look's. So the ring keeps the default look's 6.6 px from the tail tip and 4.1 px below. The gills poke through its top on the default look too, so the top is not measured. Bubble look scale: 1 + 0.95 x (farthest reach on any side - 81.5) / 80, never under 1, so it keeps the default look's 2.6 px from the farthest ink. The default look and every look reaching no further keep 84 x 54 and 80. The longest look (Grown-up Chubby Boy, Long tail, side reach 97.5) gets about 99.2 x 63.8 and 95.2. Measured once per look change, never per frame. Both are placed from the sprite's scale and the texture's bake scale. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Distinct | Distinct from the carried-home bubble (below) by shape: the ring is an empty double-line ellipse behind the axolotl; the bubble is a filled circle in front of it. They never show together: the ring shows only while swimming, the bubble only while carried home. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Carried-home bubble.** PRD A11. After the fourth leg is lost, the axolotl floats home inside a big bubble. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

| Detail | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Shape | A circle centred on the axolotl's anchor, local (0,0), radius 80 for the default look at Large. It grows by the bubble look scale and the size factor (protected ring, Size). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Paint | Fill rgba(255,255,255,0.38) (the bubble fill). A white rim rgba(255,255,255,0.9), 3 wide, on a circle 3.4 inside the edge. Outline rgba(43,35,80,0.85), 2.0 (the bubble stroke). Glint: a white arc 5 wide on a circle 0.76 of the radius, from 1.1 pi to 1.36 pi (upper left), plus a white dot r 3.2 at (-0.58, -0.3) times the radius. | `derived (provisional)` |
| When | Only in the carrying phase. It appears and goes at once, with no fade. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Layer | Depth 50 (`bubbles`), in front of the axolotl. Never rotated or mirrored. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Motion | None of its own. It moves with the axolotl as it floats home (section 9). Under reduced motion it is still too: the axolotl rests in the bubble where it was for 1.5 s. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Not a cue object | It shows only while the axolotl is carried home, and that phase takes no input, so there is nothing to find or dodge. The trip-end card (below) then says what happened in text. Its outline is ink at 0.85, so it does not meet the pair rule, and it does not need to (section 10). | `derived (provisional)` |

**Trip-end card.** The trip-end card is the tank arrival card (8), shown over the Home Tank after the carry. It fades in over 0.4 s (alpha 0 to 1). Under reduced motion it appears at once, as a still card. It leaves at once. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

### 6r. Tail length

Character creator option (PRD F2). Looks only. All values are `derived (provisional)` except the prototype Medium tail (`cartoon.js`).

| Tail | Core length, root to tip | With the Boy cue (6u) | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Short | 36 | 40 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Medium (default) | 44 (prototype: x -30 to -74) | 48 | `cartoon.js`, `derived (provisional)` |
| Long | 52 | 56 | `derived (provisional)` |

**Rules**

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Baby | A Baby's core length is x0.85 (6t), after the Boy cue. The shortest core is a Baby Girl's Short tail (30.6); the longest is a Boy's Long tail at Young or Grown-up (56). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Root | The tail root sits 15 units in front of the torso's back end (x -30 on the prototype torso, whose back end is x -45). So Body and Age move it: x -30 for Slim and Regular, -34 for Chubby at Young; Baby and Grown-up per 6t. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Half-height | 13 x torso ry / 19 at the root (13 on the prototype torso; 6s), tapering to 2 at the tip with the prototype curve. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Fins | The fin band start (tail point 3 of 14), the rays (points 5, 8, 11) and the fin reach (2 plus 9 top, 2 plus 7 bottom) are the prototype's at every length. A Boy's reach is x1.6, and his fin band starts at tail point 1 (6u). | `cartoon.js`, `derived (provisional)` |
| Wave | Amplitude 11 x s, as the prototype, over the look's tail. | `cartoon.js` |
| Tail space | s runs over the look's tail, so tail marks (spots, morph marks, the Tail slot anchor) stay on the core at every length (6i). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Greyscale | Length reads by silhouette. | `derived (provisional)` |

### 6s. Body

Character creator option (PRD F2). Looks only. All values `derived (provisional)` except Regular (`cartoon.js`).

| Body | Torso radii (Young) | Torso centre (Young) | Belly radii (Young) | Belly centre (Young) | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Slim | 37 x 15 | (-8,4) | 26 x 4.2 | (-6,12) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Regular (default) | 37 x 19 (prototype) | (-8,4) | 26 x 6.2 (prototype) | (-6,14) | `cartoon.js` |
| Chubby | 39 x 24 | (-10,4) | 28 x 9.5 | (-8,15.7) | `derived (provisional)` |

These are a Boy's values. A Girl adds 3 to the torso y radius, 3 to the belly y radius and 2 to the belly x radius (6u). Body depth (twice the torso y radius) is 30 / 38 / 48 for a Boy and 36 / 44 / 54 for a Girl, at every Age. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Rules**

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Head joint | The torso's front end stays at x 29 for every option, so the torso centre x is 29 - rx, and a wider torso grows only toward the tail. The head never moves. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Torso centre line | y 4 for every option. | `cartoon.js` |
| Belly | Its x radius and its offset from the torso centre (+2) stretch with Age (6t). Its bottom edge stays 2.8 above the torso's lowest point, so a taller belly grows upward. A Girl adds 3 to both the torso and belly y radii and 2 to the belly x radius (6u). The belly always stays inside the torso. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Marks and legs | Leg roots, body marks and the body shine move onto the look's torso (6e, 6g). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Tail | The tail root and its half-height follow the torso (6r). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Clearly different | Neighbouring Bodies differ in depth by 8 or more and by 20% or more, as a Boy or a Girl, at every Age. At the smallest drawn scale (Small in the shrunk preview, 0.432 px per unit) that is at least 3 px. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Greyscale | Body reads by silhouette depth. | `derived (provisional)` |

### 6t. Age

Character creator option (PRD F2). Age changes proportions only, never the size scale (6o). The head, face, gill roots and the Head, Neck and Face anchors never move, so a Baby's head looks bigger from its shorter body, tail and gills. All values `derived (provisional)` except Young (`cartoon.js`).

| Age | Torso x radius | Tail core length | Gill length | Legs and toes | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Baby | Body value - 6 (31 for Slim and Regular, 33 for Chubby) | x0.85 | x0.8 | x0.7 long (widths kept) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Young (default) | Body value (prototype) | x1 | x1 | x1 | `cartoon.js` |
| Grown-up | Body value + 2 (39 for Slim and Regular, 41 for Chubby) | x1 | x1.12 | x1 | `derived (provisional)` |

**Rules**

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Torso | The head joint stays at x 29 (6s): the Baby torso's back end moves forward (x -33 for Regular, tail root -18), the Grown-up's back (x -49, tail root -34). The torso y radius comes from Body and the Girl cue only. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Belly | Its x radius and its +2 offset from the torso centre scale by the torso x radius over the Body value. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Gills | The factor scales every stem, side filament and tip pair. Roots, angles, bends, widths, colours and flutter never change, so the gill-root zone (6i) holds. Grown-up Long gill ink reaches about y -74.4 (section 11). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Legs | Leg and toe lengths only; tube widths and outlines stay as 6e. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Greyscale | Age reads by proportion: head size against body, tail, gill and leg length. | `derived (provisional)` |

### 6u. Boy or girl cues

Character creator option (PRD F2): Boy or Girl, always set, fixed after creation. Looks only. Text about one axolotl says "he" or "she" to match (8, tank arrival message). All values `derived (provisional)`.

**Clear, not subtle (issue #58).** The owner approved clear Boy and Girl cues in issue #58. This replaces issue #52's rule that the cues are kept subtle (PRD F2). The cues read at every size, on all eight real morphs, in colour and in greyscale. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

| Cue | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Boy: longer tail | Tail core +4 (6r), before any Baby factor. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Boy: taller fins | Top and bottom fin reach x1.6: peaks 2 + 14.4 = 16.4 (top) and 2 + 11.2 = 13.2 (bottom) beyond the core, against a Girl's 11 and 9. That is at least 1.45 times a Girl's peak reach, top and bottom, at every Tail, Body and Age. Rays as 6r. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Boy: longer fin band | The fin bands and the core edge lines start at tail point 1 (s about 0.07), not tail point 3, so the fin reads longer. A fin-free morph rim (Wild, Axanthic) ends there too (3b). The Tail slot anchor (tail point 4, 6i) stays past it. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Girl: rounder torso and belly | Torso y radius +3, belly y radius +3 and belly x radius +2 (6s). A Girl is 6 deeper than a Boy of the same Body and Age. The tail root half-height grows with the torso (6r). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Girl: eyelashes | Three lashes per eye, on every eye variant (6l), on the eye layer (6a layer 12), above every Wearable layer. Each is an ink line 2.0 wide over a white under-stroke 3.6 wide (0.8 of white shows each side), with butt caps (set for the lashes only, then restored), painted inside the eye's blink squash, so a blink squashes them too. Each lash aims from its root at a point on a circle 0.05 inside the lash reach circle (eye radius + 4.0, 6i): angle pi + 0.5, pi + 0.85 or pi + 1.2 (the upper back, facing right, y down). It stops where the first corner of its 3.6 white stroke meets that circle, so every painted corner, white and ink, stays inside the lash reach circle at any blink. Every lash ends past the eye zone edge (eye radius + 1.8), so it reads at Small. Least ink clear of the bead, lowest of both eyes: Happy 3.77, Sleepy 3.52, Determined 3.54, Curious 3.15, Grumpy 3.52 (floor 2.5). At the smallest drawn scale (Small in the shrunk preview, 0.432 px per unit) Curious shows 1.36 px (floor 1 px). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Lashes on open eyes | Happy and Curious: roots 1 inside the bead edge on the same three angles, so each lash runs straight out along its radius. Lengths: Happy 4.79 near, 4.77 far; Curious 4.15 near, 4.24 far. The bead paints after the lashes and covers their roots. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Lashes on lidded eyes | Sleepy, Determined and Grumpy: roots on the lid line at 0.3, 0.42 and 0.55 of the way (in curve parameter) from where it meets the bead edge at its back end to its middle, so they fan from the lid up into the space above it, past the bead edge. Lengths, near eye: Sleepy 6.23 / 8.07 / 9.73, Determined 5.07 / 5.69 / 6.19, Grumpy 5.65 / 7.00 / 8.45 (far eye shorter; Grumpy far 5.27 / 6.36 / 7.56, on the issue #87 lid). Both corners of each root end sit inside the bead circle, so the bead covers every root below the lid line. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Lash contrast | The white under-stroke is there because ink on the Melanoid body #4f4a6e is only 1.74:1. Ink vs white is 14.39:1. A lash reads on every morph when the white edge or the ink gets 3:1 or more against the body, and the white edge does wherever the ink falls short. White vs body, then ink vs body: Leucistic 1.62, 8.86; Wild 2.12, 6.80; Golden albino 1.42, 10.13; White albino 1.08, 13.38; Copper 1.56, 9.24; Piebald 1.13, 12.75; Axanthic 4.53, 3.18; Melanoid 8.28, 1.74. So the ink carries the lash on the six light bodies, both carry it on Axanthic, and the white edge carries it on Melanoid. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Not brows | Lashes sit at the upper back of each eye as separate strokes, never one line across the top (6l). Grumpy's brows belong to the eye variant, on a Boy and a Girl alike, so they are not a Girl cue. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Girl: cheek heart | Issue #87. A small heart on the near cheek, below and behind the near eye, between the gill-root zone and the mouth zone, near the near blush. The curve is x = 16 sin³ t, y = -(13 cos t - 5 cos 2t - 2 cos 3t - cos 4t) (y down), scaled to 7.00 wide (about 6.33 tall) about the centre (18.2,9.6), the middle of its smallest enclosing circle. Paint: a white under-stroke 3.2, then the fill #ff5c9a (3a) over its inner half, then the ink outline 1.6, so 0.8 of white shows outside the ink. Layer 10.2: after the blush (10), before the Head item (10.5). Drawn only on a Girl, and only when the palette has the blush on, so a palette with the blush off (the Collection Book silhouette) shows none. All its paint sits in the cheek heart zone (6i), radius about 5.32, whose edge is 13.1 from the near eye's centre. Every Wearable keeps off that zone. Ink vs the fill is 4.97:1 and ink vs white 14.39:1 (10). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Greyscale | Boy: longer tail, taller fins and a fin band that starts nearer the body. Girl: eyelashes, the cheek heart and a rounder body and belly. On the Boy or girl step each picture button also has its label, "Boy" or "Girl", and the boy or girl sign: a circle with an arrow, or a circle with a cross (8). | `derived (provisional)` |

## 7. Environment kit

### 7a. Elements in the prototype

| Element | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Sky | Vertical gradient #4fc3ff to #c4f1ff down to SURF + 4 | `cartoon.js` |
| Sun | Centre (38,32), radius 13, #ffe14a with `shape` outline. Eight rounded rays #ffb52e from radius +6 to +11, tube 3.2 wide. Shine arc rgba(255,255,255,0.9). | `cartoon.js` |
| Clouds | Three, at (118,24) scale 1.0, (212,52) scale 0.8, (262,18) scale 0.7. Each is four circles plus a flat base ellipse, #ffffff with a #cfeeff shine arc. | `cartoon.js` |
| Willow trees | Two, at x 352 and x 440. Brown curved trunk, six drooping strands behind a column of seven foliage ellipses. | `cartoon.js` |
| Chinampa bank | Soil block on the right (x 296 to 486, y 40 to 84), #a2623a with soil dots and a strata line in #7f4a2a, plus a #63d94f grass cap with a scalloped lower edge (12 px scallops) | `cartoon.js` |
| Water | Gradient 3 stops, wavy top edge (see section 9 for the wave) | `cartoon.js` |
| Light rays | Three shafts at x 120, 250, 372 (widths 30, 38, 26), slanting 70 px to the left | `cartoon.js` |
| Canal bed | Wobbly top edge at y 318 to 326; gradient #b78552 to #8c5e3a; lip line; 12 mud dots; 16 pebbles in five colours with a white shine | `cartoon.js` |
| Reeds | Two clusters of six (x 20 to 80 and x 400 to 460). Stalk, leaf, and sometimes a cattail head. Sway only. | `cartoon.js` |
| Lily pads | Three, at x 90 (rx 24), x 160 (rx 26, with a flower), x 250 (rx 21), all ry 5.2, resting on the surface and tilting with the wave | `cartoon.js` |
| Flower | Five petals (four #ff82bf, one front petal #ffb4dc with a white highlight) and a #ffe14a centre | `cartoon.js` |
| Bubbles | Rise from the mouth, see section 9 | `cartoon.js` |

**Area size.** PRD N2, issue #73. An area is W x H screens, wide x tall; the PRD N2 area table gives each area's size. One screen is 480 x 360 world px, the reference viewport (1), so an area is 480W x 360H world px. The canal (Area 1) is 4 x 1, 1920 x 360, and is unchanged. A tall area (H of 2 or more) is H rows of 360 world px stacked down, and each row uses the y values above inside its own row (row r starts at world y 360 x (r - 1)). The surface is only on the top row and the bed only on the bottom row; the rows between are deep water. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

| Row | What it holds | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Top row | Sky, sun, clouds, chinampa bank, willows, lily pads and flower, the water surface and its wave at y 80 (SURF), and the light rays (they fade to 0 by y 300, as now). The clean water gradient (3c) runs from the surface to water deep #0a5c98 at y 330, then flat #0a5c98 to the row bottom. No bed, mud, pebbles or reeds. Bubbles still vanish at the surface. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Middle rows (2 to H - 1) | Deep water only: flat water deep #0a5c98, the gradient's last stop, so a row meets the next with no seam. No sky, surface, light rays, bed or reeds. Build detail: every middle row shares one flat deep-water texture. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Bottom row | Flat #0a5c98 down to the canal bed at its y above inside the row (wobbly top 318 to 326, bed gradient to 360), with its lip line, mud dots, pebbles and reeds. | `derived (provisional)` |
| One-row area (H = 1) | The top row and the bottom row in one, as the canal draws now. | `cartoon.js` |
| Haze | rgba(40,200,215,0.12) over the water of every row (3a), drawn before the hero, never over it. | `derived (provisional)` |

Cue objects keep the pair rule on deep rows with no new samples: the pair method (10) holds on any background, #0a5c98 included.

**Pond look (Area 2, issue #75).** The lily pad pond paints the same chunks as the canal, from the habitat look (`src/art/habitatLook.ts`, 3c): the sky, sun, clouds, bank, willows, surface wave at y 80 and light rays are unchanged. What changes is below. A tall pond row would use the pond deep #2f8f58 as its flat deep water in place of #0a5c98, and the bed only on the bottom row (the pond is one row tall today, so this is only the rule). All values are `derived (provisional)`.

| Part | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Water, haze, surface line | The pond gradient #a4e57a / #58bf6a / #2f8f58 at stops 0 / 0.45 / 1 (the canal stop positions), haze rgba(130,210,90,0.12) laid over the plants and bed and under the hero, surface line rgba(246,255,226,0.95) (3c) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Bed | The canal bed's shape and wobble, in the soft olive mud #b8a064 to #9a8450 with the lip #d9c48c. Soft mud: 22 mud dots rgba(120,96,47,0.45) (the canal has 12) and 5 pebbles (the canal has 16), in the same five pebble colours with the pebble shine. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Edge reeds | A cluster of six reeds and cattails at the area's left edge and one at its right edge, rooted in the bed (the base 6 below the bed edge) and leaning toward the water. They sit 6 to 54 from the area edge. The left cluster is tall, 92 to 168; the right cluster is low, 30 to 44, so it stays under the stream band (y 280 and below). Stalks #3a8a40 and #4a9a44 in turn, leaves #58a843, cattail heads #a8693a on about 6 in 10. They are painted into the background chunk as a still frame, so they do not sway (the canal's reeds are a separate swaying layer, 9). The canal paints none. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Stream | A stream flowing in at the stream mouth (below; the area's right edge in Area 2). A lighter band of water STREAM_BAND y 120 to 280 (the canal's current rows, so the current zone sits in it) that widens toward the right edge and fades out 200 to its left, in rgba(240,255,228,0.3). Five white flow lines rgba(255,255,255,0.6), 2.4 wide, each trailing from a left-pointing chevron (11 wide, 12 tall, a 36 long tail) at [from the right edge, y] [34,150], [92,180], [40,205], [104,232] and [52,258]. Two stones at the mouth, one above the band and one below: pebble D #97a6c4 centred at y 112 and pebble B #a9bfd6 at y 288, 6 in from the edge, radii 17 x 12, `shape` ink 2.6, each with a pebble shine. Static, like the rest of the chunk; anything the scene draws for a current sits over it. The chevrons all point left, the way the water runs, so the stream reads without colour. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Stream mouth (issue #98).** The stream's mouth is the right edge of the column that holds the right end of the area's current (`streamColumnFor` in `src/art/drawScenery.ts`; a current that ends exactly on a column edge maps to the column on its left), so the stream art always sits just upstream of the current. All values are `derived (provisional)`.

| Case | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Mouth at the world edge (Area 2) | The current ends in the last column, so the mouth is the area's right edge, as before, and the chunk bakes exactly what it did. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Mouth mid-world (Area 3) | The current ends at x 2240, so the mouth is x 2400, with one more column beyond it. The band and the flow lines taper to clear over the last 40 px before the mouth (`STREAM_TAPER`): the band holds its full light until 40 px before the mouth and fades to clear at it, and the flow lines fade over the same 40 px, so no hard edge shows in open water. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Mouth stones that spill | The two mouth stones are centred 6 px inside the mouth with radius 17, so they reach 11 px past it (`STREAM_STONE_OVERFLOW`). When the mouth is mid-world, the next column paints them again, shifted one chunk left, so they are not clipped at the chunk edge. Only the stones spill: the band and the flow lines are clear past the mouth. The spill draws no randomness. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Reading | In Area 3 the root arch (7b) sits just right of the mouth under the bank (the cave spans x 2432 to 2568; the bank is on the last column, x 2400 to 2880), so the stream reads as flowing from under the bank roots beside the root arch. | `derived (provisional)` |

The light rays are at 0.6 of their alpha. | `derived (provisional)` |

### 7b. Still to design

Designed in this guide: tilapia (7c), Home Spot (7d), hidden treasures, Dash pot, hint sparkle, the small cave and past-the-current hiding places, each catalogue Decoration and Wearable (as a treasure icon), and Paint shells (7e), Home Tank kit (7f), rarity badges (3e, 8), the banking celebration card and the Trip Bag icon and display (8), the tank arrival card, the Home Tank hub, the decorate controls and the Treasure Chest (8), the Collection Book pages and unfound-morph silhouettes (3g, 8), the morph badge shapes (8), the stranded axolotl's "a little lost" pose with its lost thought bubble (3g, 9), the Area exit and Area stars (7g, 8), the eight Wearable slots and how each Wearable looks on the axolotl (6i), the six perk icons (below), the Outfit tab and the explorer card (8), the Shell sparkle (9), the carried-home bubble and trip-end card (6q), and, from issue #75, the lily pad pond look (3c, 7a), the hollow log and the plastic worm (below), the queasy look (6j), the Lily green Dream colour (3f), the eleven pond treasure icons (7e) and the pond Area map marker (8), and, from issue #98, the dusk pond look of Area 3 (3c, 7a), the stream mouth (7a), the root arch and the fireflies (below), the heron (7h), the Firefly gold Dream colour (3f), the eleven Area 3 treasure icons and the 144-wide Firefly string lights tank sprite (7e, 7f) and the five Area 3 worn looks (6i).

Still to design:

- trajinera boats; chinampa crops; pollution debris;
- the looks of areas 4 to 10 (chinampa gardens, spring cave, deep canal and the rest of the PRD N2 area table). The lily pad pond (Area 2) and the pond at dusk (Area 3) are designed (3c, 7a). Their tall-area rows follow Area size (7a).

All must follow sections 2, 4, and 5. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Perk icons.** One icon per perk type (PRD F9, F10), in the 32 icon box (the Home Tank icon rules of 8): ink #2b2350 only, no fill, round joins and caps, ink 3.0. They scale with the box, so the ink does too (2.25 at 24 px, 1.7 at 18 px). They sit on a white panel or a white disc (ink 14.39:1, 3:1 or better for icons). They are always paired with the perk's name and value as text (8, Wearable display), so none relies on shape alone for its meaning; the shapes are also told apart in greyscale because they are six different outlines. Sizes: 24 px beside 18 px text in rows and the outfit summary, 32 px in detail views. Each is a still frame, so it is also the reduced-motion version. Perk icons never reuse a rarity badge shape (circle, diamond, shield, star), a morph badge shape, or the Dream swatch tile.

| Perk | Shape (coordinates in the 32 box, y down) | Greyscale cue | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Speed | A lightning bolt, one closed outline: (19,3.5) to (8.5,17.5) to (15.5,17.5) to (12.5,28.5) to (23.5,13.5) to (16.5,13.5) and back to (19,3.5). | A zig-zag outline. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Regrowth | A leg and a spark. The leg: a capsule outline from (12.5,4) to (12.5,19), 9 wide (x 8 to 17), and three toe strokes from (12.5,19) to (8,25), (12.5,26) and (17,25). The spark: the 4-point star of 4e, R 6, outline only, centre (23.5,9), ink 2.0. It echoes the legs display's regrowing leg and its small star (6q), but the star there is the yellow curved one of the regrowing mark (3g), not the 4e star. As built, the spark's left tip (x 17.5) meets the leg's right edge (x 17, inside its 3.0 ink), so the two outlines merge there (12). | A leg with toes and a star. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Dash | The Dash button's two right-pointing chevrons (`src/art/drawUi.ts`): (7,9) to (14,16) to (7,23) and (17,9) to (24,16) to (17,23). The same icon as the Dash pot and the Dash button, so a kid links them. | Two chevrons. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Shell | A scallop shell, outline only. The base: a short line from (11,27.5) to (21,27.5). The fan: from (11,27.5) up the left to (5.5,17), then three round bumps (radius 5.2, centres (10,13), (16,10.5) and (22,13)) over the top, down the right to (26.5,17) and in to (21,27.5). Two ridge strokes, ink 2.0, from (16,27) to (11.5,16) and from (16,27) to (20.5,16). The same shell family as the Paint shell and the Shell necklace (7e). | A fanned scallop. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Treasure sense | A magnifying glass with a spark. The ring: centre (13.5,13.5), radius 8.5. The handle: a line from (19.8,19.8) to (27.5,27.5). Inside the ring, a 4-point star (4e), R 4.2, outline only, ink 1.8, centred (13.5,13.5): the hint star. | A ring with a handle. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Snack magnet | A horseshoe magnet. The U: from (8,5) down to (8,16), a half circle of radius 8 round (16,16) to (24,16), up to (24,5). Two pole bands: strokes from (4.5,8.5) to (11.5,8.5) and from (20.5,8.5) to (27.5,8.5). | A U with two bands. | `derived (provisional)` |

The icons are painted with the other UI icons. Their 24 and 32 px bakes need no frame bigger than the box.

**Pond props (issue #75).** Colours are in 3g. Both are scenery or hazard props with a still frame each, so each is also its reduced-motion version. All values below are `derived (provisional)`.

Fill #a9774a, `shape` ink 3.5. Its outer ink edge lands on the bed line (the lowest face point is half the ink above it). | `derived (provisional)` |

A plastic worm is a cue object. Its fills are under 9:1 with ink (4.48:1 and 4.25:1), so it passes by its ink outline alone, which the pond contrast rule (3c) holds at 3:1 on every pond background (10).

**Dusk pond props (issue #98).** Colours are in 3g. Both are scenery with a still frame each (`src/art/drawPond.ts`, baked in `src/art/pondTextures.ts` only for a look that needs them), so each is also its reduced-motion version. All values below are `derived (provisional)`.

### 7c. Tilapia (canal hazard)

PRD A1 to A4, X1; ADR 0008. Colours are in 3g. All values below are `derived (provisional)`.

**Character rules**

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Shape | Round, cartoony, calm. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Never | Teeth. Open jaws (except the small lunge "o"). Angry brows. Red eyes. Spikes or sharp fins. Real tilapia spines are drawn as soft rounded lobes. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Never shown | Catching, holding, or eating the axolotl (A11). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Hierarchy | Never warmer or more saturated than the hero, and no tilapia fill is lighter than the hero's belly #ffe3ed. The body #c2d2bb (relative luminance 0.61) is a touch lighter than the hero body #ffb7cf (0.60), because 0.607 is the least an edge partner can be (section 10). | `derived (provisional)` |

**Construction (as built, `src/art/drawTilapia.ts`).** Local units, facing right, around the body centre. Drawn at 1 scene px per unit (no x0.95), mirrored to face left, never rotated. Each pose is its own still frame. The body is built along a curved centreline, so one set of rules makes all four poses. Below, t runs along the centreline from 0 at the tail root to 1 at the nose, and "half-widths" are multiples of the body half-width at that t, measured from the centreline toward the back (negative is toward the belly).

| Part | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Centreline | 16 equal steps from the tail root to the nose, turning evenly by the pose's bend (Poses below). The body centre is the mean of its 17 points. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Body | Half-width 8.5 + 22.5 sin(pi t) up to t 0.5, then 31 x the square root of (1 - 0.96 u²), with u = (t - 0.5) / 0.5, for a blunt round nose; times the pose's width. One smooth closed outline, `blob` ink 3.5, fill #c2d2bb. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Tail fin | Rounded fan behind the tail root. It leaves the root 7 either side, spreads to 22 x the fin spread each side at 34 back, and its round trailing edge reaches 39.25 back at the middle. No notch. Three fin rays from 4 back to 31 back, ending at 0 and 14 x the fin spread each side. `blob` ink 3.5. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Dorsal fin | Three rounded lobes along the back from t 0.24 to 0.66, about 11, 14 and 10 high (times the fin spread), with 2.5 valleys between. One fin ray per lobe. `blob` ink 3.5. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Anal fin | Ellipse under the body at t 0.26, centred 2 below the body edge, radii (10 x fin spread + 2) by (6 x fin spread + 1.5), turned 0.35 rad from the heading. `blob` ink 3.5. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Pectoral fin | Ellipse radii 11 x 6 at t 0.64, -0.12 half-widths, turned -0.5 rad from the heading. `shape` ink 2.8, drawn in front of the body. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Belly | A band clipped to the body, t 1/16 to 15/16, from -0.42 to -1.4 half-widths, so it reaches the lower body edge | `derived (provisional)` |
| Bars | Three soft bars at t 0.3, 0.43 and 0.56, from 1.2 down to 0.1 half-widths (the back half), each 4.5 wide, clipped to the body. Internal, not ink. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Gill line | Soft curve at t 0.66 from 0.62 to -0.55 half-widths, bowing back through the centreline at t 0.6, 2 px. Internal, not ink. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Fin rays | 1.5 px on the tail and dorsal fins. Internal, not ink. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Shine | Ellipse radii 12 x 3 at t 0.42, 0.66 half-widths, along the heading | `derived (provisional)` |
| Eye | Bead r 5.6, #1b1535, at t 0.82, 0.3 half-widths. Two white glints: r 0.38R at (+0.3R, -0.35R) and r 0.17R at (-0.35R, +0.35R). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Smile | Ink 2.4 curve from t 0.985, -0.2 half-widths, 10 back along the heading (7 in the wind-up), sagging 4.5 toward the belly, plus a 2.0 ink tick at its back end | `derived (provisional)` |
| Layer order | Back to front: tail fin, dorsal fin, anal fin, body (belly, bars and shine clipped inside), gill line, pectoral fin, face | `derived (provisional)` |

**Poses.** One still frame per tilapia phase.

| Pose | Phase | Centreline length | Bend | Width | Fin spread | Face | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Swim | Patrol | 112 | 0.2 rad (heading -0.1 at the root) | 1 | 1 | Open eye, smile | `derived (provisional)` |
| Wind-up (A2) | Telegraph | 108 | 3.2 rad: a coil like a drawn bow, back on the outside of the C, nose and tail both pointing right | 0.64 | 1.25 (puffed) | Open eye, short tight smile. Plus the glint and the "!" bubble below. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Lunge | Lunge | 130 | 0 (straight) | 0.8 | 0.55 (folded) | Open eye, small "o" mouth: ellipse radii 4 x 6.5 along the heading at t 0.95, -0.1 half-widths, fill #e8567e, ink 2.4 (`shape`). No streaks. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Rest | Cooldown | 106 | 0.1 rad | 1.05 | 0.8 | Closed happy eye (an upward ink 2.8 arc, 10.8 wide), smile. It reads as resting. | `derived (provisional)` |

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| After a hit | In cooldown it faces away from the axolotl. There is no contact pose. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Bump (touch without a lunge) | No pose change: the pose follows the phase only. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Size.** As built, owner confirmed 2026-10-21. The ink of the swim pose is about 158 x 87 px (x -98.8 to 59.5, y -49 to 38 from the body centre). The wind-up is about 117 x 112, the lunge about 176 x 66 and the rest pose about 152 x 85. The texture frame holds every pose: x -108 to 69, y -49 to 65, plus 6 of padding. The size and geometry are unchanged, so the hit and contact distances are unchanged: hit radius 26 px, contact radius 29 px and body radius 24 px (`TILAPIA` in `src/logic/tuning.ts`), checked by `src/logic/tilapia.test.ts` and `src/logic/encounter.test.ts`. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Telegraph signals.** The wind-up pose, the glint and the bubble show for the whole wind-up (0.9 s, never under 0.8 s; tuning). They read without colour and without motion.

| Signal | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Glint | A white cross, not the 4-point star: two round-capped tubes 24 long, 4.4 wide, with `ol` 1.8 (`inkMany`), and a white core r 3.4. It is white with no diagonal arms, so it does not read as a yellow twinkle (4e) or the eight-arm Tail Dash sparkle (7e). Centred 46 px ahead of the body centre (in the facing direction) and 12 px up, at the tilapia's depth (35). | `derived (provisional)` |
| "!" bubble | A sun yellow #ffe14a disc, r 14, ink 3 (`shape`), with one white shine arc 2.2 wide at the upper left. An ink "!": a bar 4.2 wide from y -7 to 1.6 and a dot r 2.5 at y 6.6. A thought dot below, r 3 at (3,20), the same yellow, ink 2.4. Centred 62 px above the body centre, at depth 80 (`fx`). Always upright, never mirrored or rotated. | `derived (provisional)` |

### 7d. Home Spot

PRD A13 to A15. It is a burrow, not a buoy (this resolves PRD open question 4). It sits on the canal bed where play happens and reads as an axolotl home. Colours are in 3g. All values below are `derived (provisional)`.

| Part | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Arch | Seven rounded stones (ellipses 12 x 9) in pebble colours A and C in turn (A, C, A, C, A, C, A), `shape` ink 2.6. Outer width 72, height 44. Base on the bed. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Opening | Rounded arch 40 wide, 28 tall, flat bottom, fill #1b1535. A glow ellipse rgba(255,225,74,0.25), 24 x 12, at the bottom centre. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Marker post | Tube #a56a3b, width 3.6, `ol` 2.5, rising 34 px from the arch top | `derived (provisional)` |
| Marker flag | Rounded flag 24 x 18, corner radius 5, #ffe14a, ink 2.8, carrying the house icon | `derived (provisional)` |
| House icon | Ink 2.4: a square body 10 x 8 under a rounded roof 14 wide and 6 tall. Reuse it for any "go home" UI. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Safe-area edge | Opaque white #ffffff dots of radius 3 with an ink 2.0 stroke (section 10), every 16 px along the safe-area circle, drawn behind the axolotl. The circle radius is gameplay tuning. The tilapia never crosses it. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Ring | A circle of radius 72 (tuning) centred on the trip start (120, 200 in the canal). It sits strictly inside the tilapia keep-out rectangle (x 40 to 300, from the water line to the bed top): the ring spans x 48 to 192 and y 128 to 272. The keep-out stays a rectangle; only the ring is drawn. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Ring dot count | One dot per 16 px of circumference, rounded: count = round(2 x pi x radius / 16), evenly spaced. Radius 72 gives 28 dots, 16.2 px apart. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Placement | Burrow base on the bed, directly below the ring centre. The marker post base sits on the arch top (44 above the burrow base). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Sizes | Stone "12 x 9" is the full ellipse (radii 6 x 4.5), its long side along the arch. Stone centres lie on a half-ellipse 30.2 wide and 30.9 tall, so the outer ink edge is 72 x 44. The flag hangs to the right of the post, its top level with the post top. House icon sizes are to the outer edge of the ink, as for the 32 icon box; on the flag it is centred on the part clear of the post. | `derived (provisional)` |
| House icon, 32 box | The 7d house icon at x 1.5 about the box centre: 21 x 21 to the outer ink edge, ink 3.6. Used on the Go home buttons and by the tank arrival card title. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Shines | One pebble shine rgba(255,255,255,0.8) at the upper left of each stone. The flag has no shine (the house icon fills it). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Layer | Above the haze and below the collectibles: burrow and flag, then the ring dots over them, so the ring edge stays whole. The hero is never hazed, nor are these cue objects. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Greyscale cues | Arch silhouette, flag, house icon, dotted ring | `derived (provisional)` |

### 7e. Hidden treasures

PRD B1, B5 to B8. Colours are in 3g. All values below are `derived (provisional)`.

**Hiding.** Treasure icons under cover (reeds, pads, the cave, the pond's hollow log or the dusk pond's root arch, past the current) draw at depth 9. That is behind the reeds (10), the pads (70) and the cave front (12), and under the haze (20). They are deliberately hidden and are not cue objects while hidden. The opaque hint star at depth 75 is the cue object for a hidden treasure. Dash pots, and treasures revealed by a crumble, draw at depth 28, above the haze, as cue objects. This resolves the old conflict between "draw before reeds" here and "cue objects never hazed" in section 10: a hidden icon is not a cue object, and a cue object is never hazed.

**Depths** (higher draws in front; the full order is in `src/art/layers.ts`)

| `hiddenTreasure` | 9 | Treasure icons under cover | `derived (provisional)` |
| `reeds` | 10 | Reeds | `derived (provisional)` |
| `caveFront`, `currentMarker` | 12 | Cave rocks, the hollow log's front or the root arch's roots and mat (7b), past-the-current marker (hazed scenery) | `derived (provisional)` |
| `haze` | 20 | Water haze | `derived (provisional)` |
| `heronShadow` | 22 | Heron shadow, both tones (7h): above the haze, below every cue object | `derived (provisional)` |
| `homeSpot`, `homeRing` | 24, 26 | Home Spot burrow and flag, safe-ring dots; Area exit gate (24) and sign (26), 7g | `derived (provisional)` |
| `treasureGlow` | 27 | Rarity glow behind a revealed treasure and its pickup pop. Never used for a hidden treasure. | `derived (provisional)` |
| `treasure` | 28 | Dash pots, treasures revealed by a crumble (cue objects) | `derived (provisional)` |
| `collectibles` | 30 | Collectibles, plastic worms | `derived (provisional)` |

**Hint star.** A 4-point star (4e), white with ink, drawn at depth 75, above the reeds and pads, 20 px above the hiding spot. Rarer is smaller. Ink and fill are always opaque (section 10). It is steady while in range and never blinks. It differs from the yellow eight-arm Tail Dash sparkle by shape and colour. The range is gameplay tuning (PRD B7). It is the cue object for a hidden treasure. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

| Rarity | R | Ink | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Common | 7 | 1.8 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Rare | 6 | 1.8 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Epic | 5 | 1.6 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Legendary | 4 | 1.6 | `derived (provisional)` |

The hint star is the 4e shape: inner radius 0.38R, sharp inner corners. Each tip is rounded by a quadratic corner cut of 0.15R along both edges, so the apex sits near 0.93R. Anchor is the star centre. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Last-snack hint star.** PRD H3, open question 16. When 1 to 3 snacks are left, each uncollected snack carries the Common hint star (R 7, ink 1.8, the same baked art), 16 px above the snack, at depth 75. It is always on (not range-based, so no hysteresis) and follows its snack as it drifts and bobs. It hides at once when its snack is eaten. Ink and fill stay opaque and alpha stays 1.0 (section 10). Motion in section 9. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Snack drift.** PRD H4. In Area 1 each snack wanders around its spawn point: x +/-16 px over 10 s (sine), y +/-8 px over 8 s (cosine), with a golden-angle phase per snack (about 2.4 rad times its index) so neighbours stay out of step. Peak speed is about 11.9 px/s. Drift is gameplay and runs under reduced motion (section 9). Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Dash pot.** Local units, anchor the pot centre. Drawn at depth 28, above the haze, as a cue object.

| Dash icon | The Dash button icon from `src/art/drawUi.ts`: two open, right-pointing chevrons, each 7 wide and 14 tall in a 32 box, round joins. Drawn here in ink 2.4, about 10 px tall with its ink, centred at (0,3) on the belly. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Shine | One ellipse rgba(255,255,255,0.75), radii 3.6 x 1.8, at (-8,-3.4), tilted -0.5 rad | `derived (provisional)` |

**Catalogue icons.** The treasure icon of each Decoration and Wearable, in the colours of 3g. Each is authored in a 28-unit box centred on (0,0) and scaled uniformly, so the longest side is the icon size with the ink included. The ink never scales (4b). Baked sizes are 28 px (revealed treasure), 32 px (icon rows of the banking card and the tank arrival card, Treasure Chest rows, and the find toast), and 48 px (decorate tray squares). Texture frame is the size plus 1 px each side. Anchor is the icon centre. Round joins and caps. Each painter is a still frame, so it is also the reduced-motion version. There are 48 icon slugs (`TREASURE_ICON_SLUGS`): the twelve canal items, the eleven pond items, the eleven Area 3 items (issue #98) and the fourteen Paint shells. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Tank decoration bake.** Each Decoration that can stand in the Home Tank is baked at 80 px tall in tank px, with constant ink (4a): the canal's Pond plant, Toy boat, Castle tower and Sun pyramid; the pond's Lily pad, Cattail clump, Frog statue and Lotus lantern; and Area 3's Reed bundle, Drifting feather, Wooden duck decoy, Firefly string lights, Firefly jar and Golden lotus throne (issue #98): fourteen in all. An 80-wide Decoration is baked from its catalogue icon painter at 80 px on the longest side, in the same frame (size plus 1 px each side). A wider one has its own tank painter on its width x 80 design box (`paintWideDecoration`), baked at that box plus 1 px each side: today the 144-wide Firefly string lights (7f), a wider swag of the icon's parts. Its tray and Chest icons stay the 28-box icon. The anchor is moved from the box centre to the Decoration's anchor (7f), by its placement kind. A Floor item (Pond plant, Toy boat, Castle tower, Sun pyramid, Cattail clump, Frog statue, Reed bundle, Golden lotus throne) is anchored at its base: the lowest ink edge at the horizontal middle (the lowest fill edge of the design, Pond plant 12.3, Toy boat 11.2, Castle tower 12.4, Sun pyramid 11.8 units below the centre, and 12.6 for the Cattail clump, the Frog statue and every Area 3 Decoration, scaled to 80 px, plus the outer ink), so the sprite stands on its base point. A Floating item (Lily pad, Lotus lantern, Drifting feather, Wooden duck decoy) is anchored at the bottom-centre of its 80 box, whose edge is its lowest fill edge (the Lily pad's root, the Lotus lantern's bead, the Drifting feather's quill stub, the decoy's anchor weight), with its waterline 20 units below the box top, so it floats on the water surface. A Hanging item (Firefly string lights, Firefly jar) is anchored at the top-centre of its box, its hook, so it hangs from the glass top; its lowest fill edge is on the box bottom (the jar's flat base; the string lights' middle bulbs). The dev `?decor` test Decorations (PRD open question 145) are baked the same way from their own painters on their width x 80 boxes; they are no longer the only Floating, Hanging or wide Decorations. Tray icons are baked at 48 and 32 px in the catalogue icon frame, anchored at the icon centre. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Dream swatch tile bake.** One tile per Dream colour, in the 3f order, baked at 32 px to the outer ink edge (the tile of section 8). Texture frame is 32 plus 1 px each side. Anchor is the tile centre. Corner radius 8 and outline 2.6 at 32 px; the outline never scales. The pattern is the Paint shell blob pattern (above) scaled 2.1 times and clipped to the tile, except Rainbow, whose six bands fill the whole tile with 1.6 px dividers. Solid tiles get the one blob shine. The dark colours (Midnight blue, Galaxy, Tie-dye galaxy) get a 1.2 px #cec9f0 rim along the top edge and down part of both sides, just inside the ink. Still frame, so it is also the reduced-motion version. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Rarity glow bake.** PRD B2, issue #68. A soft disc in the badge fill of a rarity (3e, 3g): alpha 0.35 from the centre to half the radius, then a smoothstep fade to 0 at the edge, with no ink and no outline. Each glow is baked once, like the icons, and shown as one image per treasure, so there is no per-frame repaint. Texture frame is the size plus 1 px each side. Still frame, so it is also the reduced-motion version. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

| Bake | Size (across) | Used for | Anchor | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Per rarity, in the treasure textures | 40 px | Behind every 32 px icon or swatch box (the find toast, the banking card, the tank arrival card, the first-find card, the Treasure Chest's found rows) and behind the 28 px revealed treasure in the canal | Disc centre, on the icon centre | `derived (provisional)` |
| Per rarity, in the treasure textures | 56 px | Behind the 48 px icon in a 64 px decorate tray square | Disc centre, on the icon centre | `derived (provisional)` |
| Per Decoration, in the Home Tank textures | 1.2 x the Decoration's width across by 96 px tall: 96 px for the standard 80 px Decoration, and 173 x 96 for the 144-wide Firefly string lights (and the dev Test string lights), the disc stretched across its box | Behind a placed Decoration, in the rarity of that Decoration (Pond plant Common, Toy boat Rare, Castle tower Epic, Sun pyramid Legendary; Lily pad and Cattail clump Common, Frog statue Rare, Lotus lantern Epic; Reed bundle and Drifting feather Common, Wooden duck decoy and Firefly string lights Rare, Firefly jar Epic, Golden lotus throne Legendary) | The Decoration's anchor: its base point (Floor and Floating) or its hook (Hanging), so the glow is centred on the Decoration's box and takes its position, scale, sway and bob | `derived (provisional)` |

The glow shows only on a treasure the player owns or can pick up: never on an unfound silhouette, a hidden treasure (depth 9), the restyle Dream colour tab's swatch badge, the Collection Book, perk icons, the placement ghost, or a Wearable worn on an axolotl. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Unfound silhouette bake.** PRD C11, issue #69. Each treasure icon (every Decoration, Wearable and Paint shell slug) is also baked at 32 px as a flat silhouette in the same frame as its icon: every outer fill and tube in pebble A #d4cfe6, every inner detail skipped, the same outer ink, then the ink "?" (4b) at the icon centre. One plain Dream swatch silhouette is baked at 32 px in the swatch tile frame: the tile in pebble A with ink 2.6, no pattern or shine, and the same "?". Anchor is the icon or tile centre. Silhouettes never glow. Still frames, so also the reduced-motion version. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

Every fill on the outer silhouette is 9:1 or more with ink, so a revealed icon passes on any background (10). Trims, gems, and patterns are inside the silhouette and are free. The rarity look ladder is 6i: Common is one flat colour plus one shine; Rare adds one trim band; Epic adds one gem (radius 2.5); Legendary adds #ffc12e trim and one static 4-point star (R 3, white, ink 1.6). Details are counted, never judged by colour.

Notes:

- The icon star is the 4e star with its inner corners rounded by 0.08R and its tips by 0.15R (arc corners), unlike the hint star, whose inner corners are sharp.
- The Heart glasses frame and the Shell necklace cord use soft pink #ffd9e6 (11.19:1), not the accent #ff82bf (6.30:1), because they are on the outer silhouette and must reach 9:1.
- The Shell necklace and the Heart glasses have no separate trim band: the cord and the frame are their second colour. They count as the Rare and Epic detail in greyscale by the shells and the gem.
- The four slot Wearables added by issue #16 reuse the 9:1 outer fills of 3g (Tank top the leaf green #86e660, Stripy socks the pebble C #efdcba, Diving tank the pebble A #d4cfe6, Flippers the sun yellow #ffe14a), so they pass the pair rule (10) with no new colour in 3g. Their trims are the boat yellow #ffe14a (Diving tank strap) and the castle pink #ff82bf (Flippers cuff); the Flippers ridges use the Paint shell ridge colour #e0b095. The Stripy socks stripes and the Flippers ridges are texture, not trim bands, so those items still read as Common and Rare by their counted details (6i, 12).
- The pond icons (issue #75) are the pond's eleven treasure painters; the Lily green Paint shell uses the generic Paint shell painter (below). Every outer fill is a 9:1 colour from 3g, so they pass the pair rule (10). The cattail heads and the ladybug shell are light tan and pale red, because a true brown and a true red fail 9:1. They reuse existing colours inside the silhouette (pebble A for the ladybug head and the wing harness, sun yellow for the glasses bridge), the trims #ffe14a, #ff82bf and #ffc12e, and the gems #55e4d8 and #ff5f8d. The floating Decorations (Lily pad, Lotus lantern) are the Floating kind (7f); their waterline sits 20 units below the top of the 80 box. At 28 px the 2.8 ink eats most of the crown blossoms and the glasses fill; the shapes still read.
- The Area 3 icons (issue #98) are eleven more painters; the Firefly gold Paint shell uses the generic Paint shell painter (below). Every outer fill is a 9:1 colour from 3g (`src/art/treasurePalette.ts`), so they pass the pair rule (10); the worn looks of the five Wearables import the same fills. The decoy and the jar lid are pale carved wood and the cape a pale periwinkle, because a true wood brown and a true night blue fail 9:1. They reuse existing colours where they can: pebble A for the hook rings, pebble C for the throne base, the lotus lantern pink for the throne seat, soft pink #ffd9e6 for the sockets and the pearl chain, sun yellow for the decoy beak, and the trims #ffe14a and #ffc12e and the gems #55e4d8 and #ff5f8d. The fireflies inside the jar and on the cape are a flat yellow glow round an ink body, inside the silhouette. The Floating Decorations (Drifting feather, Wooden duck decoy) put their waterline 20 units below the top of the 80 box, and the Hanging ones (Firefly string lights, Firefly jar) hang from the top-centre hook (7f).
- The worn art of every Wearable is its icon's design re-fitted to its slot (6i, worn looks). All thirty-four catalogue items (fourteen Decorations and twenty Wearables) have their own outline: the canal's leaf plant, wide hat, boat, necklace loop, tower, pair of heart frames, stepped pyramid, bow, strapped top, boot-shaped sock, upright tank and fan flipper; the pond's notched pad, cattail clump, stone frog, lantern with petals, wreath, drape, pair of wing-shaped frames, hooded coat, round-shelled pack, four wings and frog-eyed boot (issue #75); and Area 3's tied sheaf of stalks, leaning quill, duck on a cord, bulbs on a swag, lidded jar, petal throne, cap with a plume, sock with pad marks, shirt with a blossom, pearl on a chain and scalloped cape (issue #98).

**Paint shell.** One per Dream colour, fourteen in all (twelve in the canal, the pond's Lily green and Area 3's Firefly gold), in the 3f order. Scallop shell about 25.4 wide and 22.5 tall at the fill edge (28 wide with ink at the 28 px size), fill #efdcba, five ridges #e0b095 at 1.6 px (internal), `shape` ink 2.6. A soft cup ellipse #e0b095 (radii 8.2 x 7.4, at (0,0.8)) holds a round paint blob of radius 6, centred at (0,-0.4), with ink 2.4. The blob shows the Dream swatch look (section 8). Anchor is the shell centre. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

| Dream colour | Blob pattern (clipped to the blob) | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Mint, Sky blue, Lavender, Sunset orange, Bubblegum, Midnight blue, Lily green, Firefly gold | Plain body colour (3f) with one shine (radii 1.7 x 0.8) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Hearts | One heart 6.8 x 6.2 in #ff5f8d | `derived (provisional)` |
| Tie-dye | A two-turn spiral to radius 4.3, bands #ff7fbf, #7fd4ff, #ffe14a by half turn, 1.5 wide | `derived (provisional)` |
| Iridescent | Stripe pair slanted 45 degrees: white rgba(255,255,255,0.7), 2.6 wide, then rgba(143,127,224,0.6), 1.8 wide | `derived (provisional)` |
| Rainbow | Six vertical bands #ff6b6b, #ffa64d, #ffe14a, #7fe07f, #6cb8ff, #b07cff with 1 px dividers rgba(255,255,255,0.8) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Galaxy | Nebula ellipse rgba(255,127,191,0.35), radii 4.4 x 2.6, and three white 4-point stars (R 2.1, 1.7, 1.3), no outline | `derived (provisional)` |
