The dark blobs (Midnight blue, Galaxy, Tie-dye galaxy) get a 1.2 px rim arc in #cec9f0 along the top, just inside the ink (3f).

**Revealed treasure.** Its catalogue icon (or Paint shell) at 28 px on the longest side, drawn at depth 28 above the haze as a cue object, with a gentle bob (section 9). It has no hint star once revealed. Its 40 px rarity glow sits just behind it at depth 27 (`treasureGlow`), centred on the icon and following its bob, with a slow breathe under full motion (9). The glow also follows the pickup pop, growing and fading with the icon. It shows only while the treasure is revealed (a crumble opened its Dash pot) or popping; a treasure under cover at depth 9 never glows, even while its hint star shows, so the hint star stays the only cue for a hidden treasure (PRD B7). Source: `derived (provisional)`.

### 7f. Home Tank kit

PRD C7 to C9. UI text says "Home Tank", never "fish tank". Layout at the 480 x 360 reference viewport (section 1). Colours are in 3g. All values below are `derived (provisional)`.

**Layout**

| Part | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Tank frame | The 480 x 360 tank scene is contain-fit to the canvas: scale min(canvas w / 480, canvas h / 360), centred across and bottom-aligned, so the stand sits on the canvas bottom. The wall colour #fff1d6 fills the rest of the canvas (no letterbox colour here). Tank units below scale with the frame. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Wall | Full background, #fff1d6 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Stand | Band y 340 to 360, #a56a3b, with an ink 3.5 top edge | `derived (provisional)` |
| Tank | Rounded rectangle x 16 to 464, y 24 to 340, corner radius 20, ink 3.5 (`shape`) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Lid | Rounded bar x 10 to 470, y 16 to 30, #d4cfe6, ink 3.5 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Air gap | y 30 to 44, #c4f1ff | `derived (provisional)` |
| Water | Clean gradient (3c), stops 0 / 0.45 / 1 from y 44 to 340. Surface line as the canal (rgba(236,255,252,0.95), 3.4 px, same wave) at y 44, across the glass from x 16 to 464. The surface line is drawn live, not baked. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Gravel | y 300 to 340. Bed gradient #b78552 to #8c5e3a under packed rounded pebbles of radius 4 to 7 in pebble colours A to E, ink 2.6, with pebble shine. Top edge wobble +/-3. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Glass shine | Two slanted stripes, rgba(255,255,255,0.25), 18 and 8 wide, at the upper-left. Drawn before the axolotls (the hero is never hazed). | `derived (provisional)` |

**Layer order, back to front (as built).** Back layer (wall, stand, lid back, air gap, water, gravel), placed decorations (every placement kind at the one decorations depth, behind the axolotls, back to front by base y, then catalogue order, so Floating and Hanging items, at base y 104, draw behind every Floor item; a Hanging item is drawn from its hook on the glass top, one footprint above its base point), glass shine, axolotls, surface line, front layer (tank outline and lid front), UI (the hub buttons and name panel with a focused axolotl's focus ring and name tag, or the decorate controls with their focus rings and the ghost), the Treasure Chest or the restyle screen, then the tank arrival card on top. There are no tank bubbles yet. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Decorations and axolotls**

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Art | Each 80-wide Decoration is its catalogue icon (7e) baked at 80 px on the longest side, at its anchor (the tank decoration bake, 7e). The one wider Decoration, the 144-wide Firefly string lights, has its own tank art (below); no other has separate tank art yet. Fourteen Decorations ship (issues #75 and #98): the Floor items are the canal's Pond plant, Toy boat, Castle tower and Sun pyramid, the pond's Cattail clump and Frog statue, and Area 3's Reed bundle and Golden lotus throne; the Floating items are the pond's Lily pad and Lotus lantern and Area 3's Drifting feather and Wooden duck decoy; the Hanging items are Area 3's Firefly string lights and Firefly jar. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Base | The base point (bottom-centre) of a Floor item stands in the floor band: base y 304 to 332 (the gravel top is about y 300). A Floating or Hanging item has one fixed base y, 104, the bottom of the top band (tank y 24 to 104), so it moves along that line only. Base x stays 8 px inside the glass sides plus half the footprint: x 64 to 416 for an 80 wide item, x 96 to 384 for a 144 wide one. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Footprint | The item's width (80 unless its catalogue entry sets another) x 80, standing on the base point; for a Floating or Hanging item that is tank y 24 to 104. A Hanging item is drawn from its hook at y 24, on the glass top, down to the base point. The focus rings and the ghost marks go round it. | `derived (provisional)` |
| New placement | A Decoration placed from the tray goes to the first suggested spot whose footprint overlaps no placed one: base y 318 for Floor, 104 for Floating and Hanging; x tried in the order 240, 144, 336, 96, 384, 192, 288, each clamped into the item's x range. When none is free, the spots are used in turn. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Keyboard move | One arrow press moves the base point 16 px across or 8 px up or down. A Floating or Hanging item moves Left and Right along its line only; Up and Down do nothing and show no invalid cue. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Touch move | A drag starts after 8 px of pointer travel; the ghost follows the pointer, and the Decoration moves on release only at a valid base point. A Floating or Hanging item snaps y to its line (104) and clamps x into its range. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Ink | Standard ink rules (4a to 4c) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Motion | The Pond plant sways about its base point (section 9). A Floating item (the Lily pad, the Lotus lantern, the Drifting feather and the Wooden duck decoy) bobs +/-3 units over 3 s. A Hanging item (the Firefly string lights and the Firefly jar) sways +/-0.04 rad over 5 s about its hook. Both are still at once under reduced motion. Every other item is still, and no Decoration's fireflies or bulbs glow or blink: each is one still bake. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Castles | Rounded towers, round windows, rounded crenellations | `derived (provisional)` |
| Axolotls | Same construction at scene scale 0.95, times the tank frame scale, each in its own look (6l to 6u) at its own size: x0.72, x0.85 or x1.00, about 87, 103 or 121 px long at tank frame scale 1 for the prototype proportions (6o). A rescued axolotl arrives in the default look at Medium with its boy or girl cues, until it is restyled. An axolotl with a Dream colour on is drawn in that Dream colour (6p). Each axolotl is drawn in its outfit (6a, 6i), with its Socks and Feet items on all four legs; a new or rescued axolotl wears nothing until it is dressed on the Outfit tab (8). The size factor (6o) is applied everywhere the axolotl is drawn: on trips, in the Home Tank and in the creator and restyle preview. On trips the mouth bubble spawn and the tail sparkles scale with it, so they stay on the body, and they also follow the tilt and the turn. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Axolotl swim | Every owned axolotl laps the tank on its own: x 88 to 392 and back, eased at each end, facing flips at each end. Lap period 16 to 24 s, fixed per axolotl. Each axolotl has its own lane, spread through y 84 to 264 so the bob stays in the water, a hover bob of +/-6 px over 3 s and a tilt of up to 0.12 rad that follows the bob. Under reduced motion each stands still in its lane, spread along the lap, facing right and left in turn, with no tilt (section 9). | `derived (provisional)` |

**Area 3 Decorations (issue #98).** Six Decorations, each its 7e icon at the tank decoration bake (7e), except the wide Firefly string lights. A Hanging item is anchored at the top-centre of its box, its hook, and hangs from the glass top at tank y 24 down to its base point (y 104); a Floating item floats with its waterline 20 units below its box top; a Floor item stands on its lowest ink edge. All values are `derived (provisional)`.

**Firefly string lights tank sprite.** Its own painter on the 144 x 80 box, centred on (0,0) in tank units and scaled by the Decoration size / 80; the ink never scales (4a). Three hook rings, pebble A #d4cfe6, radius 3.2, ink 1.6, at x -66, 0 and 66 on y -36, so their ink touches the box top (y -40): the end hooks and the centre hook. A thin ink wire (1.6) hangs in two swags, each from an end ring to the centre ring, its cubic controls a third of the way in. Each swag carries three round glowing bulbs #ffe98a (`shape` 2.8) at 0.2, 0.5 and 0.8 along it, radius 7, 8.4 (the middle one, the biggest) and 7, each under a pink #ffd9e6 socket 6.4 wide and 6 tall (corner radius 1.2, ink 1.6), the one trim. The two middle bulbs are the lowest fill edge, their ink on the box bottom (y 40). One shine, on the left middle bulb. Anchor is the top-centre hook. Its tray and Chest icons are the 28-box icon (7e). Its glow is 173 x 96 (7e). Source: `derived (provisional)`.

**Placement**

| State | Look | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Selected or focused item | The double focus ring of section 8 round the footprint: 3 px lines, inner white 3.25 px and outer ink 6.25 px outside the footprint, corner radius 18 plus the offset. Both opaque. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Valid move | A ghost: the whole item (fills and ink) at 0.5 alpha, inside the opaque double focus ring. The ring is opaque, so it carries on any background (10). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Invalid move (a Floor item outside the floor band or the x range; a Floating or Hanging item outside its x range) | The ghost with a dashed ink outline round the footprint (ink 3.5, dash 8, gap 6), still inside the double focus ring, plus the "no" icon centred on the footprint's top-right corner: a white disc with an ink ring and a diagonal ink bar, 24 px, ink 3, all opaque. A keyboard move to an invalid base point shows it for 0.7 s at that point and does not move the item. A Floor drag shows it while the pointer is at an invalid point. The cue shapes are the same for every kind and follow the item's footprint width. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Deviation (as built).** The guide asked for the ghost's ink outline to stay opaque with only its fills at 0.5 alpha. The build fades the whole item, ink included, to 0.5. The opaque double focus ring is what carries the ghost on any background, so the pair rule (10) still holds for the cue; the ghost's own ink does not. Listed in section 12. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

Never colour alone.

### 7g. Area exit and Area stars

PRD H1 to H8. Colours are in 3g. All values below are `derived (provisional)`. Every painter is a still frame, so it is also the reduced-motion version.

**Area exit.** The exit is a stone gate standing on the bed with a round sign above it. Closed and open differ by shape and icon, never by colour.

| Part | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| World position | Exit circle centre (1850,200), radius 40 (gameplay, inclusive edge; PRD H1). The gate base-centre is on the bed under it at (1850,318), the bed top. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Gate frame | One U-shaped band of two posts and a round crown, fill pebble A, `shape` ink 2.8, no line across the opening. Clear opening 96 wide x 158 tall to the inner ink edge (y 160 to 318 at the exit). Outer width to the ink edge about 114.8; height to the keystone top about 186.3. Outer crown vertical radius 70. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Keystone | Rounded block 20 x 22, corner radius 5, pebble C, ink 2.6, at the crown top | `derived (provisional)` |
| Crown seams, shines | Four radial soft seam lines across the crown (1.6, internal, not ink). One pebble shine on the crown and one on the left post. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Closed | Six rounded vertical slats across the opening, evenly spaced, tube 5 wide with `ol` 2.2, pebble C, clipped to the opening. Each bottom end sits 5 above the base, so its round cap shows. The frame outline is drawn over the slat ends. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Open | The opening is clear. Each leaf is folded into a pleated zigzag tube over its post (seven pleats, each 14 tall, reaching x 53.5 to 58 from the centre), tube 5.5 wide with `ol` 2.2, pebble C. The open frame is about 126 wide with the leaves. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Texture frame | Both states share one frame (the open width), anchored at the base centre. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Sign | A round white disc, 32 px to the ink edge, ink 3.5 (`shape`). Centre (1850, about 107.7): 8 px of clear water above the keystone top. Closed: an ink padlock (shackle 2.6, filled body 11 x 9 with a white keyhole dot). Open: an ink right arrow (3.2). Anchor is the disc centre. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Layers | Gate at `homeSpot` (24), sign at `homeRing` (26). Above the haze and below the collectibles: both are cue objects and never hazed (10). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Opening burst | When the exit opens (the last snack is eaten), the gate and sign swap to open art at once, and three twinkles (4e, the baked twinkle at 0.46 scale, about 12 px) sit evenly round the sign, the first straight up. They start at the sign's rim, move 6 px out, and fade over 0.6 s, once. At depth 80 (`fx`). An exit already open when the area loads shows open with no burst. Motion in section 9. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Greyscale cues | Slats across the opening against a clear opening with folded leaves; padlock against right arrow. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Area star.** Each area has three stars (PRD H5, H6): reach the exit, own the special treasure, and rescue the stranded axolotl or keep every leg. A star is a five-point rounded star inside a white bubble ring. Painted in a 24 box and scaled uniformly by size / 24; the ink widths never scale (4b).

| Part | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Sizes | 24 px on the Home Tank stars badge, 32 px as the "Area beaten" card head icon, 40 px in the card's star row (gap 12, row 144 wide). Each size is baked on its own, earned and missing. Anchor is the bubble centre. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Bubble | White band, radius 10.7, `shape` ink 2.6. Inner disc radius 8.9 in #c4f1ff with a soft ring line #a9bfd6 at 1.2 (internal, not ink). One shine ellipse rgba(255,255,255,0.8) at the upper left, radii 1.9 x 0.9, tilted -0.8 rad. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Star | Five points, one up: outer radius 8.6, inner radius 4.1, tip rounding 1.3, notch rounding 0.6, centre 0.8 below the bubble centre so the star sits visually in the middle. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Earned | Fill #ffe14a, ink 2.0 (`shape`) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Missing | No fill; a dashed ink outline, 2.0, dash 3, gap 3, butt caps (as the legs display). The bubble stays. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Not the Legendary badge | The Legendary rarity badge (3e, 8) is a lone #ffc12e rounded five-point star with no bubble ring, always beside the word "Legendary". The Area star is always in the white bubble ring, is #ffe14a when earned, and has a missing state. Different hue is the weakest cue; the bubble ring and the dashed missing state are the cues. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Greyscale cues | Filled star against dashed outline. The earned fill and the inner disc are only 1.08:1, so the dashed outline, not the fill, carries missing. | `derived (provisional)` |

### 7h. Heron (pond predator)

PRD N2 (Area 3), N3, A11; ADR 0007, ADR 0008 and [ADR 0016](adr/0016-heron-timer-warnings-and-area-look-variants.md). Issue #98. The heron is the pond's signature predator, in Area 3. It is seen only as its shadow on the water and, when it strikes, as a closed beak dipping in from above. Colours are in 3g. The painters are `src/art/drawHeron.ts` (still frames, baked in `src/art/predatorTextures.ts`); the scene glue is `src/scenes/HeronView.ts`. All values below are `derived (provisional)`.

**Character rules**

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Gentle | Round, soft and calm, never scary, as the tilapia (7c). No sharp points: every corner of the shadow is round, the beak tip is round and the crest is a soft tuft. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Never | Teeth, an open bill, angry brows, red eyes, talons or spikes. The beak is always closed, with one ink seam down it. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Never shown | Grabbing, lifting or eating the axolotl (A11). The beak dip is a closed beak at a fixed spot. A hit shows only the shared leg-loss poof (3g, 9), as a tilapia hit does. | `derived (provisional)` |
| No "!" bubble | The warning is the shadow itself: it holds at the strike spot and turns darker. There is no "!" bubble and no glint: those are the tilapia's telegraph (7c). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Hierarchy | Never warmer or more saturated than the hero, and no heron fill is lighter than the hero's belly #ffe3ed (0.823): the head 0.643, the beak 0.727. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Shadow.** A see-through shade on the water near the surface, in two tones.

| Part | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Silhouette | A flying heron facing right around the body centre: one wing raised with a round tip, the neck tucked, a round head with a blunt beak, and the legs trailing to round feet. One closed outline: a quadratic B-spline through the midpoints of a 29-point control ring, so it lies inside the ring's hull and every corner comes out round. The ring spans x -84 to 92 and y -60 to 14. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Fill and ink | The tone's see-through ink shade (3g), then one centred opaque ink line, 3.5 (`shape`), on the same path: no inner seams, and the same ink in both tones. The fill may use alpha (10, Alpha); the ink is opaque. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Frame | 192 x 90 px: the ring's hull plus half the ink, with 6 px of padding. Anchor is the body centre. Authored facing right; the scene mirrors it to face left, never rotates it. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Tones | Two still bakes of the same shape and ink: the glide tone rgba(43,35,80,0.32) and the warning tone rgba(43,35,80,0.56), darker. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Darkening | While the heron warns or strikes, the shadow holds at the strike spot and crossfades from the glide tone to the warning tone over 0.4 s, and back over 0.4 s after. Under reduced motion it switches at once. As built, the two tones draw on the same path during the crossfade, the glide at alpha 1 - d and the warning at alpha d, so their ink covers at 1 - d(1 - d): 0.75 at the midpoint, for less than 0.4 s. The Alpha rule (10) allows a lowered outline only on a fade in or out; this is recorded here as built. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Outline for greyscale | In greyscale the shadow is a dark shape with an ink outline. Over the dusk water stops with their haze the glide tone is 1.52:1 to 1.81:1 darker than the water, and the warning tone is 1.40:1 to 1.70:1 darker again, so the warning reads as a darker shape that has stopped. The opaque ink outline carries the edge on every pond and dusk background (the pond floor, 3c); inside, it is 1.73:1 to 2.80:1 against the warning fill, so it still shows. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Position | It glides with the heron's position along its stretch near the surface (Area 3: x 2200 to 2620 at y 120; the heron reaches 60 px from it, so its strike band runs from the surface to y 180). At y 120 the raised wing tip reaches y 60, above the water (y 83), so the top of the wing draws over the sky band, and over the bank soil on the last column, where ink is 2.88:1 under the dusk wash (3c). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Show and fade | Shown 4 s of each 8 s cycle (the glide-in of 2 s to the warn mark, then the warning, strike and cool-down, or the glide-off), gone in the 4 s gap (`HERON` in `src/logic/tuning.ts`, provisional). Under full motion it fades in over the first 0.4 s and out over the last 0.4 s, so it never pops in or out at the ends of its route. Under reduced motion it stays where it became visible, fading in over the last 0.4 s before the warn mark, moves only onto the strike spot when the warning starts, and fades out over 0.4 s where it was. Motion in 9. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Beak dip.** The strike: a closed beak dips down at the strike spot, the head above it and the neck running up off the top of the frame.

| Part | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Beak | Straight sides from 7 either side of the centre, 36 above the tip, to a round tip (radius 3) at the anchor. #ffd98a, `blob` ink 3.5 all outside the fill. One ink seam, 2.0, down its middle below the head: the beak is closed, never an open bill. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Head, crest and neck | One merged `blob` in #d4cfe6 (ink 3.5 all outside): a head ellipse, radii 14 x 15, centred 44 above the tip; a round crest tuft at the back of the head (radii 8 x 4, tilted -0.8 rad), never a spike; and the neck, 16 wide, running up off the top of the frame with no cap. One head shine rgba(255,255,255,0.6), radii 5 x 2.2. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Eye | A bead #1b1535, radius 4.2 (smaller than the tilapia's 5.6: a smaller head), 5 off the centre line toward the way it faces and 2 above the head centre, with the tilapia eye's two white glints | `derived (provisional)` |
| Frame | 54 x 128 px: from 120 above the tip (`HERON_BEAK_NECK`) to the tip's ink plus 4 px of padding. Anchor is the beak tip, the spot the dip aims at. Only the neck runs off the top edge. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Dip | It starts on the heron's strike and shows for the strike's 0.4 s (tuning) at the fixed strike spot, facing the way the shadow faced. Under full motion the tip starts 24 px above the spot, dips down to it at the middle of the 0.4 s and rises back 24 px (a half sine), once. Under reduced motion it holds still at the spot for 0.4 s. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Clamp | The tip never goes below y 200 (SURF + 120), so the cut end of the neck stays at or above the surface. Deeper water is out of its reach: "stay deep". | `derived (provisional)` |
| Layer order | Back to front: beak, then head, crest and neck, then the shine, the beak seam and the eye. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Depths** (`src/art/layers.ts`)

| Layer | Depth | Rule | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `heronShadow` | 22 | Above the haze (20), so the shadow is never hazed; below `homeSpot` (24), so every cue object (the Home Spot, Dash pots, treasures, snacks, plastic worms, the tilapia and the axolotl) draws over it. The surface (60) and the pads (70) draw over it too. | `derived (provisional)` |
| `heronBeak` | 36 | In front of the tilapia (35); behind the protected halo (38), the stranded axolotl (39) and the axolotl (40), so the beak never covers the hero. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Greyscale cues.** The glide shadow: a soft dark shape with an ink outline, moving. The warning: the same shape, darker, holding still. The strike: a closed beak with a pale head, and only the leg-loss poof on a hit. None relies on hue (10).

### 7i. Xochimilco refuge

PRD N2 (Refuge); issue #74; [ADR 0017](adr/0017-xochimilco-refuge-and-habitat-milestones.md). The refuge screen (8) shows one section picture per habitat, dirty or clean, with trash laid over a dirty one, then a "More to come" teaser. Colours are in 3g and widths in 4b. The painters are `src/art/drawRefuge.ts` (still frames, baked in `src/art/refugeTextures.ts`); the scene glue is `src/scenes/RefugeScene.ts`. Every painter is a still frame (no time, no randomness, no game state), so it is also the reduced-motion version. The fade of the trash, the growth of the plants and the sparkle burst are the scene's (9). Text is never painted: the habitat name, "???", the hint and "More to come" are scene text. All values below are `derived (provisional)`.

**Character rules**

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Gentle | The trash is tidy and soft: round corners, no faces, nothing messy, sharp or gross. Dirty water is a calm grey-brown, never black or a toxic green. | `derived (provisional)` |
| True | A clean-up shows real conservation work in Xochimilco (PRD N2): the trash is lifted out, the plants come back, and small axolotls rest on the bed. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Keyed by look | The pictures and plants are keyed by habitat look (canal, pond), so a new look cannot ship without refuge art. Canal: stone banks. Pond: lily pads and cattails. | `derived (provisional)` |
| UI, not a cue object | The picture is UI, not a cue object (10). Its ink frame must read on every colour that meets it, so each is 3:1 or more with ink: the sky stops, the water stops (dirty or clean), the bed and the canal's stones. The lowest is the dirty canal's mid stop, 3.48:1. The small ink objects inside carry their own edges with fills of 9:1 or more (trash) or 8.86:1 (the resting axolotls). | `derived (provisional)` |

**Section picture**

| Part | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Frame | A rounded window, 200 x 96 to the outer ink edge, corner radius 14 on the centreline, ink 3.5 drawn over the clipped edge. Anchor is the top-left ink corner; texture frame padding 2. The hidden silhouette and the teaser use the same window. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Sky | The look's sky gradient, top to horizon, from y 0 to the surface at y 22. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Water | A wavy surface at y 22 (ten humps, +/-2.4) with the look's surface line along it, and the water down to the bed at y 78. Clean: the look's shallow stop to its mid stop, with two soft light shafts (3g) slanting from the surface to the bed. Dirty: the same stops under the tint (3g), with four murk blotches. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Bed | A flat band from y 78 to 96 in the look's bed top colour, the bed lip line (2.2, the look's lip colour) along y 78, and six mud dots (r 1.4 to 1.8) in the look's mud colour. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Canal | Stone banks in both states: eight pebble ellipses a side (pebbles A, B and C, ink 2.6), the left bank from x 0 to 38 and the right bank its mirror. Dirty: bare stones, tinted water and murk. Clean: four grass tubes and one light reed (15 tall) on the cap stones of each bank, with one grass shine; clear water, the two shafts, and two resting axolotls on the bed at (86,70) facing right and (138,72) facing left. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Pond | Dirty: two bare lily pads on the waterline (rx 13 and 11, at x 76 and 140, no flowers) and two short dull stalks at the edges (30 tall, #a8986a, no leaf and no head), tinted water and murk. Clean: four lily pads (rx 15, 11, 15 and 11, at x 52, 92, 128 and 156; the first and last with flowers), four cattails (64 tall at the edges, 44 inside them), clear water, the two shafts, and two resting axolotls at (70,71) facing right and (124,69) facing left. The pads and cattails are the 7a lily pad and reed painters. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Resting axolotl | About 30 x 17 to the ink edge, centred on its body, facing right or mirrored left. One merged `blob` silhouette (a body ellipse 9 x 4.6, a head 5.4 x 4.6, a tail 8 x 2.6 and two leg stubs) in the Leucistic body #ffb7cf, ink 2.2 all outside the fill; three gill tubes behind the head; a bead eye #1b1535, r 0.9. Clean pictures only. Still: no bob, tail wave or blink. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Plants layer | Drawn over a clean picture, its base at picture (100,82), bottom-centre on the bed; it grows upward from there (9). Frame x -40 to 40 and y -60 to 0, padding 4, anchored at the base. Canal: three reeds (36, 44 and 30 tall) and a grass tuft. Pond: two cattails (46 and 38 tall) and a grass tuft. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Trash.** One batch for each area of the habitat that is not beaten yet, laid over a dirty picture by the scene.

| Part | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Batch | About 44 x 34 (frame x -22 to 22, y -17 to 17, padding 2), anchored at the batch centre. Three items, ink 2.4 (`shape`). The variant goes by slot in turn: cans, bottles, bags, then round again. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Cans | Two upright cans and one lying down. Each body is an 11 x 15 box with straight sides, #c9d3e3, with a band line #8fa0bf across it (internal, not ink) and a white rim ellipse (ink 2.0) at its top. Built from straight lines and an ellipse only. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Bottles | Three long bottles lying at slight angles: a body with rounded ends and quadratic shoulders into a neck, #bfe9f0, a yellow cap #ffe14a (ink 2.0) and one white highlight line. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Bags | Three squat sacks drawn all in bezier curves, #f3efe4, each with two handle loops (tubes) behind it and a crumple fold line #c9c2b0 (internal). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Slots | One batch sits mid-water at x 100. More spread evenly from x 56 to x 144, clear of the canal banks, at heights in turn of y 54, 42, 58 and 46, so every whole batch stays in the water (y 22 to 78). Up to three sit side by side clear of each other; more overlap a little. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Hidden section, teaser, sparkle and nudge badge**

| Part | Construction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Hidden section ("???") | The picture's window filled flat with pebble A #d4cfe6, a soft wave line in pebble B at the surface (internal, not ink), the ink frame 3.5, and a rounded ink "?" in the middle: the unfound treasure glyph scaled by 96 / 64, a 3.0 tube with a round dot. It shows no habitat, so it gives nothing away. Scene text: the name "???" over it and the hint "Beat Area N" under it. | `derived (provisional)` |
| "More to come" teaser | A white window with a dashed ink outline (3.5, dash 8, gap 6, butt caps) and a small sprout in the middle: a stem tube and two leaves in #86e660 (ink 2.6), over a soft ripple in pebble B. The scene shows it at alpha 0.5 (not a cue object, 10), with the label "More to come" over it. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Clean-up sparkle | The 4-point star (4e): white, R 10, ink 1.8. Frame x -11 to 11, padding 2, anchored at the centre. One over each batch as it is cleaned up (9). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Nudge badge | A #ff82bf disc, 22 px to the outer edge of its ink ring (2.6), holding a solid ink 4-point star (the 4e shape, R 6.4, no white fill). Ink on the pink is 6.30:1. Anchored at the disc centre, which sits at the hub Refuge tile's top-right corner pulled in 8 px on each axis, on the full tile or on the 48 px icon-only tile, where it is 22.6 px from the tile centre, clear of the 32 px icon, and overlaps the corner by design (8). | `derived (provisional)` |

**Greyscale cues.** Dirty against clean: trash and murk blotches with bare banks or bare pads, against plants, shafts and resting axolotls. The three trash kinds by outline: a box with a rim ellipse (cans), a long body with shoulders and a neck (bottles), a squat sack with handle loops (bags). Hidden by the "?" glyph on a flat window; the teaser by its dashed outline against the solid frame of every picture and the silhouette; the nudge badge by a disc with an ink ring and an ink star. None relies on hue (10).

## 8. UI direction

All values in this section are `derived (provisional)` unless the row says otherwise.

| Topic | Direction | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Text colour | Ink #2b2350 on every light fill. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Primary button | Fill #ffe14a (11.05:1 with ink). At least 64 px tall. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Secondary button | Fill #55e4d8 (9.24:1). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Accent | Fill #ff82bf (6.3:1). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Success | Fill #86e660 (9.25:1). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Panels | #ffffff (14.39:1) or #ffd9e6 (11.19:1). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Button shape | 3.5 px ink outline, corner radius at least 16, a small top shine. Pressed state moves the button down 3 px. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Focus ring | Double ring: inner #ffffff, outer #2b2350. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Touch targets | At least 48 x 48 CSS px; primary actions at least 64 px tall; at least 12 px between targets. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Text size | Body at least 18 px. Button labels at least 22 to 24 px, bold. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Typeface | A rounded sans-serif. Final font choice is deferred; names are candidates only. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Icons | Always paired with a text label, except the compact Home Tank tiles (Home Tank hub, Decorate controls): icon-only only where the full-size buttons do not fit, and always named by a caption while focused. A disabled button uses an icon (for example a lock) as well as a dimmed colour, never colour alone. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Text on letterbox | White text on #0c6a8c is 6.08:1 (`cartoon.html` value). | `cartoon.html` |
| Morph display | Morph name plus a badge with a distinct shape per morph (eight morphs, eight shapes). Never colour alone. Morph badges must not reuse the rarity shapes (circle, diamond, shield, star) or the Dream swatch tile, and no two of the eight morph shapes repeat. Shapes and fills are in the morph badge table below. Badge box 24 x 24 px in the Collection Book and the find toast. Outline 2.6 (`shape`, constant). One white shine rgba(255,255,255,0.8) at the upper-left, along the edge it sits on. The fill is the body colour of the morph that wears it; the shape carries the meaning. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Rarity display | Badge plus the rarity name in ink bold (at least 18 px) beside it, everywhere. Badge box 24 x 24 px in lists and the find toast, 32 x 32 px in detail views. Outline 2.6 (`shape`, constant). One white shine rgba(255,255,255,0.8) at the upper-left. Shapes are in the table below. Fills are in 3e; Common is green #1fb98d, and its ink outline carries its edge on a white panel. Rarity glow: every found or owned treasure icon and Dream swatch tile (the find toast, the banking card, the tank arrival card, the first-find card, the Treasure Chest's found rows, the decorate tray and placed Decorations) has a soft glow behind it in its rarity's fill, and a revealed canal treasure has one too (7e). The glow adds to the badge and the rarity name and never replaces them. Unfound silhouettes, the restyle Dream colour tab's swatch badge, the Collection Book, perk icons and worn Wearables have no glow. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Dream colour display | A swatch tile: a rounded square 32 x 32, corner radius 8, outline 2.6. It shows the body colour plus a simplified pattern. Hearts: one 14 px heart. Tie-dye: a two-turn spiral. Iridescent: the stripe pair. Rainbow: six vertical bands with dividers. Galaxy: three stars. Tie-dye galaxy: a spiral and two stars. Solid tiles are plain with one shine. The name label always sits beside the tile. In the Treasure Chest and the Collection Book the label "Dream colour - not found in nature" goes with it (PRD C11, C12, F15). The PRD text writes that label with an em dash; this guide is ASCII only, so the build text must follow the PRD wording. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Find toast (B8) | A short toast at a screen edge, placed per HUD overlay fit (the top edge when it fits). One compact white panel (panel style), padding 8 across and 4 down. The treasure icon 32 px over its 40 px rarity glow (7e) on the left, then 8 px, then two lines, all centred on the panel height. Line 1: the name in bold ink 20 px. Line 2: the rarity badge 24 px, 6 px, then "<Rarity> <Category>" in bold ink 18 px (for example "Rare Wearable"). The name wraps between 120 and 360 px; line 2 wraps at 90 px or wider. Text never shrinks. Non-blocking: it never covers the controls or open water in the middle of the play area and takes no input. At most one: a newer find (or rescue) replaces it at once at full opacity and restarts the 2 s hold. No queue; earlier finds of the same moment still go to the Trip Bag. A find and a rescue at the same moment show the rescue. Never shown over the banking card; it is cleared when the banking card opens or the trip ends. Motion in section 9. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Find toast, rescue variant (C3) | Shown when a stranded axolotl starts following; same panel, layout, motion and replace rule as the find toast. The axolotl portrait (rest pose, in its morph colours) 32 px wide in place of the treasure icon. Line 1: the name "<Morph> axolotl" (for example "Wild axolotl") in bold ink 20 px. Line 2: the morph badge 24 px, then "Following you!" in bold ink 18 px. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Wearable display | The slot name as text with every Wearable. The perk is shown as one line: the 24 px perk icon (7b), 6 px, then the perk's name and value as text in ink 18 px, never smaller (PRD F10). The form is a name and a signed value or a count: "Speed +4%", "Snack magnet +30", "Shell blocks 1 hit"; the catalogue code builds the text and its units. The icon never stands alone and the text never hides. Wherever a Wearable is listed (the Treasure Chest detail line, the Outfit tab choices, the outfit summary below) the icon comes before the text. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Outfit summary (PRD F10, F11) | A block of perk rows for one axolotl's outfit: one row for each perk type it wears, in PRD order (Speed, Regrowth, Dash, Shell, Treasure sense, Snack magnet). Each row is the 24 px perk icon, 6 px, then the combined value as text in ink 18 px ("Speed +10%", "Shell blocks 1 hit"), and, when that perk type has reached its hard cap (F11), the **max mark** 8 px after the text: a pill, white fill, ink 2.6 outline (`shape`), the word "max" in bold ink 18 px, padding 6 across, 24 px tall. With no perk worn the block is the one line "No perks yet" in ink 18 px, with no icon. Rows are 6 px apart; when stacked rows do not fit, the rows flow side by side in whole rows, 12 px apart across. Icon, text and the max mark carry the meaning, never colour. It shows on the Outfit tab and on the explorer card. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Outfit icons | In the 32 icon box, ink only, round joins and caps, always with a label, like the Home Tank icons. **Shirt** (Change outfit): a sleeveless top, ink 3.0: straps (11.5,4.5) to (11.5,10) and (20.5,4.5) to (20.5,10); a neck scoop, an arc from (11.5,4.5) through (16,9.5) to (20.5,4.5); the body from (11.5,10) curving out to (6.5,12.5), down to (7.5,27.5), across with rounded corners to (24.5,27.5), up to (25.5,12.5) and back in to (20.5,10). **Plus** (empty slot): two strokes, (16,7) to (16,25) and (7,16) to (25,16), ink 4.5, shown at 16 px in its badge. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Restyle Outfit tab (PRD C10, F8 to F11, F16) | A restyle-only step screen, tab "Outfit" right after the Dream colour tab (tab order: Colour, Dream colour, Outfit, Boy or girl, Eyes, and on; the creator has no Outfit step and stays at 11). Heading "Pick your outfit!". The axolotl choices card's Outfit (below) opens restyle straight on this tab, on the slots view with Head focused. The same step screen as the Dream colour tab (panel, tab row, helper line, live preview, picture buttons, More button, step buttons), with these changes. **What shows.** The Home Tank (7f), trips and the explorer card draw an axolotl in its outfit (the layers of 6a). On the restyle screen, every step, this tab included, draws the axolotl in the draft outfit (what it wears now, with the picks made on this tab so far) in its live preview and on its picture buttons, so the outfit stays in view while other options change; on the Spots step a worn Shirt covers the spots under it (6a layer 9.3). The character creator draws no outfit: a new axolotl wears nothing. The preview here is the same size (166 x 142 at x1.00) and idles as on the other tabs. **Slots view.** One picture button for each of the eight slots, in PRD order: Head, Neck, Face, Shirt, Back, Tail, Socks, Feet. Each is a square, panel style, the largest whole side from 48 to 96 px that fits, in the fewest balanced rows, 12 px apart, showing that slot's crop (11) of the axolotl, in its own look and Dream colour at the still pose (9), at x1.00, wearing its whole outfit so the layering shows. The slot name is the label under it (bold ink 18 px, 4 px under). A worn slot shows the item on the axolotl and its perk icon on a white disc (r 12, ink ring 2.6, the 18 px icon) in the lower-left badge spot (24 x 24, 4 px inside the corner). An empty slot shows the **empty-slot cue**: the slot's footprint (6i) as a dashed ink outline (2.0, dash 3, gap 3, opaque; on Socks and Feet one round each of the four feet's Feet envelope), and a plus badge: a white disc r 10 with an ink ring 2.6 and the 16 px plus icon, centred 13 px in from the top-right corner (where the tick sits on the other screens). A press, or Enter or Space, opens that slot's choices view. **Choices view.** The picture buttons are "None" first, then every Wearable of that slot the player has banked, in catalogue order. Each shows the slot's crop with that choice worn on the axolotl (None: the empty-slot cue, with the dashed outline and no plus badge). The label block under each picture is up to 168 px wide; beside it (once the last fit step below is taken) it is up to 168 px, or wider when that takes fewer lines (fit step 8). It wraps at word breaks and never shrinks or hides: line 1 the name (bold ink 18 px; "None" for the first); line 2 the perk: the 24 px perk icon, 6 px, the perk text (Wearable display); and, when a different axolotl wears that Wearable, line 3 the **"On <name>" mark** with that axolotl's name (bold ink 18 px, for example "On Milo"); choosing it takes it off that axolotl and puts it on this one. What this axolotl wears now (None when the slot is empty) has the chosen mark: the 5 px ink ring just inside the outline and the tick badge, as on every step screen. A pick shows in the preview and the pictures at once, and the view stays open. **Buttons.** In the choices view "Back to slots" (secondary tile, left chevron over the label, label-sized: about 184 x 64, never shorter) is first among the step buttons; once the tab row hides (see Paging and fit) it is instead the first part of the body, centred above the pictures. Surprise me is hidden in both views, with its place kept so Cancel and Done never move, and never changes the outfit; Done keeps the outfit; Cancel and, in the slots view, Escape leave without keeping any change. **Summary and caption.** Under the slot buttons in the slots view, the outfit summary (above) when it fits. When it does not fit, a one-line caption in its place, bold ink 18 px, for the focused or pressed slot: "<Slot>: <Wearable name>, <perk text>" or "<Slot>: empty", so every perk icon still has its text on screen. **Helper**: slots view "Tap a slot to choose what to wear."; choices view "<Slot>: tap a picture to try it on."; a slot with no banked Wearable shows only None and the helper "No <slot> items yet. Find some on a trip!". **Paging and fit**: at most 8 picture buttons to a page (the slots view has exactly 8). Labels, perk icons and perk text never hide (fit step 2 does not apply, as on the Dream colour tab). The fit order is: the outfit summary or caption hides first (slots view); then fit steps 1 and 3 to 6, then Cancel in the tab row; then fewer picture buttons per page, one fewer at a time, down to 1. **The choices view then has two more steps**, each taken only when no count from the most down to 1 fits before it, and each starting the fit steps again from the first (so a step that gave back the preview or the heading shows them again if they fit): **7. The tab row hides.** Back to slots joins the body as its first part, centred above the pictures (12 px from a picture, 4 px from a label); Cancel and Done stay the step buttons, with Surprise me still hidden and its place kept, and Cancel in its usual place, not in the tab row. Fit step 6 may still hide the heading, so the step is then named by Back to slots alone. **8. Each label sits beside its picture**, not under it: the picture on the left, 12 px, then the label block (up to 168 px wide; when 168 does not fit, it may wrap wider, up to the band less 60 px, the least picture side 48 plus the 12 px gap, which is 204 px at 320 x 440, and only when that takes fewer lines), the picture and the block centred down a row as tall as the taller; rows 4 px apart (more if two pictures would be under 12 px apart); the More wearables button under the last row, 12 px from a picture and 4 px from a label. A picture and its label block side by side take about 90 px of height, not about 142 under each other, which is what lets Back to slots and the step buttons fit. Measured on the layout grid with the worst label block (a longest-named axolotl): 112.5 px tall at 168 px wide, 91.5 px at 204. It fits every supported canvas. With the whole catalogue on the tab (the canal's Wearables, the pond's and Area 3's), a slot can have two or more choices, up to 5 (Back: None and four Wearables): at 460 x 320 and at 320 x 440 the choices view shows 1 choice a page with More wearables whenever a slot has 2 or more choices; a single choice (None alone) has no More button, its label under the picture at 460 x 320 and beside it at 320 x 440. **More slots** (slots view, 132 x 48) and **More wearables** (choices view, 180 x 48) show only when the buttons take more than one page: panel style, label-sized for the bold ink 20 px label, 48 tall, centred 4 px under the pictures' labels. Each press shows the next page, wrapping to page 1, and keeps the focus on the button. **Focus and keys**: Tab order is the tabs, the picture buttons, the More button (when shown), Back to slots (choices view), Cancel, Done; with the tab row hidden (fit step 7) it is Back to slots, the picture buttons, the More button, Cancel, Done. Arrows move in the picture buttons of the page. Focus starts on the first slot (Head) in the slots view, on the chosen option in the choices view, and returns to that slot's button after Back to slots. Escape in the choices view is Back to slots. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Explorer card (PRD F7, F10, F11, F16) | "Who's going exploring?" Shown when Go exploring is pressed, before the trip starts. Dim the Home Tank behind (ink at 0.45); the dim takes every press. White panel, padding 24 (12 when tight), centred. The title "Who's going exploring?" in bold ink 34 px, centred; it wraps. Under it the owned axolotls, up to 8, as picture buttons: panel style, square, the largest whole side from 48 to 96 px that fits, in the fewest balanced rows, 12 px apart, centred. Each picture is the whole axolotl (11) in its own look, Dream colour and outfit at the still pose (9), at x1.00, with its name as the label (bold ink 18 px, 4 px under; the cell is as wide as the longer of the picture and the name). The chosen axolotl has the 5 px ink ring and the tick badge, never colour alone. The last explorer is chosen when the card opens; with one axolotl it is chosen already. A press, or Enter or Space, chooses. Under the pictures, the chosen axolotl's outfit summary (above): a row for each perk type it wears with its total, the max mark when the cap is reached, or the one line "No perks yet". Under that, the buttons, in this order, 12 px apart, flowing in centred rows (stacked, Go! on top): **Go!** (primary #ffe14a, wide, play icon, label "Go!", 64 tall), **Change outfit** (secondary #55e4d8, wide, the Shirt icon, label "Change outfit", 64 tall) and **Back** (secondary tile, left chevron over the label, 65 x 64). **Focus and keys**: focus starts on Go! and the double focus ring always shows on it. Tab and Shift+Tab move through the axolotl pictures, More axolotls (when shown), Go!, Change outfit and Back, wrapping; arrows move in the pictures (left and right by one, up and down by a row); Enter and Space press, ignoring key repeat; Escape is Back. Go! ignores presses for the first 0.8 s the card shows, so the press that opened the card cannot start the trip. **Go!** starts the trip with the chosen explorer, in its own look and outfit. **Change outfit** opens the restyle screen on the Outfit tab for the chosen axolotl; Done or Cancel there returns to this card with the same axolotl chosen and Change outfit focused. **Back** closes the card and returns the focus to Go exploring in the hub. **Fit**: text and buttons never shrink (title 34, names and summary 18, buttons 64 tall). When the card does not fit, in order: 1. padding 24 to 12; 2. the picture side steps down toward 48; 3. the outfit summary flows side by side instead of one row each; 4. fewer axolotl pictures per page, one fewer at a time down to 1, with a "More axolotls" button (panel style, label-sized for the bold ink 20 px label, 168 x 48) 4 px under the pictures, whose press shows the next page, wrapping, and keeps the focus on it; 5. the title hides; 6. the outfit summary hides (Change outfit still opens the Outfit tab, which shows it); 7. More axolotls sits 12 px beside the pictures (to their right, centred down them) instead of under them, and steps 4 to 6 are tried again from the most pictures per page with the title and the summary shown, so they come back when they fit. Each try keeps the steps before it, and the first try that fits wins. The names and the buttons never hide. Measured on the layout grid with the worst names (12 wide letters) and a six-row capped summary, steps 1 to 6 alone do not fit 460 x 320 (the buttons take two rows there), so step 7 was added; with it the card fits every supported canvas (1): 460 x 320 shows 1 picture a page at step 7, and 320 x 440 shows 1 picture a page at step 6, both with no title and no summary (section 12). Below the smallest canvases the buttons still stay on the canvas. Appears and leaves at once (9). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Legs display | White panel (panel style), padding 12: the label "Legs" in bold ink 22 px, then four 32 px leg icons, 4 px apart. Details in 6q. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Snack counter (H3) | White panel (panel style), padding 12, at top-left above the legs display. The 32 px snack icon, 6 px, then the snacks left as "N left" in bold ink 22 px. Icon and number carry the meaning. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Snack banner (H2) | Soft pink #ffd9e6 panel (as the First-trip hint banner), ink text 24 px, or 20 px when no place has room at 24 px (HUD overlay fit), placed at a screen edge per HUD overlay fit, after the find toast. It fades in over 0.25 s, holds until 3 s after it shows, then fades out over 0.25 s (at once under reduced motion). Exit open (the last snack eaten): "Yum! That was the last snack. The exit is open!". Exit closed (the axolotl touches the closed exit): "Eat every snack to open the exit! N snacks left." ("1 snack left." for one). Plastic worm spat out (issue #75, PRD N3): "Bleh! That was plastic, not food." It is the same banner, placed and timed the same way. | `derived (provisional)` |
| "Area beaten" card (H5) | The tank arrival card (below) after the open exit is reached. Title "Area beaten!" (bold ink 34 px). The head icon is the earned Area star at 32 px, in place of the house icon. Under the head, a row of three 40 px Area stars, gap 12 (144 wide), centred, each earned or missing this trip. The body opens "You swam through the open exit!" and names the treasures banked this trip ("This trip you brought home <treasures>."). An axolotl rescued this trip is named first in that sentence, by the display name the Go home card uses: "You brought <Morph> axolotl to your Home Tank, plus <treasures>." (no treasures: "You brought <Morph> axolotl to your Home Tank."). Then the body has one line per star in star order (star 1 earned: "You reached the exit!"; missing: "Reach the exit next time."; star 3 earned by rescue: "You rescued an axolotl friend!"; earned by legs: "You kept all four legs!"; missed with a rescue still possible: "Rescue or keep every leg next time."; missed with no morph left to rescue: "Keep all four legs next time."), then the best so far ("Area 1: 2 of 3 stars"). The short wording is the one line "2 of 3 stars! Best: 3 of 3.". The star row is a fixed-height slot in Card fit and never shrinks. Motion as the tank arrival card (9). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Home Tank stars badge (H7) | White panel (panel style), padding 12: the area label ("Area 1") in bold ink 18 px, 8 px, then three 24 px Area stars, 4 px apart, earned or missing as the best so far. Two shapes, with the same text and star sizes: **beside** (the label at the left, the stars beside it, centred down) and **stacked** (the label centred across at the top, the star row centred 8 px under it; about 104 x 78.5 for "Area 1" to "Area 10"). When the hub reaches (Home Tank hub), the badge is stacked and always in the bottom-right corner inside the 16 px margins, never centred, even where it would fit centred (480 x 414): owner ruling A on issue #74 ([ADR 0017](adr/0017-xochimilco-refuge-and-habitat-milestones.md)); at 460 x 320 it is at {340, 225.5, 104, 78.5}. Otherwise it is beside, centred 12 px under the hub title and buttons (at the top of the hub's room when there are none) when it fits across and ends inside the room of the hub's last row (Home Tank hub), so never in the top band; else in the bottom-right corner inside the 16 px margins (844 x 320). At 1280 x 720 it sits under the full-size row, at y 382.5. Shown in the hub and while the tank arrival card is up; hidden in the Treasure Chest, in the Collection Book and in decorate mode. Not interactive, and not a focus stop. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Area map (PRD N2, issue #73) | Shown after Go! on the explorer card (F7), before the trip starts: one marker per area, in area order. Opaque page background #0c6a8c (3d) that takes every press. **Colours**: the 3d set, plus one pond colour: the backdrop #0c6a8c, the path #55e4d8, unlocked markers #ffe14a (the unlocked pond marker is #4fd462, see Marker), locked markers and every panel and badge disc #ffffff, the message line #ffd9e6, the "New!" pill #ff82bf, and ink #2b2350 for every outline, icon and text; the Area stars keep their 7g art. **Fixed parts** (they never scroll). **Back**: the standard wide secondary button, #55e4d8, 200 x 64 (Button width), with the left chevron icon and the label "Back", at the top-left inside the 16 px margins. It returns to the explorer card with the same axolotl chosen and Go! focused. **Title**: "Where to?" in bold ink 28 px on a white panel (panel style, padding 8), 12 px right of Back, centred on the canvas when that is not left of there, and centred down Back; when it does not fit beside Back, centred 12 px under Back. **Message line**: on the bottom margin, centred: a soft pink #ffd9e6 panel (panel style), padding 8 across and 4 down, the text in bold ink 20 px, wrapping at the canvas width less the margins. Its room is always kept, so the map never moves, but it shows only after a press on a locked marker: "Beat Area N to unlock!", where N is the area before it (a press on Area 4 shows "Beat Area 3 to unlock!"). It holds until 3 s after it shows. A new locked press restarts it, any other press clears it, and it takes no input. Motion in section 9. **Viewport**: the full canvas width, between the title or Back (the lower) and the message line, 12 px clear of each. Only the map in it scrolls. **Scroll axis by aspect**: across when the canvas is at least as wide as tall, else down. A drag scrolls the map along the axis, 1:1 with no fling, once the pointer has moved 8 px (a shorter move is a press); the mouse wheel scrolls it too. A map shorter than the viewport along the axis is centred in it and does not scroll. **Habitats**: the markers are grouped by habitat (PRD N2 area table), one group per run of areas in the same habitat. Today the map holds two areas in two groups, Canal and Lily pad pond. Each group has its habitat label at its start: the habitat name (for example "Lily pad pond") in bold ink 20 px on a white panel (panel style, padding 8 across and 4 down, 32 tall for one line). The labels sit in a band across the axis, 16 px in from the map edge: above the markers when the map scrolls across, left of them when it scrolls down. A label above the markers wraps at the canvas width less the margins; a label left of them wraps at the canvas width less the margins, 12 px and one cell (80). The lane of markers starts 12 px past the band and ends 16 px before the far map edge. A group starts 32 px past the end of the group before it: its last cell or its label, whichever is longer. **Path**: one winding tube through the marker centres in area order, from the first marker to the last (a smooth curve through the centres), #55e4d8, 10 wide with `ol` 2.6 (4b), under the markers. Across the axis the markers step middle, one side, middle, the other side, repeating, with up to 48 px between the furthest centres, so the path winds; it never leaves the lane. **Marker cell**: the room each marker needs, 80 across and 112 down: a 12 px slot for the "New!" pill, the 64 px marker, 12 px, then the star row. The lane is as deep across the axis as the cell plus the winding. Along the axis, neighbouring markers are 32 px apart when the map scrolls across (their cells 16 px apart); when it scrolls down, neighbouring cells are 32 px apart (the markers 80 px apart), so one marker's star row never meets the next one's pill slot. **Marker**: a round disc 64 px to the outer ink edge, `shape` ink 3.5, its centre 44 px down the cell; fill #ffe14a when unlocked, #ffffff when locked. **The marker follows its habitat look** (issue #75): the canal look is this disc. The lily pad pond look is a **notched lily pad**, in the same 64 px frame, anchor and size to the outer ink edge, with the same `shape` ink 3.5, so cells, targets, the focus ring and the badges do not change. Unlocked it is #4fd462, locked #ffffff. (The lily pad green #47ce5a is not used: it is 2.96:1 on the backdrop, under 3:1.) Ink on #4fd462 is 7.49:1 and on white 14.39:1; #4fd462 on the backdrop is 3.16:1 and white 6.08:1, so each marker edge passes 3:1 by its fill. The notch is a wedge cut into the lower rim: it points down, centred on 90 degrees, half angle 0.5 rad, its tip at radius 21 from the centre, so it stops short of the number. It stays clear of the padlock badge (upper right) and the "New!" pill (top), and takes at least 5% of the disc's area, which is the shape cue apart from the disc in greyscale (10). The area number ("1" to "10") in bold ink 28 px, centred. Its tap target is the marker's 64 px square. **Stars**: three 24 px Area stars (7g, earned and missing styles) in a centred row, 4 px apart (80 wide), 12 px under the marker's ink edge, as the best so far; a locked area shows three missing stars. **Locked**: the padlock badge, a white disc r 12 with an ink ring 2.6 holding the 18 px lock icon (Lock icon, below), centred 24 px right of and 24 px above the marker centre, so it overhangs the rim at the upper right. The number still shows. A press starts nothing and shows the message line. **"New!"**: on the newest unlocked area while it has no stars (best 0 of 3), a pill 56 x 24, #ff82bf, `shape` ink 2.6, with the word "New!" in bold ink 18 px (padding 6 across), centred across the marker with its centre on the marker's top ink edge. The bob (9) may poke the pill 2 px past the top of the cell; nothing collides, as cells are at least 16 px apart. The word is the cue and the pill is its shape. A locked marker never has it. **Press**: a press on an unlocked marker starts the trip in that area with the chosen explorer. The markers ignore presses for the first 0.8 s the map shows, so the press on Go! cannot start a trip. **Focus**: the double focus ring of this section round the focused marker, as circles about its centre: white 3 px at radius 35.25 and ink 3 px at radius 38.25, both opaque, over the marker and under its badge and pill. Focus starts on the area last played when it is on the map, else on the newest unlocked area. The map opens, and follows the focus, scrolled as little as it can to show the whole focused marker (its 64 px tap square) with 16 px round it (motion in 9). The star row under it, or the "New!" pill over it, may stay partly hidden at the far edge of the viewport (section 12). Keys and targets are in section 10. **Fit**: text, markers (64), stars (24) and Back (200 x 64) never shrink. The path winds as much as the lane has room for, up to 48 px, down to straight. When the cells do not fit across the viewport, in order: 1. the title moves from beside Back to under it, only when it does not fit beside Back (as wide as the room and no taller than Back); 2. each habitat label leaves the band and sits along the axis at the start of its group, centred across the lane, 12 px before the group's first cell (this frees the band); 3. the title hides. Steps 2 and 3 do not force step 1: the title stays beside Back through step 2 when it fits there. The first try that fits wins. By this guide's arithmetic (0.6 em text estimate, a line height of 1.2 em so a 20 px label with 4 px padding down is 32 tall, all ten areas of the PRD N2 table): at 460 x 320 the title fits beside Back (167 of 216 px), the message takes one line, the viewport is 460 x 168, and step 2 is taken: the lane is 136 deep and the path winds 24 px, with about 4 markers across. At 320 x 440 the title is under Back (step 1), the message room is sized for the longest text, "Beat Area 10 to unlock!" (the largest area number on the map; the longest message a press can show is "Beat Area 9 to unlock!", for locked Area 10), which wraps to two lines, so the viewport is 320 x 202, and the labels stay in the band (wrapping at 196 px, so "Chinampa gardens" takes two lines and "Lily pad pond", 172 px, is the widest): the lane is 104 wide and the path winds 24 px, with one whole cell down at a time (section 12). Below the smallest canvases, Back and the focused marker stay on the canvas. Appears and leaves at once (9). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Lock icon | In the 32 icon box: the 7g exit sign padlock at x 1.5 about the box centre, as the house icon (7d). The shackle, an ink stroke 3.9 (2.6 x 1.5), from (10.9,14.88) up to (10.9,10.38), a half circle of radius 5.1 round (16,10.38) to (21.1,10.38), then down to (21.1,14.88). The body, a filled ink rounded rectangle x 7.75 to 24.25 and y 15.18 to 28.68 (16.5 x 13.5), corner radius 3.75. A white keyhole dot r 1.95 at (16,22.08). It is 16.5 x 25.35 to the outer ink edge (y 3.33 to 28.68). It scales with the box, ink too (2.93 at 24 px, 2.19 at 18 px), as the perk icons do (7b). Used at 18 px in the Area map's padlock badge, and for any locked control (Icons, above), always with text nearby. Still frame, so it is also the reduced-motion version. | `derived (provisional)` |
| World bubbles ("!" and "dash needed") | The "!" bubble is a sun yellow #ffe14a disc, ink 3, with a thought dot (7c). The dash-needed bubble is white, ink 3.5. Both always upright, never mirrored. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Trip Bag icon | A drawstring pouch in the 32 icon box: rounded body from (12.5,12) down to y 27.5, a gathered top flaring to y 6, and one loose drawstring end. Ink 3.0 only, round joins and caps, no fill. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Home Tank icons | In the 32 icon box, ink only, round joins and caps, always with a label, or on a compact icon-only tile with the focus caption naming it. Chest (Treasure Chest): a domed lid over a rounded body, x 5.5 to 26.5 and y 6.5 to 27, seam at y 15, a filled lock dot r 2.2 at (16,17.5), ink 3.0. Book (Collection Book): an open book, two rounded pages from a centre spine at x 16, x 5.5 to 26.5, their tops curving from y 8 at the spine to y 10 at the outer edge and their bottoms at y 25 with rounded outer corners, one short text line per page at y 15.5 (x 9.5 to 12.5 and 19.5 to 22.5), ink 3.0. Decorate: a sprig, an upright stem from y 27.5 to 6.5 at x 16 with a lower-left and an upper-right leaf, ink 2.8. Close: a rounded X from (9,9) to (23,23), ink 4.5. Left and right (Back and Next): one chevron 9 wide and 18 tall, ink 4.5. Check (OK and Done): a tick (7,17) to (13.5,23.5) to (25,9), ink 4.5. Put away: a down arrow from y 5.5 into an open tray (x 5.5 to 26.5, y 18 to 27), ink 3.2. Refuge (issue #74): strokes only, no fill, ink 3.0: a notched lily pad over one wave line. The pad is a circle r 7.5 about (16,11) with a wedge notch opening to the lower right (centred on 45 degrees, half angle 0.42 rad, its tip at the pad centre); the wave runs from (5.5,26) to (26.5,26) in one swell, its crest 1.75 above the line and its trough 1.75 below. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Surprise icon | In the 32 icon box, ink only, always with the "Surprise me" label: two outline 4-point stars (the 4e star shape: inner radius 0.38R, tip rounding 0.15R, no fill), a big one centred at (14,18) with R 10 and a small one at (25,8) with R 5, ink 3. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Trip Bag display (A16) | White panel (panel style below), padding 12, under the legs display at top-left. Bag icon 32 px, the label "Trip Bag", the count as a number in bold ink 22 px, and "not safe yet" in ink 18 px or more while the bag holds finds. Icon, number, and text carry the meaning, never colour. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Panel style | Fill, ink 3.5 outline, corner radius min(height / 2, 18). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Banking card (A14) | Dim the game behind (ink at 0.45). White panel, padding 24 (12 on short screens, per Card fit), centred. Title in bold ink 34 px ("Safe at the Home Spot!"). The treasure icons (each treasure's icon in a 32 px box, gap 8, over its 40 px rarity glow, 7e), in wrapping rows, above the description text (20 px, max width 420). The icon rows wrap at the icon row width: the canvas width less the 16 px margins and the 24 px padding each side, never narrower than the text. That is the text width on canvases up to 500 px wide; wider canvases fit more icons in a row (18 at 800 px wide), while the text keeps its 420 max. The banked total as text, 20 px. A rescued axolotl banked with the finds is named first in the description by its display name (for example "Wild axolotl is safe now!"); the short wording calls it "Your axolotl friend". The icon row and the banked total count treasures only. Two wide buttons, both 64 px tall with a 12 px gap: Keep exploring (primary #ffe14a, play icon) and Go home (secondary #55e4d8, house icon). Side by side, Keep exploring on the left; stacked, Keep exploring on top, when the canvas is under 460 CSS px wide. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Go home button (contextual) | Wide secondary #55e4d8 button, 64 px tall, house icon and the label "Go home", top-right under the Pause button with a 12 px gap. Shown only at the Home Spot. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Tank arrival message | Shown over the Home Tank after every trip, in place of the carried-home card and the old interim screen. Dim the Home Tank behind (ink at 0.45); the dim takes every press. White panel, padding 24 (12 on short screens, per Card fit), centred, with the house icon (32 px) 12 px left of the title (bold ink 34 px), then one 20 px text block (max width 420) holding the body text and the banked line on its own line, an icon row of the treasures the body names (each treasure icon in a 32 px box, gap 8, over its 40 px rarity glow, wrapping at the icon row width as on the banking card), and one wide primary #ffe14a button, 64 px tall, with the tick icon and the label "OK". OK always shows the focus ring. It ignores presses for the first 0.8 s the card shows. The hub buttons stay drawn under the dim, unfocused. Fits the canvas per Card fit. Motion in section 9. The body names a rescued axolotl banked this trip by its display name, now in the Home Tank ("Wild axolotl moved into your Home Tank!"). After a carried-home trip it names a following axolotl that swam back, with the waiting message in "he" or "she" to match its boy or girl ("Wild axolotl swam back to where you found him. He's waiting for you!" or "... found her. She's waiting for you!"; more than one: "... found them. They're waiting for you!"). The short wording says "Your axolotl friend". The icon row shows treasures only. After OK, the first-find cards that are due show one at a time in its place (First-find card (C15)); the hub takes the input only once the last one closes. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Tilapia fact card (A21), plastic worm fact card (N3) and heron fact card (N3) | Shown over the paused swim the first time the axolotl meets a tilapia (outside the safe area, within 180 px, tuning), once per trip until it is seen. The plastic worm fact card (issue #75) is this card, shown the first time the axolotl meets a plastic worm (within 100 px, tuning): title "Plastic is not food", the fact as its body, and the same OK, dim and rules. The heron fact card (issue #98) is this card too, shown the first time the axolotl meets a heron while the heron is shown (outside the safe area, within 180 px of its shadow, tuning): title "Meet the heron", the fact as its body, and the same OK, dim and rules. Play pauses under it and the Pause button hides. Dim the game behind (ink at 0.45); the dim takes every press. White panel, padding 24 (12 on short screens, per Card fit), centred. Title "Meet the tilapia" in bold ink 34 px, centred. **Title fit** (issue #75): the title wraps at the tight-padding width, the canvas width less twice the canvas margin and the tight padding (12), from 120 to 420 px (404 px at 460 wide), up to 24 px wider than the body text on canvases under 484 px wide, so a long title such as "Plastic is not food" wraps to fewer lines and always shows. The body keeps its 420 px max. **Body fit** (issue #98, [ADR 0016](adr/0016-heron-timer-warnings-and-area-look-variants.md)): the body first wraps at the body width; when the card does not fit the canvas that way, the same full body wraps at the title's tight-padding width instead, which takes fewer lines on canvases under 484 px wide. Only the heron card at 320 x 440 uses it: its body takes 9 lines at 240 px, which does not fit, and 8 lines at 264 px, which does. The fact as one centred 20 px text block (max width 420). One wide primary #ffe14a button, 64 px tall, with the tick icon and the label "OK"; it always shows the focus ring and ignores presses for the first 0.8 s the card shows. Enter, Space, P, or Esc press OK. No picture on the card; the tilapia picture is on its Collection Book page. Fits the canvas per Card fit. Measured on the layout grid at 460 x 320, the plastic worm card needs about 279.5 px of the 288 px the canvas leaves inside the margins, so it fits. Motion in section 9. | `derived (provisional)` |
| First-find card (C15) | Shown over the Home Tank right after the tank arrival card's OK, once per save for each kind: the first banked Decoration, Wearable and Paint shell. When several are due they show one at a time, in the order Decoration, Wearable, Paint shell, so "Try it now!" comes last. Never mid-trip, and never when the Home Tank opens from the title screen. Dim the Home Tank behind (ink at 0.45); the dim takes every press. White panel, padding 24 (12 on short screens, per Card fit), centred. Head row, centred: the icon of the first banked treasure of its kind in a 32 px box over its 40 px rarity glow (7e), 12 px, then the title in bold ink 34 px ("You found a Decoration!", "You found a Wearable!", "You found a Paint shell!"). The body as one centred 20 px text block (max width 420); the Decoration and Wearable bodies are their Treasure Chest section lines. Buttons, 64 px tall, 12 px apart: the Paint shell card has "Try it now!" (wide primary #ffe14a, play icon, focused first; it opens restyle on the Dream colour tab) and "Maybe later" (wide secondary #55e4d8, close icon; it closes the card), side by side, or stacked with "Try it now!" on top when they do not fit across; the other cards have one wide primary "OK" with the tick icon, which always shows the focus ring. Every button, and Escape, ignores presses for the first 0.8 s the card shows, so the press that closed the arrival card cannot press it. Touch works too. Tab, Shift+Tab, left and right move the focus; Enter and Space press, ignoring key repeat; Escape closes the card like its close button. Fits the canvas per Card fit, trying the wordings richest first: the Paint shell card falls back to the short body "It unlocks a Dream colour. Try it in Restyle.", then also the short title "New Paint shell!". Appears and leaves at once in both motion modes (9). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Title screen | White panel (panel style, padding 16) at top-centre with the name "Glam Gills" in bold ink 34 px, the subtitle "Find Your Flair" in bold ink 22 px 4 px below it, the controls text 18 px, and the version label in small text. When the panel would not fit above the Start button, the Start over button and the update prompt, the controls text hides first. The name, subtitle and version always show. Start is a wide primary #ffe14a button with the play icon; the Start over button (below) sits centred 12 px under it while it shows. Focus starts on Start. Tab and Shift+Tab move the focus between Start and Start over, wrapping; Enter and Space press the focused button. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Start over button (D7) | A wide secondary #55e4d8 button at size 0.8 (as Put away: 51.2 px tall, 22 px label), label-sized (Button width), with the close icon and the label "Start over". Centred 12 px under Start on the title screen. Shown only once an axolotl is owned and only while no update prompt shows (section 12, recorded residual). A press opens the first Start over card. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Start over cards (D7) | Two cards in a row; erasing happens only after the go button is pressed on both. Dim the title screen behind (ink at 0.45); the dim takes every press. White panel, padding 24 (12 when tight, per Card fit), centred. Title in bold ink 34 px, centred, then one centred 20 px text block (max width 420). Step 1: title "Start over?", the body "This erases your axolotls, treasures, tank and stars on this device." and the grown-ups note (below) on its own line; buttons Keep playing (primary #ffe14a, play icon) and Start over (secondary #55e4d8, close icon). Step 2: title "Are you sure?", the body "This can't be undone."; buttons No, keep them (primary #ffe14a, tick icon) and Yes, erase (secondary #55e4d8, close icon). Wide buttons, 64 px tall, label-sized (Button width), 12 px apart: side by side, the keep button on the left, or stacked, the keep button on top, when the two with the 12 px gap are wider than the canvas less the 16 px margins and the 24 px padding each side. Fit, in order: the full wording, then the short wording ("This erases everything." in place of the step 1 body), then the title hides (section 12, recorded residual); padding 24 before 12 at each try, as Card fit. The grown-ups note never drops: it is in every wording. Below the smallest supported canvas (1), the text is cut to whole lines. Text and buttons never shrink. Focus starts on the keep button of each step and always shows the double focus ring. Tab and Shift+Tab move it between the two buttons; Enter and Space press the focused button; Escape keeps, as the keep button. The go button (Start over, Yes, erase) ignores presses for the first 0.8 s each step shows. Keep closes the card with nothing erased. Motion in section 9. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Start over grown-ups note (D8) | Part of the step 1 body, on its own line after the body: "Grown-ups: progress is saved only in this browser on this device. Clearing browser data erases it." Same 20 px ink text, centred. The README carries the same note. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Card fit (banking card, tank arrival card) | The buttons sit at the panel bottom, and the panel is never taller than the canvas minus the 16 px margins. When the content does not fit: padding 24 to 12, then fewer treasure icon rows (whole rows, from the end), then no icons, then the short wording (a count instead of names; icons come back if they fit). Icon rows are counted at the icon row width (Banking card), so on canvases over 500 px wide the same icons take fewer rows. Below the smallest supported canvas (1), the text is cut to whole lines. Text and buttons never shrink (body 20 px, title 34 px, buttons 64 px). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Button width | A button's body may be wider than its standard body to hold its label: a wide button is at least 200 wide, a square tile at least 64. A label-sized body is just wide enough for its content row plus 6 px each side, rounded up to a whole CSS px. A wide button's row is the 32 px icon, an 8 px gap and the label (bold 24 px). A tile's row is the label alone (bold 22 px), because its icon (0.85 of the 32 box) sits over the label. Label width is estimated at 0.6 em per character, which errs wide. Width never changes the height, icon, label size or press offset; those are fixed by the button size (64 px tall at size 1). An icon-only tile (the compact Home Tank tiles) is a square tile at size 0.75, a 48 x 48 body with only a 32 px icon centred on it (not scaled by the size) and no label drawn; its label names it in the focus caption. The compact Done is a label-sized wide button, 110 x 64, with its icon and label. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Home Tank hub | The five hub actions over the Home Tank (7f), in focus and layout order: Go exploring, a wide primary #ffe14a button with the play icon, 225 x 64; Decorate, Treasure Chest, Collection Book and Refuge (PRD N2, issue #74), secondary #55e4d8 square tiles with the icon (sprig, chest, book, refuge) over a 22 px bold label, 118 x 64, 197 x 64, 210 x 64 and 92 x 64 (sizes per Button width). Refuge opens the refuge screen (below); its Back returns to the hub with the Refuge tile focused. The Refuge tile wears the nudge badge (Refuge nudge badge, below) while a refuge clean-up is pending. They keep clear of the tank's top band, tank y 20 to 108 (the Floating and Hanging band, y 24 to 104, plus 4 for the bob and sway, 7f). They go in two rooms: the wall, from the 16 px top margin to 12 px above the tank lid; and the open water, from 12 px under that band to 12 px above the Decoration band (the top of an 80 px Decoration standing on the band top, 7f) and 16 px above the canvas bottom. A room under 48 px tall is not used. They flow in centred rows, 12 px apart across and between rows, within the canvas width less the margins, each row whole inside one room. The first that fits is used: full size in one room (the wall, then the water); compact in one room; compact rows split across the rooms in order (414 x 440 and 430 x 440); the **reach try**: compact rows in the reach rooms of the Decorate controls (below: the wall down to 12 px above the top band, and the open water from 12 px under the band down to the bottom margin, over the floor Decorations, never into the top band), one room top first, then split down them in order; compact from the top of the tallest room. The hub reaches only at 460 and 480 wide by 320, 340, 360, 375 and 414 tall; with the four actions before Refuge it never reaches. Compact: Decorate, Treasure Chest, Collection Book and Refuge become 48 x 48 icon-only tiles (Button width); Go exploring keeps its 225 x 64 body and its label. With five actions the full row is 890 wide, so the hub is newly compact (by the same full-size-first rule) on 320, 340, 459, 460 and 520 x 568, 320, 340 and 520 x 600, 480 x 568, and 844 x 320 to 480 ([ADR 0017](adr/0017-xochimilco-refuge-and-habitat-milestones.md)). At 460 x 320 the compact hub reaches in two rows in the water from y 108 (room y 108 to 304): row 1 is Go exploring (y 108 to 172) and the Decorate, Treasure Chest and Collection Book tiles at y 116; row 2 is Refuge at {206, 184, 48, 48}, its bottom over the floor Decorations.
