At 320 x 440 it is two compact rows on the wall (Go exploring and Decorate, then the other three); at 1280 x 720 there is no wall room, so the hub keeps full size in the water, the name panel at y 228 and the buttons at y 306.5 (the row x 195 to 1085). The name panel, a white panel (panel style, padding 12) holding "Home Tank" in bold ink 34 px, sits centred at the top of the hub's room with the buttons 12 px below it, only when the hub uses one room, the panel fits across, and the buttons still fit that room, and never when the hub reaches; otherwise it hides. The hub focus caption, a white panel (panel style, padding 12) with the focused tile's label in bold ink 18 px, shows only while an icon-only tile has the focus in the hub: centred 12 px under the hub inside its room, else in the open water (where it may sit over the floor Decorations) inside the bottom margin, never in the top band, and 12 px clear of the stars badge; it hides when there is no place. When the hub reaches, the room under the hub is its reach room: at 460 x 320 the caption is centred 12 px under Refuge at {137, 244, 186, 46.5}, 17 px from the stacked stars badge (27 px at 480 x 320). Focus starts on Go exploring, and comes back to Decorate, Treasure Chest or Collection Book when those close, and to Refuge after the refuge's Back. Tab, right and down step the focus forward and Shift+Tab, left and up step it back, wrapping; Enter and Space press, ignoring key repeat. The hub buttons hide while the Treasure Chest, the Collection Book or decorate mode is open. UI text says "Home Tank" (7f). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Refuge nudge badge (PRD N2) | The "refuge grew" cue on the hub Refuge tile (7i): a 22 px #ff82bf disc with an ink ring 2.6 and a solid ink 4-point star, over the tile, its centre at the top-right corner of the shown tile (the full 92 x 64 tile or the 48 x 48 icon-only tile) pulled in 8 px on each axis. Shown only while the hub shows and a refuge clean-up is pending; it goes once the refuge opens, which takes the pending clean-ups to play them. Not interactive, and not a focus stop. A disc with a star, unlike the plus badge, the padlock badge and the "New!" pill (a word in a pill), so it reads in greyscale and never by colour alone (10). Still in both motion modes. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Refuge screen (PRD N2, issue #74) | Opened by the hub Refuge tile. Opaque #0c6a8c backdrop (3g) that takes every press, as the Area map. **Fixed parts** (they never scroll): **Back**, the standard wide secondary button, 200 x 64, left chevron and the label "Back", top-left inside the 16 px margins; it returns to the hub with the Refuge tile focused. **Prev and Next**: secondary square tiles, the left chevron over the label "Previous" and the right chevron over the label "Next", label-sized (Button width): Previous 118 x 64, Next 65 x 64. Top-right (Prev 12 px before Next) when the canvas is at least 439 wide (16 + Back 200 + 12 + Previous 118 + 12 + Next 65 + 16), else in a bottom row on the bottom margin, centred, 12 px apart. At 460 x 320 they sit at x 249 and 379, and the title hides. **Title**: "Xochimilco refuge" in bold ink 28 px on a white panel (the Area map title panel, padding 8), 12 px right of Back, centred on the canvas where it can be and centred down Back, only when it fits there (up to 12 px before Prev, or to the right margin when the pan tiles are in the bottom row, and no taller than Back); else it hides. **Viewport**: the full canvas width, from 12 px under Back down to the bottom margin, or to 12 px above the bottom pan row. Only the refuge in it scrolls, along x only. **Sections**: one per habitat that holds an area, in habitat order, each 200 wide: from the top, the name row (the habitat name, or "???" while hidden, in bold ink 18 px on a white panel, padding 8 across and 4 down, centred), 8 px, the 200 x 96 section picture (7i), 8 px, then a 64 px slot. The slot holds, centred, the **"See it again"** tile on a clean section (a secondary square tile, 171 x 64, the play icon over the label; it shows that habitat's milestone card again, naming the current explorer), else the hint panel on a hidden section ("Beat Area N" in bold ink 18 px on the Area map's soft pink #ffd9e6, padding 8 across and 4 down). A dirty section has an empty slot and its trash on the picture. Sections run 12 px apart from 16 px in, then the "More to come" teaser (7i, at alpha 0.5) with its label in the name row style over it, then 16 px to the content edge; the sections are centred down the viewport, and content narrower than the viewport is centred and does not scroll. At 460 x 320 the viewport is 460 x 212 and two whole sections show (206.5 tall); at 320 x 440 it is 320 x 256 with the pan tiles in the bottom row and one whole section. **Scroll**: a drag 1:1 along x with no fling, once the pointer has moved 8 px (a shorter move is a press); the mouse wheel; Prev and Next (or Left and Right) pan one section, lining the section's start up 16 px in from the viewport edge, and do nothing at either end; a focused "See it again" tile scrolls as little as it can to show its whole section with 16 px round it. The refuge opens scrolled to the first section with a clean-up to play. Motion in 9. **Focus and keys**: Tab and Shift+Tab step through the "See it again" tiles, Prev, Next and Back, wrapping; focus starts on Back. Left and Right pan, on key repeat too; Enter and Space press the focused control, ignoring key repeat; Escape is Back, on key repeat too. Focus shows by the focused button visuals (the double focus ring), never by colour alone. **Fact cards**: the tilapia fact card frame (above: dim, panel, OK) shows "Axolotl refuges" the first time the refuge opens, and "Canal clean-ups" once the refuge shows trash already cleared, or after the clean-ups; each shows once and is found in the Collection Book. Keys go to an open card first. **Fit**: text, tiles, pictures and Back never shrink; the title is the only part that hides. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Refuge milestone card (PRD N2, issue #74) | Shown in the Home Tank after a trip that newly made a habitat's section clean, after the tank arrival card's OK (at once on create when there is no arrival card), one card per habitat in habitat order; and again from the refuge's "See it again" tile. Dim the scene behind (ink at 0.45, 3g); the dim takes every press. White panel, padding 24 (12 when tight, per Card fit), centred. Stacked: the title "All clean!" in bold ink 34 px, centred; the habitat's clean section picture (7i), 200 x 96; the body as one centred 20 px text block, "You and <name> cleaned up the <habitat name in lower case>! Real people in Xochimilco do this work too." (for example "the lily pad pond"), naming the explorer, wrapping at the body width, then at the tight-padding width when that fits (the fact card body fit); one wide primary #ffe14a button with the play icon and the label "Keep exploring", 64 px tall, which always shows the focus ring and closes the card. Fit per Card fit; when nothing fits with the title, the title hides and the card fits again without it. Text, picture and button never shrink. Enter, Space and Escape press Keep exploring, ignoring key repeat for Enter and Space; every press is ignored for the first 0.8 s the card shows. Appears and leaves at once in both motion modes (9). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Home Tank axolotl picking (PRD F6) | After the hub buttons, the hub focus runs on through each owned axolotl, then wraps. A focused axolotl shows the double focus ring round its sprite bounds (as on a decorate footprint, 7f: 3 px lines, inner white 3.25 px and outer ink 6.25 px outside the bounds, corner radius 18 plus the offset, both opaque) and a name tag: a white panel (panel style), padding 12, with the name in bold ink 18 px. The tag is centred across the axolotl 12 px above its sprite bounds, or 12 px below when above would cross the 16 px top margin, and stays inside the canvas margins. Ring and tag follow the axolotl every frame and show only while the hub has the input. Each axolotl's tap area is its texture frame, grown about its centre to at least 48 x 48 CSS px. A press and release on an axolotl, or Enter or Space on a focused one (ignoring key repeat), opens the axolotl choices card for it (below), not the restyle screen. Axolotls take taps only in the hub, so they never block a decorate press. Done or Cancel in restyle, and Back on the card, go back to the hub with that axolotl focused. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Axolotl choices card (PRD F6, F16) | What the player can do with one owned axolotl, shown when it is picked in the Home Tank (issue #82). Dim the Home Tank behind (ink at 0.45, 3g); the dim takes every press and does nothing. White panel, padding 24 (12 when tight), centred inside the 16 px margins. The title is the axolotl's name in bold ink 34 px, centred; it wraps. Under it the buttons, in this order, 12 px apart across and between rows, flowing in centred rows: **Restyle** (the pencil icon), **Outfit** (the Shirt icon, Outfit icons above), **Dream colour** (the Paint drop icon, below; shown only when at least one Dream colour is owned) and **Back** (left chevron). All are secondary #55e4d8 square tiles, as in the Home Tank hub: the icon over the 22 px bold label, 64 tall. The choices are one width, as wide as the widest choice label shown needs (171 x 64 with Dream colour, 105 x 64 without), and Back is 65 x 64. **Focus and keys**: focus starts on Restyle and the double focus ring always shows on the focused button. Tab, right and down move forward through the choices and Back; Shift+Tab, left and up move back; both wrap. Enter and Space press the focused button, ignoring key repeat. Escape is Back at once, repeats included. Every button press (pointer, Enter and Space) is ignored for the first 0.8 s the card shows, so the press that opened it cannot choose. **Restyle** opens the restyle screen on Eyes; **Outfit** opens it on the Outfit tab's slots view with Head focused; **Dream colour** opens it on the Dream colour tab with the chosen option focused. Done or Cancel there goes back to the hub with that axolotl focused, not to this card. **Back** and Escape close the card and go back to the hub with that axolotl focused. **Fit**: text and buttons never shrink (title 34, buttons 64 tall). When the card does not fit, in order: 1. padding 24 to 12; 2. the title hides. Each try keeps the steps before it, and the first try that fits wins. Measured on the layout grid with the worst name (12 wide letters): with Dream colour, 460 x 320 fits at padding 24 in two rows (Restyle, Outfit / Dream colour, Back; panel 402 x 254.5), 320 x 440 at padding 12 in three rows (Restyle / Outfit / Dream colour, Back; panel 294.5 tall) and 1280 x 720 in one row; without it, 460 x 320 and 1280 x 720 fit in one row and 320 x 440 at padding 12 in two rows (Restyle, Outfit / Back). The title shows on every supported canvas (1); it hides only below them, where the buttons still stay on the canvas. Appears and leaves at once (9). **Paint drop** (Dream colour): in the 32 icon box, ink only, round joins and caps, always with a label, like the Home Tank icons, ink 3.0. A leaning drop outline with no fill and no shine: the body a circle r 7.5 centred at (13.5,17.5), the tip at (20,4) leaning right, the sides the two tangents from the tip, meeting the circle at (9.27,11.29) and (20.98,16.93), the tip rounded with `arcTo` r 1.5 (the visible tip at y 5.5); a small filled ink droplet r 2.2 at (26.5,21); and a splash line from (7,29) to (20,29). It is never a closed filled drop (the Axanthic morph badge), a rarity shape, or the Dream swatch tile (10). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Decorate controls | A toolbar in place of the hub buttons, in the same rooms by the same order of tries (Home Tank hub: centred rows, 12 px gaps, clear of the top band): the tray squares for owned Decorations not yet placed, then Put away, then Done. The look and the rooms are chosen as if every owned Decoration were in the tray, so they hold still as the tray empties. Full look: 64 px tray squares (white panel, panel style, with the 48 px treasure icon centred over its 56 px rarity glow, 7e); Put away a wide secondary button at size 0.8 (160 x 51.2, put away icon, 22 px label); Done a wide primary button, 200 x 64, with the tick icon. Compact look, only where the full look does not fit: 48 px tray squares (the 32 px icon over its 40 px glow); Put away a 48 x 48 icon-only tile; Done label-sized, 110 x 64, with its tick and label (Button width). Put away's slot is always kept, but it shows only while a Decoration is selected. Fourteen Decorations ship (four canal, four pond, six Area 3), so trays of 5 to 14 are real, not only a dev case with the `?decor` Decorations. A tray of more than 4 wraps into more rows. The rows take the hub's order of tries in the two rooms first; a tray that fits none of them takes the same order of tries in the reach rooms, which may go over the floor Decorations: the wall down to a gap above the Floating and Hanging keep-clear band (tank y 20 to 108), and the open water down to the bottom margin (rooms under a 48 px tile are dropped), else the rows stack from the top of the tallest room. Every row stays clear of the top band. Measured on the layout grid with 8 tray squares: at 460 x 320 the compact look is two rows in the open water, 7 squares at y 108, just under the top band, then the 8th square, Put away (48 x 48) and Done (110 x 64), with Done's bottom at y 232 over the floor band, whose top is at y 199.1; at 320 x 440 it is three rows on the wall (5 squares, then 3 squares and Put away, then Done alone), with the caption in the open water at y 284; at 340 x 440 the wall holds the tray alone (5 squares, then 3 squares and Put away) and Done sits alone in the open water, below the top band. Measured with all 14 shipped Decorations in the tray: at 460 x 320 the compact look is three rows, two rows of 7 squares over the floor band (y 108 and y 168), then Put away (x 145, y 236) and Done (x 205, y 228, its bottom at y 292), and the caption has no place, so it hides; at 320 x 440 it is three rows on the wall (5 squares at y 16, 5 at y 76, then 4 at y 136 with Put away ending the third row), Done alone in the open water at y 284, just under the top band, and the caption has no place, so it hides; at 340 x 440 the wall holds the same three rows, Done sits in the open water at y 273.5, and the caption shows under it at y 349.5. With 4 or fewer the controls stay inside the rooms, and the caption always shows. The caption, ink 18 px, centred, wraps at the canvas width less the margins and sits 12 px below the controls inside their room, else in the open water (where it may sit over the floor Decorations) inside the bottom margin, never in the top band; it hides when there is no place (with a tray of more than 4 it is placed or hidden by that rule). It names the focused Decoration, or "Put away" while the icon-only Put away has the focus; otherwise it shows the hint, or the message for no Decorations owned or for all of them placed. Focus order: tray squares, placed Decorations back to front, Put away while shown, Done; there is no focus until the first key. Enter or a tap on a tray square places that Decoration (7f, New placement) and selects it; on a placed Decoration it selects it. Arrows move a focused placed Decoration (7f); Up and Down do nothing on a Floating or Hanging one, with no invalid cue; otherwise left and right step the focus. Delete and Backspace put the selection away; Escape is Done. Placement looks are in 7f. Each placed Decoration has its rarity glow (7e: 1.2 x its width across by 96 tall) just behind it, in front of the Decoration behind it; the glow follows the Pond plant's sway, the Floating bob and the Hanging sway. The focus rings, the ghost and the "no" icon follow the Decoration's width. The placement ghost has no glow. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Treasure Chest (C11) | Opaque #fff1d6 backdrop (3g) that takes every press, and a white panel (panel style) inside the 16 px margins, padding 12. At the top left, the title "Treasure Chest" in bold ink 28 px, then 12 px below it the section heading in bold ink 18 px, the section name with its count ("Decorations: 2 of 14 found"; sections Decorations, Wearables, Dream colours), then 4 px below that the section line in ink 18 px (regular), wrapping at the list width, which says what the section's items are and where to use them ("Things to put in your tank. Open the tank's decorate mode to place them.", "Clothes for your axolotls. Each one gives a perk on trips. Dress up in Restyle.", "Magic paints from Paint shells. Not found in nature! Put one on in Restyle."), then 12 px below that the entry list. The heading and the section line are one heading block, as tall as the tallest heading, the 4 px gap and the tallest section line across all sections, so rows per page stay the same on every page; the line shows on every page of its section, with or without finds. When the full lines leave no room for one whole entry row, every section uses its short line instead ("Place them in decorate mode.", "Wear them in Restyle for perks.", "Magic paints. Use in Restyle."), chosen by fit: the short lines show at 460 x 320 and 320 x 440, the full lines at 800 x 600 and 1024 x 768. The chest has no other buttons. Landscape (canvas wider than tall): a right column as wide as the widest button, Close at its top and Back over Next at its bottom, 12 px apart; the page text is centred under the list. Portrait: the page text is centred above a bottom row of Back, Next and Close, wrapping when it must. Every catalogue treasure of every area has a row, found or not, in catalogue order within its section (the canal's first, then the pond's, Area 2's before Area 3's; Dream colours in the 3f order), so banking a treasure fills its silhouette in where it stands the next time the chest opens. Entry row: the 32 px lead (the treasure icon, or the Dream swatch tile for a Dream colour, over its 40 px rarity glow, 7e) at the left, then 8 px to the name in bold ink 20 px; the 24 px rarity badge, 6 px, and the rarity name in bold ink 18 px follow the name on its line (8 px after it) when they fit, else go under it; the detail line, ink 18 px, goes below, beside the lead or under it across the row, whichever ends higher. Details: a Wearable shows its slot name, then the 24 px perk icon (7b), 6 px, and its perk with its value as text (for example "Head", a middle dot separator, the icon and "Speed +4%"; Wearable display), on one line when it fits; otherwise the slot name takes its own line and the perk icon and text go under it, the text wrapping beside the icon; nothing shrinks or hides. A Dream colour shows the label "Dream colour - not found in nature" (the build text uses the PRD em dash, see Dream colour display); a Decoration has none. An unfound row: the lead is the 32 px unfound silhouette of its own icon, or the plain swatch silhouette for a Dream colour, each with its ink "?" (3g, 7e), and it has no glow; the name is "???"; the rarity badge and rarity name show as on a found row; the detail, ink 18 px, is the hint for its rarity ("Look near the main swim path!", "Look off the main path!", "Look in a cave or log, or past the current!", "Break a Dash pot with Tail Dash!"; a Paint shell's Dream colour uses its rarity's hint), then on its own line a Wearable's slot name (no perk) or a Dream colour's label. Rows are 12 px apart and all as tall as the tallest entry row, found or unfound (the unfound Dream colour row is the tallest, about 115 px at the smallest canvases, which hold one row a page); each page holds as many whole rows as fit (at least one). There is no empty text: a section with no finds lists its silhouettes. Pages run through the sections in order, each section at least one page, and wrap at both ends. The page text, ink 18 px, reads "Page 1 of 3". Back (left chevron), Close (close icon) and Next (right chevron) are secondary square tiles sharing one size, 78 x 64, wide enough for the widest label. Focus starts on Next; Tab and Shift+Tab move it over Back, Close and Next; left and right turn the page; Enter and Space press; Escape closes. Appears and leaves at once (9). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Collection Book (C12, C13, C14) | Opened from its hub tile. Same frame as the Treasure Chest: opaque #fff1d6 backdrop (3g) that takes every press, white panel, title "Collection Book" in bold ink 28 px, section heading in bold ink 18 px, page text in ink 18 px, and the same Back, Close and Next tiles (78 x 64), placement, focus and keys. Sections in order, each starting a new page, paging wrapping at both ends: Morphs (heading with its count, "Morphs: 2 of 8 found"), Dream colours (heading "Dream colours", no count), Fact cards ("Fact cards: 1 of 6 found"). It opens on the first Morphs page with Next focused; Close returns focus to the Collection Book hub tile. **Morph pages**: one morph per page, in the order Leucistic, Wild, Golden albino, White albino, Copper, Piebald, Axanthic, Melanoid. Found: the rest-pose portrait in its morph colours (ink scaled with the portrait, 4d), the morph name in bold ink 22 px, the 24 px morph badge 8 px after the name, and the fun fact in ink 18 px. Unfound: the silhouette (3g) in place of the portrait, the name "???", and the hint "Rescue me in the canal!"; no badge. **Featured layout** (morph and fact card pages): a square picture 112 px, else 64 px; first the text in a column beside the picture under the name row, else the picture and name row side by side with the text full width below. Picture gap 12, name-to-text gap 8. Text never shrinks; it is cut to whole lines only when nothing fits. **Title hide** (issue #75): the "Collection Book" title shows only while the entry fits under it; when a long text does not fit on the smallest canvases (the found plastic worm card at 320 x 440, 460 x 320 and 460 x 340; the found heron card at 320 x 440, 340 x 440, 460 x 320, 460 x 340, 480 x 320 and 480 x 340), the title hides, the section heading moves up to the title's place (same x, width and height), and the list grows upward by 47 px, and the entry is laid out in that taller list. The card's own name always shows. **Dream colours section**: all fourteen, apart from the morphs, in catalogue order, as many rows per page as fit, 12 px apart, all as tall as the tallest row. Row: the 32 px Dream swatch tile, 8 px, the name in bold ink 20 px, and under it the label "Dream colour - not found in nature" in ink 18 px (the build text uses the PRD em dash, see Dream colour display). **Fact cards section**: one card per page, the area cards in area-table order, then the refuge cards (issue #74): the tilapia card, the plastic worm card, the heron card, the "Axolotl refuges" card, the "Canal clean-ups" card, then the Boy or girl card. Tilapia found: the tilapia picture (its swim pose with a calm smile, ink scaled with it), the name "Tilapia", and the fact. Tilapia unfound: "???" and the hint "Explore the canal to find this card.", with no picture. Plastic worm found: the name "Plastic worm" and the fact, with no picture (only the tilapia has Book art today). Plastic worm unfound: "???" and the hint "Explore the lily pad pond to find this card.", with no picture. Heron found: the name "Heron" and the fact, with no picture. Heron unfound: "???" and the hint "Explore the lily pad pond to find this card.", with no picture. Axolotl refuges and Canal clean-ups found: the name and the fact, with no picture. Unfound: "???" and the card's own hint, "Visit the refuge to find this card." and "Beat an area, then visit the refuge to find this card.", with no picture. An unfound card's hint is its own hint when it has one, else it names the habitat of the first area that has it. The Boy or girl card is always found and has no picture: the name "Boy or girl?" at the top and the fact below it. Appears and leaves at once (9). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Character creator and restyle screen (PRD F1 to F3, F6, F16, F17) | One step screen per step, one choice per screen, the same screens for both. The 11 steps in order: Colour, Boy or girl, Eyes, Mouth, Gills, Spots, Tail, Body, Age, Size and Name. Opaque #fff1d6 backdrop (3g) that takes every press, and a white panel (panel style) inside the 16 px margins, padding 12. From the top: the heading in bold ink 28 px, centred ("Step 3 of 11: Pick your eyes!" in the creator; the step title, such as "Pick your eyes!", in restyle); 4 px under it the creator's progress dots or the restyle tab row; then the helper line (ink 18 px; where Surprise me shows it ends with one sentence that says what Surprise me picks on that step, such as "Surprise me picks new eyes.", PRD F1), the live preview and the step's picture buttons, or the Name step controls; and the step buttons at the bottom. **Progress dots** (creator): one 14 px slot per step, 6 px apart, centred. A done step is a filled ink dot r 4.5; the current step is a bigger ink ring r 5.75 (line 2.5) with a filled ink centre r 2.5; a step to come is a hollow ink ring r 4 (line 2). Shape and fill, never colour. **Tab row** (restyle): one tab per step, panel style, 48 px tall, each as wide as its bold 18 px label plus 6 px each side (at least 48), 12 px apart. The current tab's label is bold over a thick ink bar (5 px tall, 8 px in from each side, 4 px above the bottom edge); the other labels are normal weight. When the tabs do not fit across, they split into pages between two 48 x 48 secondary page buttons with the left and right chevron icons (32 px) and no label; the pages wrap, and the focus stays where it is. The Colour and Boy or girl tabs open read-only screens: one picture button showing the current value, chosen, with the helper "This stays the same after you make your axolotl."; picks do nothing. **Picture buttons**: every option of the step at once (up to 8), square, panel style, the largest whole side from 48 to 96 px that fits, in the fewest balanced rows, 12 px apart, each with its label (bold ink 18 px) 4 px under it. Each picture is a still of the look so far with that option on, in the morph's colours (on Colour, in that option's morph), at the still pose (9), cropped by step: the face for Eyes and Mouth, the gills for Gills, the body and tail for Spots, and the whole axolotl for every other step (11). Whole pictures draw at the option's size factor, so the Size options differ. The picture fills the button 4.5 px inside its edge. The chosen option has a 5 px ink ring just inside the outline and a tick badge: a white disc r 10 with an ink ring 2.6 and the 16 px tick icon, centred 13 px in from the top-right corner. On Colour, the 24 px morph badge sits 4 px inside the lower-left corner (badge shapes below). On Boy or girl, in the creator and on the restyle read-only Boy or girl screen, the boy or girl sign sits in that same 24 x 24 spot: a white disc r 12 with an ink ring 2.6, centred in the spot, holding the 18 px boy sign (a ring with an arrow leaving its upper right edge) or girl sign (a ring over a stem with a crossbar). In the 32 icon box, ink 3.5: the boy ring is centred (13,19) with r 7, its arrow runs to (26,6) with head legs to (19,6) and (26,13); the girl ring is centred (16,12) with r 7.5, its stem runs from (16,19.5) to (16,29) and its crossbar from (11,24.5) to (21,24.5). There are no arrows to step through options. **Name step**: the name box (192 x 48, the name box look below, the name in bold ink 20 px), the suggested-name buttons (panel style, 120 x 48 each, bold ink 20 px; names that fit the axolotl's boy or girl, as many to a page as fit, up to 8, 12 px apart), and More names (panel style, 132 x 48, bold ink 20 px) when the names take more than one page: beside the name box when both fit across, else after the names. A chosen name button has the 5 px ring and the tick badge, centred 2 px in from its top-right corner so it overhangs the corner and stays clear of the name. Pressing the name box, or Enter on it, opens the name entry field. **Name box look** (it must read as a place to type, not a button): fill #f6f3fd, a 3 px #c9c2e0 inset band just inside the outline along the top and left edges only, ink outline 3.5 (`shape`), corner radius 8 (buttons and panels use 16 to 18). Inside, from the left: 12 px padding, the text room (144 px: 12 name characters at 0.6 em of bold 20 px), a 4 px gap, the 20 px pencil icon, then 12 px padding. The name is left-aligned in the text room and centred down the box. When the name is empty the box shows the placeholder "Type a name" in #6e6790, normal weight, 20 px. The pencil, in the 32 icon box, is an outline only, ink 2.6: tip at the lower left (7,25), eraser end at the upper right (25,7), half width 3.4, a cone 6 long from the tip with a shoulder line, and an eraser band line 5 from the end. While the name box has the focus and the name entry field is closed, an ink text cursor 2.5 x 24, centred down the box, sits at the end of the name (at the start of the text room when the name is empty); it blinks 0.5 s on and 0.5 s off, and stays steady under reduced motion (9). **Step buttons**, label-sized (Button width, but without the 200 px wide-button minimum): in the creator, Back (secondary square tile 65 x 64, left chevron; from step 2, its place kept on step 1 so nothing moves), Surprise me (secondary tile 158 x 64, Surprise icon), and Next (primary wide 110 x 64, right chevron), or Done (primary wide 110 x 64, tick icon) on the last step, in centred rows at the bottom. In restyle, Surprise me, Cancel (secondary tile 92 x 64, close icon) and Done on every step, except that Surprise me is hidden on the Colour and Boy or girl tabs (and on the restyle-only Dream colour and Outfit tabs below), with its place kept so Cancel and Done never move: in centred rows at the bottom in portrait, or in landscape (canvas wider than tall) stacked 12 px apart in a right column under the tab row, with Done at the bottom. **Preview**: sized for a Large axolotl in the 166 x 142 texture frame (11): 1 CSS px per local unit (166 x 142 px) times the look's size factor, in the morph's colours. It sits above the picture buttons or beside them, 4 px away, whichever fits. **Fit**: 12 px between touch targets, 4 px between text (or the dots or the preview) and anything. Touch targets stay at least 48 x 48 and primary buttons at least 64 tall; text never shrinks. When a step does not fit, in order: 1. the helper line hides; 2. the picture button labels hide, and one caption (bold ink 18 px) under the buttons names the focused option, else the chosen one; 3. the preview shrinks to 0.6 (99.6 x 85.2 px); 4. the creator heading drops its "Step N of 11:" start; 5. the preview hides, and the chosen picture button is the preview; 6. restyle only: the heading hides, and the bold current tab names the step. Restyle then tries Cancel at the end of the tab row. Every step fits the smallest canvases (1); below them the last try may overflow. **Focus and keys**: focus starts on the chosen option (on Name, the chosen name, else the name box) and always shows the double focus ring outside the focused control (inner white 3.25 px and outer ink 6.25 px outside the box, 3 px lines). Tab and Shift+Tab move one stop at a time, wrapping. Creator order: the picture buttons, Back (not on step 1), Surprise me, then Next or Done. Restyle order: the tabs, the picture buttons, Surprise me (only where it shows), Cancel, Done. On Name the picture buttons are replaced by the name box, the name buttons and More names. Arrows move in the picture buttons (left and right by one, up and down by a row, stopping at the ends); left and right on a focused tab open the tab before or after, wrapping. Enter and Space press, ignoring key repeat; Escape is Cancel in restyle and does nothing in the creator. Every change shows in the preview and on the picture buttons at once. Surprise me on the creator's Colour step picks a whole new axolotl: the colour, the boy or girl, every look option and a suggested name that fits the boy or girl. On every other step it changes only that step's choice, always to a different one; in restyle it never changes the colour or the boy or girl. Appears and leaves at once (9). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Restyle Dream colour tab (PRD F6, F14, F15) | A restyle-only step screen, tab "Dream colour" right after the Colour tab, heading "Pick a Dream colour!". The axolotl choices card's Dream colour (above; shown only when at least one Dream colour is owned) opens restyle straight on this tab, with the chosen option focused. The same step screen as above, with these changes. **Options**, in order: "Real colour" first, then each owned Dream colour in catalogue order (3f), up to 15 in all ("Real colour" and the 14 Dream colours). Each picture button shows the whole axolotl (11) in that colour, at the still pose (9): "Real colour" in its real morph, every other one in that Dream colour with its pattern and Dream marker (6p). The lower-left badge spot (24 x 24, 4 px inside the corner, as the Colour step's morph badge) holds the Dream swatch tile at 24 px (Dream colour display, scaled from 32), or the real morph's badge on "Real colour". **Labels** always show under the picture buttons (bold ink 18 px, 4 px under), so a solid Dream colour is always named beside its swatch (F15); fit step 2 never applies here. **Helper**: "Tap a picture to try it on. Dream colours are not found in nature." when at least one Dream colour is owned; "Find Paint shells in the canal to get Dream colours!" when none is, and then only "Real colour" shows. **Paging and fit**: at most 8 picture buttons to a page, "Real colour" first on page 1. When a page still does not fit after fit steps 1 and 3 to 6 and Cancel in the tab row, the tab shows fewer picture buttons per page, one fewer at a time, down to 1. Measured with all 15 options: 8 per page at 844 x 390 and 1280 x 800, 6 at 375 x 667 (all 8 show when there are exactly 8), and 2 at 460 x 320 and 320 x 440, where 3 or more options give 2 per page (section 12). **More colours**: shown only when the options take more than one page. Panel style, sized like More names: label-sized for "More colours" in bold ink 20 px (156 x 48), 48 px tall, centred under the picture buttons, 4 px under their labels (Fit spacing above). Each press shows the next page, wrapping to page 1, and keeps the focus on More colours. A step change opens the page that holds the chosen option. **Focus and keys**: Tab order is the tabs, the picture buttons, More colours (when shown), Cancel, Done. Arrows move in the picture buttons of the page. Surprise me is hidden here, with its place kept so Cancel and Done never move, and never changes the Dream colour. Done keeps the chosen Dream colour; Cancel and Escape leave without keeping it. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Name entry field | A real text field over the canvas (browser glue, [ADR 0011](adr/0011-name-entry-stays-on-device.md)), placed over the Name step's name box and kept 16 px inside the visible viewport; until it has that box, it sits centred at the top of the visible viewport, 420 wide. At least 56 px tall. It matches the name box look (above), except for its size (section 12, recorded residual): fill #f6f3fd with the same 3 px #c9c2e0 inset along the top and left (an inset shadow), ink text in bold 22 px in the rounded UI font stack (never a webfont; 16 px or more, so phones do not zoom in), ink border 3.5 px, corner radius 8, 12 px padding across, the placeholder "Type a name" in #6e6790, normal weight, in the field's font and size, and the double focus ring always on (3 px white, then 3 px ink, outside the border). The caret is ink. Where the browser supports the CSS `caret-animation` property, the caret blinks 0.5 s on and 0.5 s off, and stays steady under reduced motion; elsewhere the browser's own caret shows (section 12, recorded residual). Holds only name characters, through `fitNameEdit`, the same rule as the cleaning (a letter with its accents is one character). Anything a name cannot hold is dropped as it is typed or pasted, with no extra cue. Space runs become one space and a leading space is dropped. A trailing space stays while typing and is trimmed when the name is kept. Typing past 12 does nothing, and a longer paste keeps the start that fits, with the caret after the kept text. During an IME composition the field is left alone; the rule applies when the composition ends, on Enter, or when the field loses focus. The browser `maxlength` is only a raw ceiling (`NAME_FIELD_RAW_MAX`), never the name limit. The name box shows the cleaned name as it is typed. Enter or tapping elsewhere keeps the name; Escape puts back the name from before typing; a name that cleans to nothing keeps the old name. | `derived (provisional)` |
| First-trip hint banner | Soft pink #ffd9e6 panel (panel style), padding 12, placed at a screen edge per HUD overlay fit, after the find toast and the snack banner, ink text 24 px, or 20 px when no place has room at 24 px (HUD overlay fit): "Bring finds to the Home Spot to keep them safe." No icon needed: the text carries it. | `derived (provisional)` |
| HUD overlay fit (find toast, snack banner, first-trip hint) | Keep-outs: the Pause button; the Go home button while it shows; the Dash button with its ring; the swim pad base plus the knob reach. Clearance is at least 12 px from the keep-outs and from other overlays, and the 16 px edge margin holds. The middle of the play area is the full width inside the margins, from 30% to 70% of the way down the water as drawn on screen (from the water line to the sea bed, not the canvas height). The swim camera never scrolls up or down in a one-screen area (the canal), so each canvas size has fixed water rows there. A taller area scrolls vertically: the HUD measures the water band on screen (PRD X10) and re-places the overlays when the band moves 8 CSS px or more, or every 100 ms while it has moved less. Inside it an overlay may cover only the snack counter, legs display and Trip Bag panels themselves, never the open water beside or between them, in every place below. Overlays are placed in priority order find toast, snack banner, first-trip hint, each in the first of these places with room: 1. the top edge, at least 12 px above the middle and clear of the snack counter, legs display and Trip Bag, centred with its top at the margin (or as near as it fits); 2. the bottom edge, at least 12 px below the middle and clear of those panels, centred with its bottom at the margin; 3. over the Trip Bag panel only; 4. over the status panels (snack counter, legs display and Trip Bag), from the top-left. Places 3 and 4 never cover a control, and inside the middle they cover only the panels themselves. Text wraps narrower (toast name 120 to 360 px, toast line 2 at least 90 px, banner text at least 120 px). The find toast never shrinks (toast name 20, toast line 2 18, badge 24, icon or portrait 32). A banner (the snack banner or the first-trip hint) is tried in every place at 24 px first; only when no place has room at 24 px is it tried in every place again at 20 px (still above the 18 px body minimum), so 24 px wins wherever it fits. It never goes under 20 px. An overlay with no room is held: hidden, with its timer stopped, until it fits. No UI scale-down. Measured, each overlay alone: each toast always shows on every supported canvas at least 375 px tall or at least 520 px wide, and each banner shows on every supported canvas. Banners need 20 px at 460 wide and 320 to 360 tall (the snack banner always, the hint while Go home shows) and at 480 wide and 320 to 360 tall (both, while Go home shows). Only the "Tie-dye galaxy Paint shell" toast waits, at 460 x 320 and 460 x 340 with Go home showing (this does not happen in play: the Trip Bag banks and the toast clears; section 12). With two or three overlays up, a lower-priority banner uses 20 px more often on narrow or short canvases. At 460 x 360, 480 x 320 to 360, 320 x 440, 390 x 440, 667 x 375, 844 x 360 and 1024 x 360 nothing waits. The toast sits over the status panels at the top-left, above the middle, at 460 x 320; over the panels from y 79.2 at 320 x 440; and at the top edge at 800 x 600 and 1024 x 768. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Rarity badge shapes** (24 x 24 box, origin top-left; scale up by 32 / 24 for the 32 px badge)

| Rarity | Shape | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Common | Circle, r 10.5 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Rare | Rounded diamond: a square of side 15.5 rotated 45 degrees, corner radius 2.5 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Epic | Rounded shield: flat top at y 2 from x 3 to 21 with corner radius 4, straight sides to y 12, curving to a rounded point at (12,22.5) with tip radius 2.5 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Legendary | Rounded five-point star: outer radius 11.5, inner radius 5.5, tip radius 2, one point up | `derived (provisional)` |

**Morph badge shapes** (24 x 24 box, origin top-left, centre (12,12); scale up by 32 / 24 for the 32 px badge; the 2.6 outline never scales)

| Morph | Shape | Fill | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Leucistic | Rounded triangle, one point up: corners (12,2.5), (22,20.5), (2,20.5), corner radius 3 | #ffb7cf | `derived (provisional)` |
| Wild | Flat-top hexagon: circumradius 10.5 about the centre, a point at the left and right, flat top and bottom edges, corner radius 2.5 | #b0b86e | `derived (provisional)` |
| Golden albino | Flower: six round petals (r 4.6, centres 6.2 from the centre, one petal up) drawn as one outline with notches where the petals meet, no line inside | #ffd36b | `derived (provisional)` |
| White albino | Cloud: a left bump r 5 at (7,15), a top bump r 6.5 at (12,10.5), and a right bump r 5 at (17,15), over a flat base at y 20 from x 7 to 17. Shine at (9.5,8.0), 2.2 x 0.9, rotated -45 degrees | #fff4f6 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Copper | Leaf: a lens with tips at (3.5,20.5) and (20.5,3.5), both sides bulging 5, tip fillet r 1. Shine at (10.6,10.6), 2.8 x 1.0, rotated -45 degrees | #fac595 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Piebald | Capsule: a rounded rectangle from (2,6.5) to (22,17.5), corner radius 5.5. Shine at (9.0,9.6), 3.0 x 1.0, not rotated | #f8eff1 | `derived (provisional)` |
| Axanthic | Drop: a circle r 7.5 at (12,14.5) with a tip at (12,2.5), tip rounded r 1.5. Shine at (8.3,11.9), 2.2 x 0.9, rotated -0.96 rad | #74767f | `derived (provisional)` |
| Melanoid | Crescent moon: a circle r 10.5 at (12,12) minus a circle r 8.5 at (16,9), opening to the upper right, both tips rounded (fillet r 1) | #4f4a6e | `derived (provisional)` |

Never a circle, diamond, shield, or star (the rarity shapes), and never the Dream swatch tile. Each fill is the body colour of its morph; the shape and the morph name carry the meaning. Source: `derived (provisional)`.

---

## 9. Motion feel

Everything is soft, slow, and looping. Times are periods (one full cycle).

| Motion | Period | Amplitude / detail | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Gill flutter | 0.8 s | Gills +/- 0.13 rad; filaments +/- 0.22 rad; each gill has its own phase offset | `cartoon.js` |
| Tail wave | 1.2 s | Travelling wave from body to tip, amplitude 11 x s (s = 0 at root, 1 at tip) | `cartoon.js` |
| Legs | 1.2 s | +/- 0.35 rad; diagonal pairs move in opposite phase | `cartoon.js` |
| Bob | 3 s | +/- 15 px vertical | `cartoon.js` |
| Tilt | 3 s | +/- 0.12 rad; uses cosine, so it is a quarter cycle off the bob | `cartoon.js` |
| Swim lap | 16 s | +/- 110 px horizontal around x 240 (y centre 210); facing flips instantly at each end | `cartoon.js` |
| Blink | Every 4.6 s | Lasts 0.14 s; eye squashed to 12% height | `cartoon.js` |
| Willows | 4 s | Sway +/- 2.2 px | `cartoon.js` |
| Reeds | 4 s | Sway amplitude 4 to 7.5 px (varies per reed) | `cartoon.js` |
| Light rays | 8 s drift, 6 s fade | Drift +/- 6 px; alpha cycles 0.12 +/- 0.05 | `cartoon.js` |
| Clouds | 24 s | Drift +/- 6 px | `cartoon.js` |
| Sun rays | About 25 s per turn | Rotate 0.25 rad/s | `cartoon.js` |
| Water surface | Continuous | y = 80 + 1.6 sin(0.045x + 1.6t) + 0.8 sin(0.11x - 1.1t) | `cartoon.js` |
| Bubbles | New one every 0.7 s | Spawn at the mouth as it is drawn (following tilt and turn); rise 40 px/s; sideways wobble +/- 6 px over 1.3 s; radius 2.6 to 5.6; at most 10 on screen; vanish at the surface | `cartoon.js` |
| Lily pads | Follow the wave | Tilt to match the local surface slope | `cartoon.js` |
| Frame step | - | Clamp dt to 50 ms so a slow frame never causes a jump | `cartoon.js` |
| Reduced motion | - | Show one still frame (time t = 1.0 s) | `cartoon.js` |

**MVP motion.** Every row has its reduced-motion (RM) still. Source for all rows: `derived (provisional)`.

| Motion | Period and detail | RM still |
|---|---|---|
| Tilapia swim | None in the art: each pose is one still frame (7c), with no tail swing, fin flap or bob. Only its position moves (patrol 45 px/s, tuning). | Same. Position still moves for gameplay. |
| Telegraph | The wind-up pose and the "!" bubble show at once, at full size, and hold. The glint grows from scale 0.6 to 1.0 across the whole wind-up. | Pose, glint, and bubble at full size at once. |
| Lunge | The pose swaps to the lunge frame at once and holds for the lunge (up to 0.4 s, tuning). No streaks. | Same. |
| Cooldown | The rest frame, facing away from the axolotl, for the cooldown (2.5 s, tuning). No tail motion. | Same. |
| Leg-loss puff | 0.6 s. The puff grows from 0.7 to 1.0 of its size (scale 0.8) over the first 0.36 s. Its alpha holds at 1 for the first 0.2 s, then goes 1 to 0 over the last 0.4 s (0.2 to 0.6 s), so the growth and the fade overlap from 0.2 to 0.36 s. Its twinkles are part of the puff, so they grow and fade with it. | Puff at scale 0.8 and alpha 1.0 for 0.6 s, then gone. |
| Regrowth | The leg grows with progress (20 s per leg, tuning; snacks speed it up), in steps of 0.1. The twinkles by the toes stay in place and pulse in size, 0.6 to 1.0, on the swim cycle (1.2 s at full paddle). At full, the regrown burst: three yellow eight-arm Tail Dash sparkles start 12 px from the leg root, 120 degrees apart, and rise 10 px while alpha goes 1 to 0, over 0.7 s, once. | The twinkles hold still (the swim cycle stops). The burst is three still sparkles 12 px from the root at full alpha for 0.7 s, then gone. |
| Snack speed-up | Star: grows 0.6 to 1.0 and rises 8 px over 0.3 s, holds, then fades over the last 0.4 s of its 1.2 s life. Badge: holds 1.2 s, then fades 0.4 s. A new boost before the fade extends the hold. During the fade it starts a fresh star. Pause freezes both. | Star at full size and alpha 1.0 for 1.2 s, then gone. Badge hides at once after its hold. |
| Protected ring | Static: the glint never moves and the ring alpha is constant. It follows the axolotl and turns with its tilt and the leg-count wobble. | Same. |
| Leg-count wobble (A9) | Extra tilt while swimming: a sine of period 0.9 s with a peak of 0.06 rad per lost leg. 0 with four legs, +/-0.06 with three, +/-0.12 with two, +/-0.18 with one leg left (three lost). | None. The slowdown stays. |
| Queasy wobble (6j, N3) | Extra tilt while queasy after a plastic worm: a sine of period 0.6 s with a peak of 0.08 rad, for the whole queasy time (3 s, tuning). The cheek tint (6j) shows the whole time and follows the axolotl's tilt, turn and squash. 0 once it ends. | The tint shows; no wobble. The slowdown stays (0.6 of the top speed, tuning). |

When the focus moves to a marker not fully in view, the map scrolls to bring it in, easing out over 0.3 s; a new move starts from where the scroll is. A drag scrolls 1:1 with no fling. Every axolotl on screen poses every frame, at the full frame rate: the rig moves, turns and swaps its parts and bends the tail, with no texture repaints. ## 10. Accessibility

Target: WCAG 2.1 AA. Values below are `derived (provisional)` unless noted.

**Motion and safety**

| Rule | Detail | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Reduced motion | When the player prefers reduced motion, show still poses (the prototype freezes at t = 1.0 s). | `cartoon.js` |
| Flashing | No flashing. There is no invulnerability flash; protected time is the steady ring (6q). Allowed: the GFP halo pulse (2.4 s or slower; GFP is not in the MVP), the rarity glow breathe on a revealed canal treasure (2.4 s, alpha factor 0.6 to 1.0, static under reduced motion, 9), the firefly glow breathe in the dusk pond (3 s, alpha 0.6 to 1.0, each firefly out of step, still at alpha 1 under reduced motion, 7b, 9), one-time fades of 0.4 s or longer (the leg-loss puff's 0.4 s fade, the regrown burst, crumble, hint sparkle, the heron shadow's 0.4 s fades and its 0.4 s darkening, 7h), and small moving highlights at constant brightness (Iridescent sheen). The protected ring and its glint are static. One exception blinks: the name box text cursor (8, 9), a thin ink bar shown 0.5 s and hidden 0.5 s, one flash a second, far under the limit of 3 flashes a second, and steady under reduced motion. Nothing else blinks. No screen shake (PRD X4). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Hazards | Challenge hazards are signalled by shape and motion, not by hue. The tilapia telegraph is the white cross glint, the "!" bubble, and the coiled wind-up pose (7c), not hue, and it holds still under reduced motion. The plastic worm (issue #75) is a straight stiff rod with a white glint and no rings, set apart from the wavy snack worm by shape and by being darker in greyscale (7b), not by its neon hue. Being queasy is told by the text banner "Bleh! That was plastic, not food." and, with motion allowed, the wobble (6j, 9); the green cheek tint is colour only and never the sole cue. It is gentle, never frightening (ADR 0007). The heron (issue #98) warns by its shadow holding still at the strike spot and turning darker, a value change that reads in greyscale and holds under reduced motion (the shadow switches tone at once), never by hue; its strike is a closed beak with no grab, and it has no "!" bubble (7h). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Regeneration | Never gory. Leg loss is a white puff, and the lost leg is a faint dashed outline. A small whole leg grows back inside it, with twinkles by the toes (6q). | `derived (provisional)` |

**Contrast: the pair rule.** On any background, every cue object must get at least 3:1 from its ink outline or from its edge partner. Haze is never applied to the hero.

**Cue objects.** The axolotl, the stranded axolotl (waiting or following, never hazed) and its lost thought bubble, tilapia, protected ring, hint sparkle, telegraph glint and "!" bubble, Dash-needed bubble, Dream marker, leg-loss puff and twinkles, Dash pot, plastic worm, Paint shell, Home Spot (arch, flag, safe-ring dots), Area exit gate and sign, Area stars, the last-snack hint star, revealed treasure icons (depth 28, including revealed Paint shells), the heron shadow (both tones) and the heron beak dip (7h), and the Home Tank focus ring. A hidden treasure icon (depth 9, under reeds, pads, the cave front, or past the current) is deliberately hidden and is not a cue object while hidden; its cue object is the hint star. Once revealed, it is a cue object and is never hazed. Its rarity glow (7e) is not part of the cue object: it is a soft fill behind the icon, which keeps its opaque ink and 9:1 outer fills, so it counts as background. Prototype scenery (sky, bank, water, light rays, bed, pebbles, reeds, pads, bubbles), the small cave (rocks and interior), the pond's hollow log (bark, end face and interior), the pond's water, bed, edge reeds and stream (3c, 7a), the dusk pond's water, bed, edge reeds, sky and air wash (3c, 7a), the root arch (roots, soil mat and hollow) and the fireflies (7b), the past-the-current marker, and Home Tank decorations are background. The queasy cheek tint is not a cue object: it is a soft fill with no ink, over the cheeks of the hero (6j). The lost-leg ghost (6q) is faint on purpose; the legs display carries leg state. The carried-home bubble (6q) is not a cue object: it shows only while the axolotl is carried home, and that phase takes no input, so there is nothing to find or dodge; the trip-end card (the tank arrival card) then says what happened in text.

**Edge partner.** The opaque fill just inside the ink along the outer silhouette, meaning the body (merged silhouette) and the fins on the outer edge. NOT edge partners: depth details behind the body (far legs, far gills, tilapia far fin), tube details (gills, legs, Home Spot post), and wearables. Pattern overlays never touch the edge on bodies that pass alone (6p). On a rim-lit body the rim light is the edge partner along the upper silhouette, top fin edge included. The lower edge is not required to pass: light comes from above, and a full-outline rim would read as a second outline colour (section 2). See section 12.

**Method: check the pair, not the background.** Ink and partner are opaque, so their ratio P decides. On any background the better of the two is at least the square root of P:

- a background darker or lighter than both is beaten by the far colour by more than P;
- a background between them splits P into two ratios that multiply to P.

The worst background is the middle one and gets exactly the square root of P.

- A pair passes on every background (every water state, every gradient point, haze, light rays, Home Tank glass, scenery) if and only if P is 9:1 or more. No samples are needed.
- Ink #2b2350 has relative luminance 0.0229, so a 9:1 partner needs 0.607 or more.
- The clean gradient's luminance runs from 0.100 (#0a5c98) to 0.624 (#55e4d8), so for pairs from about 4.2:1 to 85:1 the worst case happens in the canal (t is 0 at the surface, 1 at y 330).
- **The pond is held to a floor instead** (issue #75). Every pond background colour has luminance 0.1687 or more (3c), so ink is 3:1 or better against all of them. A cue object on a pond background therefore keeps 3:1 from its ink outline alone, whatever its fill, and the pair method is a sufficient test there, not a necessary one. This is why the plastic worm (fills 4.48:1 and 4.25:1 with ink) is a cue object, and why Leucistic (8.86:1, 2.98:1 worst case in the canal) reaches 3:1 in the pond. The floor does not apply to the canal: its deep water and bed bottom sit where both ink and a light fill fall under 3:1.
- **The pond at dusk keeps the floor** (issue #98, 3c dusk contrast rule): every dusk background colour, the water, haze, bed, mud dots, edge reeds, stream and the sky seen through the air wash, has luminance 0.1687 or more. Its recorded exceptions are off the water (the bank soil under the wash, 0.1603) or dark on purpose (the root arch's hollow and its mat dots); what sits on the hollow passes by the pair method, not the floor. The heron shadow is held by the floor: its fill is see-through, so its opaque ink outline alone carries its edge on every pond and dusk background, except at the raised wing tip over the bank soil (2.88:1, 7h).

**Alpha.** While a cue object is on screen, its ink outline and edge partner stay at alpha 1.0. Fading either mixes it with the background and breaks the guarantee. Examples: a hint star at alpha 0.7 drops to 2.63:1 on clean water; with an opaque outline and a 0.7 white fill it still drops to 2.88:1 on some backgrounds. Only one-time fades of 0.4 s or longer (Flashing row) may lower alpha, on the way in or out. Fills inside the silhouette (patterns, spots, shines, glow) may use alpha.

Golden albino and GFP pass on any background. Melanoid passes with its rim light. Wild passes with the Wild rim: its body alone fails (2.61:1), but the rim #e2e4b8 along the upper outline gives 3.31:1 or better on any background, and its fin #cdd398 (9.16:1 with ink, 3.03:1 worst case) passes alone, so the rim stops where the fin band starts. As with the Melanoid rim, the lower body edge has no 9:1 partner (section 12). White albino, Copper, and Piebald pass on any background with their bodies alone (13.38, 9.24, and 12.75:1), and their speckles, glints, and patches stay 2 units inside the edge (6h). Axanthic passes with its rim: its body alone fails (1.78:1 worst case), but the rim #e4e6eb along the upper outline gives 3.40:1 or better on any background, and its fin #cfd1d8 (9.43:1 with ink, 3.07:1 worst case) passes alone, so the rim stops where the fin band starts. All of these use the pair ratio and its square root per the method above, with no water samples. Leucistic is 0.02 short (2.98:1) and is a recorded residual (section 12).

Under reduced motion the shadow does not glide, so the warning is told by the darker tone and its move onto the strike spot. | `derived (provisional)` |

None of these is colour. | `derived (provisional)` |

A perk type at its cap carries the "max" pill, a shape plus the word. With no perk worn the text "No perks yet" says so. Perks are never shown by colour alone (PRD F10). | `derived (provisional)` |

Go!, Change outfit and Back differ by icon and label (play, Shirt, left chevron); Go! also differs by shape, a wide button against a square tile, and by being focused. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Socks and Feet on legs | A leg that is present wears the Sock and Shoe; a lost or part-grown leg is the plain dashed ghost or growing fill with no Sock or Shoe (6q). The shape differs, so no hue is needed. | `derived (provisional)` |

Ink outlines are opaque. The card also says each star in a text line and the best as text ("Area 1: 2 of 3 stars"). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Area map (N2) | **Greyscale cues**: locked by shape, the padlock badge (a white disc with the ink lock icon) on the marker, never by fill alone (#ffe14a and #ffffff are only 1.30:1 apart; the unlocked pond fill #4fd462 and white are only 1.92:1 apart, so the badge carries it there too); the pond marker is told from the canal's disc by its shape, a lily pad with a wedge notch in its lower rim (at least 5% of the disc's area); a locked marker still shows its number, and a press on it says "Beat Area N to unlock!" as text. Stars as the Area stars: earned filled, missing dashed. "New!" by its word, in a pill. Areas by number and habitats by their labels, as text. Focus by the double ring. **Keys**: Tab and Shift+Tab move through the markers in area order (locked ones too, so their message can be reached), then Back, wrapping. The arrows move along the path: right and down to the next area, left and up to the one before, stopping at the ends; from Back, right and down go to the first marker and left and up to the last. Enter and Space press, ignoring key repeat. Escape is Back, on key repeat too, as on the explorer card. **Targets**: each marker's tap target is its 64 px square, and neighbouring targets are at least 32 px apart along the path; Back is 200 x 64. **Contrast**: ink text on #ffe14a 11.05:1 and on #ffffff 14.39:1 (number, title, habitat labels; the lock icon on its white disc); the message on #ffd9e6 11.19:1; "New!" on #ff82bf 6.30:1. Against the backdrop #0c6a8c the marker fills are 4.66:1 (#ffe14a), 6.08:1 (#ffffff) and, for the unlocked pond marker, 3.16:1 (#4fd462), so each marker edge passes 3:1 by its fill (ink on #0c6a8c is only 2.37:1), and the focus ring's white line is 6.08:1. The path (#55e4d8, 3.90:1 on the backdrop) is decorative: the numbers carry the order. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Snack counter and last-snack star | Snack icon plus "N left" as text. The star shape (R 7 Common hint star) on each of the last 3 snacks, static under reduced motion. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Treasure | Icon shape (each of the thirty-four items and the Paint shell has its own outline) plus the name as text. Its rarity glow adds colour only. Found and unfound: an unfound treasure is the flat silhouette of its own outline with an ink "?" and the name "???" (Treasure Chest). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Find toast | Badge shape plus the rarity name as text ("<Rarity> <Category>"), with the item icon and name | `derived (provisional)` |
| Trip Bag | Bag icon, the number, and the "not safe yet" text | `derived (provisional)` |
| Banking card choices | Play icon plus "Keep exploring"; house icon plus "Go home". Primary and secondary differ by icon and label, not only fill. | `derived (provisional)` |
| First-find card choices | Paint shell card: play icon plus "Try it now!"; close icon plus "Maybe later". Primary and secondary differ by icon and label, not only fill. The other cards: tick icon plus "OK". The title names the kind found as text. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Treasure Chest | Each entry: the item icon or the Dream swatch tile pattern, the name as text, and the rarity badge shape plus the rarity name. Dream colours also carry the "Dream colour - not found in nature" label. Each section heading has its count as text ("2 of 8 found") and its section line as text, and the page text says "Page 1 of 3". Found vs unfound is never told apart by colour alone: an unfound entry shows its silhouette with the ink "?", the name "???" and its hint as text, and has no glow; a found entry shows its full-colour icon or swatch, its name, and its glow. Both show the badge shape and the rarity name. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Collection Book found vs unfound | Unfound: the flat silhouette with its "?", the name "???", and the hint text. Found: the portrait, the morph name, and the badge shape. Unfound fact cards show "???" and the hint with no picture. The Morphs and Fact cards headings carry their counts as text. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Home Tank hub buttons | Each button has its own icon and label (play icon plus "Go exploring", sprig plus "Decorate", chest plus "Treasure Chest", book plus "Collection Book", the notched lily pad over a wave plus "Refuge"). The primary also differs by shape: a wide button against square tiles, not only fill. Where full size does not fit, Decorate, Treasure Chest, Collection Book and Refuge become 48 x 48 icon-only tiles (8), still told apart by their icons, and a focus caption names the focused tile in text (decorate mode does the same for Put away); Go exploring keeps its label. Targets never go below 48 x 48, and primary actions stay 64 tall (PRD X5). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Home Tank axolotl picking | The focused axolotl has the double focus ring and a name tag with its name as text. Picking it opens the axolotl choices card, titled with its name as text. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Axolotl choices card | Restyle, Outfit, Dream colour and Back differ by icon and label (pencil, Shirt, Paint drop, left chevron), not by fill: all four are secondary tiles. The Paint drop is a leaning outline with a droplet and a splash line, never the closed filled drop of the Axanthic morph badge, a rarity shape or the Dream swatch tile. Focus is the double ring. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Character creator | Every option is a picture button with its name as a label under it, or the caption once the labels hide (8), and the heading names the step as text ("Step 3 of 11: Pick your eyes!"). The chosen option is marked by shape, not colour: a thick 5 px ink ring inside the outline plus the tick badge (white disc, ink ring, tick). The progress dots show done, current and to come by shape and fill: a filled dot, a bigger ring with a filled centre, and a hollow ring. On Colour each picture also has its morph badge shape (8) and the morph name. On Boy or girl the pictures show the shape cues (eyelashes, the cheek heart and a rounder body and belly; a longer tail and a taller, longer fin, 6u), the boy or girl sign on its white disc (a circle with an arrow, or a circle with a cross, 8) and the labels "Boy" and "Girl", so Boy and Girl are never told apart by colour alone. Mouths differ by shape (fill, a "w", a one-sided line, a tongue, or the size of the ink box, 6l). Spots differ by count, size and placement (6n), and Bodies by depth (6s). Tail, Body, Age and Size read by silhouette and proportion, with their labels. The name box reads as a place to type by shape and text, not colour: the inset top and left edge, the smaller corner radius, the pencil icon, the "Type a name" placeholder and the text cursor (8). Each step button has its own icon and label (left chevron plus "Back", Surprise icon plus "Surprise me", right chevron plus "Next", tick plus "Done"); Next and Done also differ by shape, a wide button against square tiles. Focus is the double ring. A Girl's eyelashes read on every morph: ink over a white under-stroke (the Girl eyelashes pair in the table above). | `derived (provisional)` |
| Restyle screen | As the character creator, with the tab row in place of the progress dots and Back and Next. The current tab is marked by a bold label and a thick ink bar, not by colour; every tab has its step name as text. The tab page buttons are chevron shapes. The Cancel tile has the close icon plus "Cancel". The read-only Colour and Boy or girl screens show one chosen picture with its label (and, on Colour, the morph badge shape; on Boy or girl, the boy or girl sign), plus the helper text. When the heading hides (8, fit step 6), the bold current tab names the step. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Decoration placement states | Selected: the double focus ring. Valid move: the half-alpha ghost inside the ring. Invalid move: the dashed ink outline plus the "no" icon (disc and bar). Each state adds a shape, never only a colour. The ring, the outline and the "no" icon go round the Decoration's own footprint, its width by 80, for every placement kind. Up and Down on a Floating or Hanging Decoration do nothing and show no state, so no cue is shown for a move that cannot happen. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Refuge sections (7i) | Dirty against clean by shape: trash batches and murk blotches with bare banks or bare pads, against plants, light shafts and resting axolotls. The three trash kinds by outline, never by fill: a box with a rim ellipse (cans), a long body with shoulders, a neck and a cap (bottles), a squat sack with handle loops (bags). A hidden section by the ink "?" on one flat tone, with the name "???" and the hint text; the "More to come" teaser by its dashed outline against the solid frame of every picture and the silhouette, and by its label. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Refuge nudge badge (8) | Shape plus ink: a disc with an ink ring and a solid ink 4-point star on the Refuge tile's corner, unlike the plus badge, the padlock badge and the "New!" pill. The pink is the weakest cue. | `derived (provisional)` |
| Refuge screen and milestone card (8) | Every control has its icon and its label: left chevron plus "Back", left chevron plus "Previous", right chevron plus "Next", play icon plus "See it again", play icon plus "Keep exploring". Focus is the double focus ring of the focused button, never colour alone. Section names and hints are text. | `derived (provisional)` |

**Checklist**

| Item | Rule | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Text contrast | 4.5:1 for text under 18 pt (14 pt bold); 3:1 for larger text and for icons | `derived (provisional)` |
| Focus | Every interactive element has a visible double-ring focus indicator (section 8) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Touch targets | At least 48 x 48 CSS px; primary at least 64 px tall | `derived (provisional)` |
| Non-colour meaning | Morph, water state, hazard, disabled state, rarity, legs, telegraph, protected time, Dream colours, perks, the Home Spot, finds, the Trip Bag, and the creator and restyle chosen option, progress dots, current tab and boy or girl all have a shape, text, or motion cue (see the greyscale check above) | `derived (provisional)` |
| Themes | There is one theme today. If more are added, re-check every pair above. | `derived (provisional)` |

## 11. Asset production notes

Engine-agnostic. Do not commit to an engine or file format yet. All items `derived (provisional)`.

| Topic | Rule |
|---|---|
| Source art | SVG or other vector source for every asset. |
| Output | Sprite atlases at two scales: 1x (matches the 480 x 360 reference) and 2x (matches the device pixel ratio cap of 2). As built for the axolotl (issue #46): no atlas yet. Each rig part (6a) is baked once with Canvas 2D into its own Phaser texture, at the bake scale (the view zoom, at least the device pixel ratio, at most 3) times the size factor (6o) ([ADR 0013](adr/0013-axolotl-rig-and-frame-budget.md)). |
| Padding | At least 4 px at 1x and 8 px at 2x around every sprite. This leaves room for the outline, which sits outside the fill, and for gill overhang. |
| Layers | One layer per animated part, in the layer order of section 6a. As built, the axolotl rig groups the 6a layers into parts (the rig parts table in 6a): a part that never moves holds several layers (`body` 4 to 8, `face` 9.3 to 11.5), and each gill, leg and leg's Socks and Feet is its own part. |
| Morphs | Palette swap through role slots (3b), plus a pattern overlay and an eye variant where needed. Never redraw per morph. |
| Naming | Name layers by slot role (for example `body`, `far-side`, `gill-near-stem`) so a swap can find them. |
| Outline | Bake the ink outline into the art at the widths in section 4, or draw it by the three techniques. Keep it #2b2350 (`derived (provisional)`). |
| Wearables | One layer for each of the eight slots, authored in axolotl local units at Large (x1.00, the prototype scale), facing right, and checked on every Boy or girl, Tail, Body and Age (6u, 6r to 6t): `wear-tail` 9.2, `wear-shirt` 9.3, `wear-neck` 9.5, `wear-back` 9.7, `wear-head` 10.5 and `wear-face` 11.5, plus the leg layers `wear-socks-far` and `wear-feet-far` at 2.5 (the far-front and far-rear legs) and `wear-socks-near` and `wear-feet-near` at 3.5 (the near pair). A leg layer is painted per leg, in that leg's frame, so a lost or part-grown leg simply skips it (6i, 6q). Eyes (layer 12) stay above all of them. |
| Wearable art keys | The axolotl rig's re-bake key holds the outfit (the eight slot items), next to the look, morph, Dream colour and bake scale; each leg part is also keyed by its leg state, and a leg's Socks and Feet part is empty while that leg is lost or part-grown (6a, 6q). An outfit change re-bakes the rig's parts like a look change, never per pose. Pictures that show an outfit (every restyle step, which shows the draft outfit, and the explorer card) are keyed by look, morph, Dream colour, crop, bake scale and outfit, and baked again only when one changes. Pictures on the character creator's steps carry no outfit (8). |
| Wearable footprints in the frame | Nothing grows the 166 x 142 axolotl texture frame (issue #88 did not grow it). Measured over all 54 looks, every swim phase and tail amplitudes to 1.6, ink included: the Feet envelope's square corner is lowest at y 58.87, 4.13 above the frame bottom, and it reaches x -62.6 to 47.2; the lowest Socks and Flippers ink is at y 56.24, under the y 59 limit (4 inside the frame bottom); the Back box is x -32.8 to -5.5 and y -37.9 to -3.4; the Shirt is x -38 to -5.5 and y -22 to 30 (inside the torso); the Head footprint reaches y -44. Socks sit inside the Feet envelope. |
| Outfit slot crops (as built) | Still pictures for the Outfit tab's slot buttons (8) are cropped from the frame by slot, in local units. Since issue #88 the crops are not set by hand: `src/art/outfitSlots.ts` (`OUTFIT_SLOT_CROPS`) works each one out from the slot's footprint geometry. It takes the union of the footprint over all 54 Boy or girl, Tail, Body and Age looks (size only scales the bake), 13 swim phases and the tail amplitudes 0, the still pose's and 1.6, pads it by 4, rounds it outward to whole units and keeps it inside the frame. So a grown footprint grows its crop. Socks and Feet share one crop that holds all four feet and sleeves. As worked out at issue #88: Head x 12 to 50, y -48 to -15; Neck x -2 to 46, y 9 to 27; Face x 10 to 58, y -22 to 10; Shirt x -42 to -1, y -26 to 34; Back x -37 to -1, y -42 to 1; Tail x -76 to -5, y -36 to 23; Socks and Feet x -67 to 52, y 0 to 63. The code is the record when these differ. The empty-slot cue's dashed footprint outlines (8) come from the same file (`slotFootprintPaths`). Pictures are drawn at x1.00 about the crop centre and baked as the other picture buttons are (see Picture-button bakes below). The Outfit tab's choices view uses its slot's crop; the explorer card uses the `whole` crop. |
| Dream colours | Slot swap plus one `dream-overlay` layer (pattern, marker, rim light), clipped to the silhouette and fin fill (inset per 6p). |
| Spots | Layer `spots`. |
| Face variants and gill styles | Separate parts, so creator previews can swap them. A Girl's eyelashes are part of the eye. Tail, Body and Age are proportions of the one body geometry (6r to 6t), not separate art. |
| Texture frames | Must include Grown-up Long gill tips (ink to about y -74.4), Head items (the Head footprint, to y -44), the longest tail (ink to x -97.5), a Chubby Girl's lost-leg outline and regrowth glints (to about y 58.05), and the existing padding. Large is x1.00, so size never grows the frame. The protected ring and effects are separate sprites. As built, the axolotl's parts are baked separately (6a), each in its own box (its ink plus 4 units of padding) about its pivot, and placed in this frame's local units; the frame still sets the anchor and the sprite bounds. |
| Axolotl texture frame (as built) | 166 x 142 local units, with local (0,0) at 102 from the left and 79 from the top (local x -102 to 64, y -79 to 63). It holds every look (every gill style, Boy or girl, Tail, Body and Age) at every gill flutter phase and at tail amplitudes up to 1.6, ink included, with at least 4 units of padding: the far left is a Grown-up Chubby Boy's Long tail tip (ink to x -97.5, 4.5 padding); the top is Grown-up Long gill ink (about y -74.4, about 4.6); the bottom is a regrowing rear leg's glints on a Chubby Girl, whose deeper torso lowers the leg roots (about y 58.05, about 4.95); the right is the snout ink (x 56.5), keeping the old frame's 7.5 of slack. It grew from 148 x 125 (origin 84 from the left, 73 from the top) to 166 x 139: left and up for the longest tail and Grown-up gills, and down for the Chubby Girl's lost-leg outline and regrowth glints. Issue #58 grew it again, down only, from 166 x 139 to 166 x 142, for the deeper Chubby Girl (6s, 6u). The width and the origin did not change. Size (6o) changes the bake scale, not the frame: a size change re-bakes the parts at the new scale under the same key. Since issue #46 there is no single axolotl texture in this frame: the rig bakes per-part textures (6a), and the frame is the rig's anchor (local (0,0)) and its sprite bounds, for placement, focus rings and the picture-button `whole` crop. |
| Picture-button crops (as built) | Still pictures for the creator and restyle picture buttons (8) are cropped from the frame by step, in local units: `face` for Eyes and Mouth, x 12 to 58 and y -20 to 24 (both eye zones, every mouth, the snout, a Girl's cheek heart and the Grumpy brows; issue #87 moved its left edge from x 14); `gills` for Gills, x -63 to 25 and y -77 to 22 (every gill style at every flutter phase and Age); `spots` for Spots, x -85 to 7 and y -22 to 20 (every spot mark of every Boy or girl, Tail, Body and Age at every swim phase at tail amplitude 1, with 4 units of padding, plus the back of the head to x 7); `whole` for every other step, the full 166 x 142 frame. Whole pictures are drawn at the option's size factor about the crop centre; face, gill and spots pictures at x1.00. |
| Picture-button bakes (as built) | Each picture is baked once at the still pose, at its button's inner side (the button side less 4.5 each side) over the crop's longer side, times the device pixel ratio, so it shows at 1 texture px per device px and is never upscaled. It is baked again only when its look, morph, crop or bake scale changes. Each step keeps its pictures (one per option) until the creator or restyle screen closes, so going back to a step, or opening its tab again, bakes only the pictures that changed. A resize bakes once, for the new layout and device pixel ratio. Picture-button stills (the creator and restyle picture buttons, and the explorer card) stay whole-axolotl still bakes (`bakeAxolotlStill`), not rigs (6a), and are outside the rig's frame budget (9). |
