**Recorded residuals** (decisions, not values to confirm)

| Residual | Root cause | Bounded impact | Remediation considered and rejected | Review |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Leucistic body vs ink | The approved prototype body #ffb7cf is 8.86:1 with ink, just under the 9:1 pair the rule needs (relative luminance 0.596; 0.607 needed). Its worst case on any background is 2.98:1, at the band of the clean gradient near t = 0.82 (#1079a9). | 0.02 under 3:1, only along the plain body edge, and only where the water is near that one shade. Fin #ffd9e6 (3.34:1) and belly #ffe3ed (3.46:1) pass everywhere. The gap is too small to see. In the lily pad pond it does not occur: ink is 3:1 or better on every pond background (3c). | Lightening the body to #ffbad1 (9.05:1) would change an approved `cartoon.js` value, and section 1 makes the prototype the record. Revisit if the prototype body changes. | PR #19, https://github.com/brenpike/glam-gills/pull/19#discussion_r4308192112 |
| Swim pad over the Trip Bag panel edge | On landscape canvases under about 332 px tall the swim pad stops at its 0.7 minimum scale, so its top (canvas height - 124.1) rises above the Trip Bag panel bottom (208). | Covers at most the panel's 12 px bottom padding at x 16 to 124 (pad top at least 195.9; panel content ends at 196). Visual only; the pad still takes every press. | A smaller pad minimum would hurt touch use (8). Shrinking the panels is the separate UI scale-down product decision. Moving the Trip Bag panel is a HUD redesign. Revisit with the UI scale-down decision. | PR #35, found while fixing https://github.com/brenpike/glam-gills/pull/35#discussion_r4309441325 |
| Wide or heavily accented names in the Name box | The 192 x 48 box holds a 144 px text room, sized from 12 name characters at 0.6 em of bold 20 px, plus 12 px padding each side and the 20 px pencil 4 px after the text room (it was 168 x 48 before the pencil, issue #58). ADR 0011 allows letters from any script with up to 4 combining marks. Wide letters wrap to a second line inside the text room width, and stacked marks can reach past the top or bottom edge. Inherited from the choice value box on main, which had the same size and estimate; there the name ran out sideways. | Visual only, on the player's own device, from their own typing. The name is never cut or changed, and the box, the text field and every button keep working. Two 20 px lines about fill 48 px; only heavy mark stacks spill. No data or safety impact. | Sizing the box from the measured name changes its size while typing, moves More names and the text field, and changes the 192 x 48 spec. Shrinking the text breaks "text never shrinks" (section 8). Clipping hides part of the name. Fewer marks or scripts needs an ADR 0011 change and drops real accents. Revisit when the typeface is chosen (section 12, Typeface) or if names show in another fixed-size box. | Local review of the #52 step creator, finding e7b54ee2. |
| Fit on some mid-size canvases after the creator looks change | Two #58 changes take room on the step screens. The texture frame grew from 166 x 139 to 166 x 142 (11), so the preview is 3 px taller (1.8 px at 0.6). The name box grew from 168 to 192 wide for the pencil (8). The fit order (8) then reaches a later step on some canvases. | Every step still fits the smallest canvases, 460 x 320 and 320 x 440, whose layouts are unchanged (1). Elsewhere only the fit step reached, or the names per page, changes. By the layout tests' text estimate (0.6 em), picture steps reach a later fit step on 21 canvases of the layout tests' grid (all 320 px wide, 320 px tall or 440 px tall), most by 1 or 2 steps, so the labels hide or the preview shrinks. The creator steps with 8 options at 320 x 480 now hide the preview (fit step 4 to 5). On the Name step the fit step rises or the names per page drops by 1 or 2 on some canvases. Examples: 375 x 667 shows 3 names per page, not 4; creator 340 x 440 reaches fit step 5, not 4, with the preview hidden; restyle 568 x 375 reaches fit step 3, not 0. Nothing is cut: More names and every option stay reachable. | Keeping the 166 x 139 frame would leave a Chubby Girl's regrowth glints with under 4 units of padding (11). A narrower name box would either drop below 12 name characters of text room or lose the pencil, which the name box look needs (PRD F17). A smaller preview would break 1 CSS px per local unit (8). Revisit with the UI scale-down decision. | Issue #58, creator looks (this guide update). |
| Name entry field caret on browsers without `caret-animation` | The entry field (8) sets its caret blink with the CSS `caret-animation` property, which is experimental: it shipped in Chrome 140 (2025), and browsers without it ignore it. There the browser's own caret shows. | Visual only, and only while the entry field is open. The browser's own caret still shows and keeps its own blink, which may go on under reduced motion. The drawn name box cursor (9) follows the 0.5 s rule on every browser, and stays steady under reduced motion. Typing, IME composition, selection and the name rules all keep working. | A custom caret drawn over the field would have to follow IME composition, right-to-left and mixed-direction text, selection and the touch selection handles, and would break where it guessed wrong. Revisit as `caret-animation` support grows. | Issue #58, creator looks (this guide update). |
| Name entry field size vs the drawn name box | The drawn name box (8) is 192 x 48 with bold 20 px text and room for the pencil on the right. The real entry field placed over it is at least 56 px tall, uses bold 22 px text (16 px or more so phones do not zoom in), and has 12 px padding with no pencil room. So while typing, the field is a little taller than the drawn box and its text a little larger. Fill, inset, border, radius, placeholder and focus ring match. | Visual only, and only while the entry field is open.
